Result: 四种模型均能恢复均值曲面；协方差恢复方面差异显著：DDPM和score-SDE恢复较好，FM表现出轻微的非平稳性衰减和方差欠离散，VAE难以恢复协方差结构。在ERA5温度异常数据上的实验进一步验证了该评估框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架能够有效诊断不同深度生成模型在空间/时空数据上对分布结构的恢复能力，尤其揭示了各模型在协方差建模上的差异。该框架可支撑面向复杂真实时空数据的DGMs验证与开发。

Abstract: Deep generative models (DGMs) are widely used for complex high-dimensional data and increasingly applied to spatial and spatio-temporal modeling. Their generated samples implicitly represent the learned data distribution and associated uncertainty. However, for real-world data, assessing whether DGMs have learned the underlying process is difficult because the ground truth is unknown and evaluation often relies on observations alone. We evaluate representative DGMs, flow matching (FM), DDPM, score-SDE, and VAE, on a known non-stationary Gaussian random field. This paper provides comprehensive metrics to assess recovery of the ground-truth mean and covariance structures, with oracle samples and a stationary control as references. All four models recover the mean surface, while their covariance recovery differs across model families: DDPM and score-SDE recover the covariance structure reasonably well, FM exhibits mildly attenuated non-stationarity and slight variance under-dispersion, and VAE has difficulty recovering the covariance structure. An experiment on ERA5 temperature anomalies further demonstrates how the framework can support the validation and development of DGMs for complex real-world spatio-temporal data.

</details>

Abstract: This thesis investigates how superperiodicity, quasiperiodicity, and disorder shape electronic and spin transport in graphene-based systems, with an emphasis on experimentally relevant length scales and realistic atomistic modeling. Using large-scale real-space quantum-transport methods, it first establishes controlled transport fingerprints that distinguish conventional Bloch propagation in periodic structures from the anomalous dynamics induced by quasiperiodic modulations. Building on this framework, the thesis analyzes magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene and shows that, within a finite disorder window where flat-band features remain robust, moderate Anderson disorder can counterintuitively enhance the mean free path. This disorder-induced delocalization is further linked to changes in the quantum metric extracted from optical conductivity, revealing a direct connection between transport, electronic geometry, and the real-space extent of the underlying states. The study then turns to graphene quasicrystal approximants and hybrid multilayer stacks, identifying sub-ballistic transport and self-similar localization patterns as signatures of quasicrystalline order, while also demonstrating their strong fragility against disorder and interlayer proximity effects. Finally, the thesis addresses spin transport in suspended monolayer graphene, showing that atomic-scale corrugations generate short-range fluctuating Rashba fields that can limit spin lifetimes to the nanosecond range even when charge transport remains close to ballistic. Taken together, these results provide a unified picture of how geometry, disorder, and structural complexity govern transport phenomena in twisted and corrugated graphene systems.

</details>

Abstract: Proximity effects in layered heterostructures are usually represented by static parameters fitted to first-principles bands, which discards the energy dependence of the virtual hybridization, the momentum transfer, and the spatial structure. The construction separates direct matrix elements from virtual hybridization through all remaining states. In graphene on hBN/Co(0001), virtual hybridization generates more than $99\%$ of the proximity exchange and gives it a resonant frequency dependence set by the Co $d$ states. In graphene/PtSe$_2$ it resolves a sublattice-selective intervalley coupling with a $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ charge modulation, and in graphene/WSe$_2$ a bond-resolved Rashba coupling of $0.24$~meV, against below $1$~$μ$eV for the direct projection alone. Our results expose the limitations of static projections and establish a fitting-free microscopic foundation for low-energy modeling, spin-relaxation theory, and transport calculations.

Abstract: Photoexcited charge carriers in topological insulators display anomalously long-range transport at cryogenic temperatures, but the underlying mechanism remains under debate. Here we use a transverse electric field as a discriminating probe of photocarrier dynamics in bulk-insulating Sb-doped Bi2Se3 nanoribbons, combining scanning photocurrent microscopy (SPCM) with ultrafast transient photovoltage (TPV) measurements. SPCM shows that suppression of the photocurrent requires a transverse electric field nearly 50 times larger than that needed to deflect free carriers, yet comparable to the expected exciton dissociation field. TPV measurements at 12 K and low excitation fluence reveal that the photocarrier diffusivity exceeds the value implied by the Einstein relation and the measured drift mobility by more than an order of magnitude, a bound that holds even at the lower end of the fitting uncertainty. Together, these observations indicate that the long-range photoresponse is not carried by free charge carriers but by a charge-neutral correlated state.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Clean, nonsplit vortex zero-bias peaks (ZBPs) can be misinterpreted as Majorana zero modes (MZMs), making static scanning tunneling microscopy intrinsically ambiguous. Lowering the tip nevertheless drives a clean, non-split vortex-center ZBP into a zero-bias dip, opposite to the protected flow of an isolated MZM, unmasking the peak as a Majorana false positive produced by a conventional vortex-core state. The same flow selectively suppresses the strongly tip-coupled channel, resolving the two-gap superconductivity. Dissipative STM thus tests dynamical protection rather than spectral appearance.

Abstract: Unstrained bulk germanium is a particularly attractive material for circuit quantum electrodynamics with spins (spin-cQED). In addition, the coupling is much less sensitive to the orientation of the applied magnetic field, which shall ease operation and limit the impact of device-to-device variability. Our results establish bulk germanium as a compelling platform for scalable spin-cQED.

Abstract: Entanglement carries universal content that labels phases and critical points. We study the entanglement entropy of the steady states of critical non-Hermitian free-fermion chains. It scales logarithmically with subsystem size, but the coefficients vary continuously with the parameters and form a Rényi family that no single central charge can reproduce. We trace this anomaly to an ``imaginary'' Dirac point, a crossing in the imaginary part of the energy where the occupied state switches between two Bloch states. Their nonorthogonality weakens the occupation discontinuity and lowers the logarithmic coefficient. A low-energy expansion yields closed-form coefficients in excellent agreement with lattice numerics in various one-dimensional critical steady states. Remarkably, weak real onsite disorder leaves this logarithmic scaling intact and enhances the entanglement. Our results provide a generic understanding of entanglement in critical non-Hermitian free-fermion steady states.

Conclusion: 该方法有效扩展了CCE的适用范围，实现了跨时间尺度的核自旋噪声模拟，并通过实验验证了其准确性。对Overhauser场慢漂移的补偿策略可大幅提升量子比特的有效相干时间，为硅基自旋量子比特的操控优化提供了定量指导。

Abstract: The dynamics of a nuclear spin bath generates magnetic noise that is a key contributor to the decoherence of electron spin qubits in electrostatically-defined quantum dots. In this paper, we extend the cluster correlation expansion (CCE) technique, which has proven useful for predicting solid-state qubit coherence times across various settings but is limited to shorter time scales, to incorporate stochastic treatments of cluster dynamics in order to efficiently predict slow drifting Overhauser fields over longer time scales. This approach combines quantum evolution with classical rate matrices to enable simulation across a wide range of temporal regimes required to simulate, for example, the long-time convergence of the ergodic $T_2^*$ from Ramsey experiments. Our methodology is validated against experimental data from various silicon spin qubit systems, demonstrating a strong agreement between simulation and measurement of Ramsey experiments presented in the form of $T_2^*$ versus averaging time, autocorrelation functions, as well as power spectral densities. Furthermore, we demonstrate significant back-action effects through modeling and experiment; specifically, the dynamics of the nuclear spin bath depends upon the electron spin occupation schedule. Finally, our modeling quantitatively predicts the benefits from compensating for the slow drift of Overhauser fields in qubit operations. Our findings indicate that compensating for an Overhauser rotation measured $Δt$ in the past results in an effective $T_2^*$, which we denote $\tilde{T}_2^*(Δt)$ for clarity, under certain scenarios of interest, can be one or two orders of magnitude larger than the ergodic $T_2^*$ if the Overhauser rotation is re-characterized every 100 milliseconds; that is, $\tilde{T}_2^*(Δt = 100~{\rm ms})$ can be $10$ to $100$ times larger than $T_2^*$.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何ChatGPT类AI模型即使在确定性贪婪解码下，仍会意外输出有害、误导性或重复内容——这一现象跨越了架构和训练差异，亟需统一的机理解释。

Method: 将token类比为自旋(spin)，建立多体相互作用模型，将倾翻现象描述为有限层系统中竞争性输出盆地之间的动力学首通过程(first passage process)；引入注意力无序性(attention disorder)控制输运方向；通过少盆地归约推导出闭合的有限层阈值条件，并进行粗粒化预测。

Result: 粗粒化预测结果在多个ChatGPT类模型家族中表现出良好的一致性，验证了有限层阈值框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 广泛的AI失效并非内在不可预测行为，而属于"可预见的工程风险"，这对AI危害的法律与社会评估具有重要启示。

Abstract: Why do ChatGPT-like AIs, despite major architectural and training differences, unexpectedly tip to undesirable content (e.g. harmful, misleading, repetitive) even under deterministic greedy decoding? We show that a broad class of such tippings is caused by the many-body interactions between tokens (spins) as they cross the finite-layer system. Tipping emerges as a dynamical first passage process between competing output basins. Attention disorder controls the transport toward, away from, or along the basins' boundary. A few-basin reduction yields a closed finite-layer threshold, whose coarse-grained predictions show good agreement across ChatGPT-like families. These results suggest that a broad class of AI failures represents 'foreseeable engineering risk' rather than inherently unpredictable behavior, with important implications for legal and societal assessments of AI harm.

</details>

Method: 将精确的短时Pauli传播数据与两种时间扩展技术相结合：(1) 基于正性条件（关联函数的谱密度应为非负）进行延拓；(2) 利用动力学通常由少数特征频率主导这一观测，通过稀疏频率拟合实现时间外推。由此绕过Pauli算符的指数增殖问题。

Abstract: Pauli propagation has shown promise for classically simulating quantum dynamics by evolving observables directly in the Heisenberg picture. In this work, we investigate its use for computing real-time two-point time-ordered correlators in one- and two-dimensional quantum systems. One major limitation of Pauli propagation is the rapid growth in the number of Pauli strings beyond short times. We overcome this limitation by combining accurate short-time Pauli-propagation data with time extension methods based on a positivity condition and the observation that the dynamics are often dominated by a small number of characteristic frequencies. This combined approach extends correlation functions far beyond the directly accessible time window while avoiding the exponential proliferation of Pauli operators. We demonstrate that the resulting correlators retain the relevant dynamical and spectral information. Our results broaden the regime in which classical methods can reliably probe the real-time dynamics of interacting quantum many-body systems.

</details>

Result: 开放系统模拟预测：在530纳秒内，基态布居数可产生约10%的偏移。这一灵敏度相比传统的二能级Unruh-DeWitt探测器提升了三个数量级。

Abstract: The Unruh effect predicts that an accelerating observer perceives the Minkowski vacuum as a thermal bath, yet direct detection remains experimentally inaccessible. Its timelike counterpart, arising from the entanglement of massless fields between the future and past light cones, offers a more feasible route but requires a detector whose transition frequency follows a specific conformal-time scaling. We propose and analyze a practical implementation of such a detector using superconducting fluxonium circuits, which naturally provide two quasi-degenerate ground states and a tunable excited state, forming an effective $Λ$-system. By modulating the excited-state transition frequency in Minkowski time, the detector accumulates a geometric phase associated with the timelike Unruh effect. Open-system simulations predict $\sim 10\%$ shift in the ground-state population within $530$ ns, representing a three-order-of-magnitude sensitivity enhancement over two-level Unruh-DeWitt detectors. These results establish a realistic quantum-circuit platform for experimentally probing the timelike Unruh effect and, more broadly, for testing fundamental nature of quantum fields using engineered quantum systems.

</details>


### [73] [A Difference Operator Approach to Quantum Random Walks: Parseval Identity, Krawtchouk Matrices, and Hermite Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24837)
*Chien-Wen Hwang*

TL;DR: 本文引入离散差分算子D_k，利用Krawtchouk矩阵研究一维Hadamard硬币量子随机游走，得到概率幅的显式组合表达式，证明Parseval恒等式确保概率守恒，并揭示D_k作为离散Hermite多项式生成元的作用，最终解释弹道扩散的O(n²)标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维Hadamard量子随机游走的概率幅具有复杂的交替符号和态依赖结构，其与经典二项分布的关系、概率守恒机制以及相干性来源尚需更清晰的组合与代数刻画。

Method: (1) 引入离散差分算子D_k作用于组合数； (2) 推导概率幅a(n,k)和b(n,k)的显式组合表达式； (3) 建立a±b与Krawtchouk矩阵对角/次对角元对应关系； (4) 利用Krawtchouk交叉恒等式归纳证明Parseval恒等式Σ(a²+b²)=2^{n-1}； (5) 证明比率h_m = (n choose k)^{-1} D_k^m (n choose k)的闭合形式并取连续极限收敛至Hermite多项式； (6) 数值验证n=6, 10情形。

Result: 移除交替符号和硬币态区分后恢复经典二项分布；a±b恰为Krawtchouk矩阵对角/次对角元；Parseval恒等式得证，保证概率守恒；D_k作为离散Hermite生成元和相干生成元/上升算子；h_m在连续极限下收敛于Hermite多项式；方差呈O(n²)弹道标度，源自所有激发态的叠加。

Conclusion: 离散差分算子D_k为QRW提供统一的代数框架，将概率幅、Krawtchouk矩阵和Hermite多项式联系起来，清晰揭示量子干涉的离散结构及其在连续极限下的行为，弹道扩散来源于多激发态相干叠加。

Abstract: We introduce a discrete difference operator D_k to study the one-dimensional quantum random walk (QRW) with the Hadamard coin. Explicit combinatorial expressions are obtained for the probability amplitudes a(n,k) and b(n,k), which encode the final step direction and carry alternating signs that reflect the merging of leftward steps. Removing these signs and the coin-state distinction recovers the classical binomial distribution. The symmetric and antisymmetric combinations $a\pm b$ are shown to coincide with diagonal and sub-diagonal entries of the Krawtchouk matrix. Using cross identities among Krawtchouk matrix elements, we prove by induction that the amplitudes satisfy a Parseval identity sum (a^2+b^2)=2^n-1, establishing probability conservation in the Krawtchouk formulation. The operator D_k acts as a discrete Hermite polynomial generator: the ratios h_m = (n choose k)^{-1} D_k^m (n choose k) admit explicit closed forms and converge to Hermite polynomials in the continuous limit. At the discrete level, D_k connects successive Krawtchouk matrices and acts as a coherence generator and a raising operator. The h_m quantify the position-dependent degree of quantum interference on both halves of the distribution, either individually (for x >= 0) or through an inverse-Pascal combination of several h_m (for x < 0), and the ballistic O(n^2) scaling of the variance emerges from the superposition of all excited states. Abstract: Unitary designs provide efficient substitutes for Haar-random unitaries in quantum information processing, randomized measurements, and many-body quantum dynamics. We propose a protocol based on time evolutions of a single chaotic Hamiltonian with intermediate unitary perturbations to generate unitary designs. We derive the frame potential of the resulting ensemble which relies on the frame potentials of the intermediate ensemble. The intermediate ensemble need not itself be Haar random or a unitary design even approximately; it is sufficient that its frame-potential growth remains well suppressed relative to the maximal possible scaling. The nontrivial Pauli set and the Clifford ensemble are simple examples satisfying this criterion, while fixed-size ensembles generally fail. We further show that, at the level of frame potentials, multi-Hamiltonian temporal protocols can be recursively replaced by one-Hamiltonian protocols interleaved with fixed trace-suppressed perturbations.

Abstract: We formulate minimal and dissipative Continuous Spontaneous Localization (CSL) dynamics in the functional Schrödinger representation for a non-relativistic bosonic field. In this framework, the Fock-space state is encoded in a wave functional, and fixed particle-number wave functions are obtained by sector projection. For the minimal CSL coupling to the smeared mass density, this projection gives the standard nonlinear stochastic dynamics in each \(N\)-particle sector, with the collapse operator acting on the total smeared density of the configuration. This makes the amplification mechanism transparent and allows us to discuss sector superpositions, local probability balance, and the status of Bohmian equivariance at the wave-function level. We then consider a dissipative extension in which the collapse operator includes a smeared current contribution. The one-particle sector reproduces the expected dissipative CSL energy balance, while fixed many-body sectors contain additional collective momentum shifts and pair-mixing terms that are not reducible, in general, to independent one-particle contributions. Within a leading compact closure, the collective pair friction produces a non-extensive stationary mean kinetic energy: in three dimensions and for weak dissipation, $T_N^{\rm comp}\simeq 2T_β/N$, whereas the corresponding dilute energy remains extensive.

Abstract: We formalize side-channel detection in Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) as a certificate-forgery hypothesis test and identify its detectability with an information-theoretic rate. Relative to a set $\mathcal{M}$ of trusted, real-time-monitored observables, the forgery- detectability rate $g_{\mathcal M}$ of a benign-statistics certificate is the missed-detection Stein exponent. We prove a dichotomy: $g_{\mathcal M}$ is monotone and strictly positive in the leaked Holevo information when the shot- noise unit is trusted, and identically zero otherwise, recovering local- oscillator and calibration attacks as the degenerate case. Monotonicity yields a certificate reusability rate bounding the Holevo leakage compatible with an undetected certificate over $N_{\mathrm{PE}}$ estimation rounds, via the non- asymptotic bound $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{PE}}\le\exp(-N_{\mathrm{PE}}ψ(r))$ whose near-threshold expansion matches the Gaussian confidence-interval scaling of standard finite-size analyses. We then give a composable finite-size key- length statement under collective Gaussian attacks, whose worst-case Holevo term is the reusability rate and whose parameter-estimation failure probability is bounded by the Stein exponent at every block length. Both results are unconditional in the trusted-correlation model, where strict monotonicity of the Holevo leakage in the excess noise follows from a noise-injection argument; for general $\mathcal{M}$ they hold under an explicit no-spurious-local-minima condition on the divergence landscape, verifiable by low-dimensional inspection. Convexity of the rate further assumes a numerically supported concavity of the Holevo bound, the sole numerical ingredient; proof status is delimited throughout.

</details>

Abstract: The influence of relativistic motion on quantum entanglement is commonly attributed to acceleration-induced thermal noise. Here we show that, for asymmetric multipartite states, the topology of entanglement can become equally important. Considering a three-qubit Star state in the Unruh-DeWitt detector framework, we compare two inequivalent acceleration configurations in which either the central or a peripheral qubit undergoes uniform acceleration. We demonstrate that these physically equivalent accelerations lead to qualitatively different entanglement dynamics: acceleration of a peripheral qubit induces a revival of one-tangle that is absent when the central qubit accelerates, whereas genuine tripartite entanglement decays monotonically but with markedly different robustness. Our results uncover a topology-dependent mechanism for relativistic entanglement degradation, showing that the response of multipartite quantum correlations is determined jointly by Unruh thermalization and the structural role of the accelerated subsystem. This work identifies asymmetric quantum networks as a distinct platform for controlling relativistic quantum resources.

Conclusion: 通过工程化通信错误偏置并巧妙设计纠错电路，可以完全避免分布式量子计算中的纠缠蒸馏，从而极大节省节点内量子资源。该方案显著放松了对光子学和通信量子比特的硬件要求，使分布式架构的可扩展性大幅提升。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing interconnects small, high-quality nodes through optical links, but this architecture carries a pronounced asymmetry: in-node gates and measurements are cheap and high-fidelity, whereas inter-node communication relies on a low-coherence communication qubit and faulty photonics. Previous approaches overcame the noisy link by placing several high-quality data qubits in each node and consuming them for Bell pair and GHZ state distillation. Here we show that distillation can be avoided altogether. The key observation is that we can engineer a communication error bias, where photonic Bell pairs suffer frequent phase errors but only rare bit-flip errors. We design the syndrome-extraction circuits so that this phase noise appears solely as a measurement error that does not propagate to the data qubits, and is therefore suppressed by simply repeating the measurement; letting the error-correcting code itself, rather than a dedicated distillation subroutine, to purify the link. This dramatically reduces the need for ancillary nuclei: Floquet codes require only a single data qubit per node, while general stabilizer codes require just one additional ancilla. We demonstrate high error-correction thresholds throughout this regime, and we identify lattice surgery as inherently robust for this setting, enabling logical operations at a threshold close to that of quantum memory. As a result, the performance of the quantum computer is limited by the high-quality data qubits, while the requirements on photon indistinguishability and coherence of the communication qubit are substantially relaxed.

</details>

Abstract: The density of states (DoS) encodes the thermodynamic and spectral properties of quantum many-body systems, yet its reconstruction becomes intractable for Hilbert spaces too large to diagonalize. Abstract: Approximate quantum error correction (AQEC) not only dictates the performance of discrete- and continuous-variable quantum error correction codes but also serves as a unifying framework across various physical disciplines. This framework yields a tighter analytical lower bound on entanglement fidelity than the conventional limit set by the transpose channel. Furthermore, by exploiting the decayed weights of noise Kraus operators, we introduce a framework based on principal component analysis to reduce the dimension. Our approach enables high-precision optimization for AQEC codes that were previously intractable due to the curse of dimensionality.

Abstract: We generalize the notion of quantum reference frames (QRFs) to cases where the frame does not necessarily correspond to a tensor factor subsystem, but to a covariant quantum instrument. This unlocks a variety of physical applications: "frames of labeling" for indistinguishable particles, suggesting explanations for the symmetrization postulate and the absence of parastatistics, and yielding a transparent description of the entanglement of bosons and fermions; and relational clocks reproducing the Schrödinger equation exactly even when all subsystems are interacting or when there are frequency superselection sectors. This also suggests operational clarifications of some aspects of constraint quantization, e.g. of the meaning of constraint equations such as $C|ψ\rangle=0$. Abstract: Observables in quantum mechanics are generally complementary, that is, they reveal mutually incompatible pieces of information about a given system. This property is not only a fundamental tenet of the quantum formalism, but also a key component in quantum cryptographic protocols. In its strongest form -- the one we consider in this work -- the information between complementary observables is not merely incompatible but mutually exclusive: maximal information about one observable implies no information about the other, and vice versa. Finally, we prove that these complementarity sets entail strong entropic uncertainty relations.

However, a turning point (minimum) corresponding to a maximum information dissipation to the spin bath is found. Abstract: We present a numerical study of subsystem distance decay following a global quantum quench in the infinite one-dimensional transverse-field Ising chain, using the mathematically rigorous Bures distance $B_A(t)$ to quantify the deviation of the time-evolved reduced density matrix from its stationary generalized Gibbs ensemble state. Abstract: We introduce a new hybrid quantum-classical algorithm inspired by an advanced control strategy known as model predictive control (MPC). This algorithm unifies the optimization-based design of variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) with the feedback-based design of feedback-based quantum algorithms (FQAs). Variational circuit parameters are optimized using a layer-wise receding horizon strategy, where observable measurements after every layer initialize a classically simulated dynamic model used to predict quantum state evolution and optimize over future parameterized gates. This hybrid algorithm can be used for applications such as ground state preparation and approximate combinatorial optimization, and presents an ideal use case for the integration of quantum computers with high-performance computing, where the latter resource can be used to increase the scale and efficiency of the predictions critical to MPC. We show through mathematical proof and numerical evidence that the MPC-based algorithm can be guaranteed to at least match the performance of FQAs. Through simulations on Max-Cut problems and a two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, we demonstrate that relaxed implementations of the MPC-based algorithm can also provide improved performance in practice compared to an FQA.

Abstract: We employ a generalized approach to the master equation for driven open $N$-level ($N>2$) quantum systems using Lewis-Riesenfeld invariants, which avoids the driving-strength restrictions inherent to conventional approaches.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解对称性保护下重复量子测量所诱导的动力学，需要一个统一的框架来描述表示扇区之间的随机跃迁，并将测量算符的解析性约束与探测器谱联系起来。

Method: 基于Peter-Weyl分解，从不可约张量算符构造协变测量算符；对对称不变态，将测量信道约化为表示图上的Markov过程；利用Sugiura定理约束探测器谱，引入指数衰减探测器模型；对SU(2)求解闭式跃迁核，证明可逆性及不变测度，并推导连续Fokker-Planck方程。

Result: 对SU(2)得到了闭式跃迁核，证明过程可逆并给出不变测度；推导的漂移和扩散系数的解析预测与数值模拟一致；成功将重复协变量子测量描述为表示空间上的Fokker-Planck扩散过程。

Conclusion: 该框架为表示空间上重复协变量子测量提供了完整的随机描述，揭示了测量动力学与群表示论之间的深层联系，可用于分析对称性约束下的量子测量诱导动力学。

Abstract: We develop a framework for group-covariant quantum measurements in which measurement-induced transitions between irreducible representation sectors are described by a stochastic process on representation space. Starting from the Peter-Weyl decomposition, we construct covariant measurement operators from irreducible tensor operators and show that, for symmetry-invariant states, the measurement channel reduces to a Markov process on the representation graph. We further show that analyticity of the measurement operator constrains the detector spectrum through Sugiura's theorem, motivating a class of exponentially decaying detector models. Specializing to SU(2), we obtain the transition kernel in closed form, establish reversibility and the associated invariant measure, and derive a continuum Fokker-Planck description of the induced dynamics. Analytical predictions for the drift and diffusion coefficients are found to agree with numerical simulations. Our results provide a stochastic description of repeated covariant quantum measurements on representation space.

</details>


### [96] [Exceptional-point braiding with native controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25109)
*Vishnu Chavva,Nero Osmancevic,Hugo Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种受约束的绝热捷径原理，利用原生控制实现非厄米系统中异常点（EP）的编织。在修饰瞬时本征态框架中，可用控制抵消可及的跃迁通道，残余通道虽在演化中保持活跃，但在闭合环路上净累积效应为零，从而在终点实现理想绝热分支交换的态转移，而非全程强制局域抵消。该方案在两模非厄米模型中进行了演示，其平滑波形仅需调整失谐和驱动幅度即可复现分支交换转移，且对校准误差、EP不确定性和有限带宽滤波具有良好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现异常点分支交换态转移的动态控制面临两难：慢速环绕通常不能传输完整的瞬时本征态集，而绝热捷径方法常需要实验原生控制流形之外的额外控制手段。问题在于如何在仅使用原生控制的约束条件下，实现EP编织的可靠态转移。

Method: 作者提出"受约束的绝热捷径"（constrained STA）原理：在修饰瞬时本征态框架中，将可用控制设计为局域抵消可及的跃迁通道，同时允许残余通道在演化中保持活跃，但约束其在闭合环路上的净累积效应为零。目标是在演化终点实现理想绝热分支交换所选择的态转移，而非全程强制局域抵消或绝热跟随。在两模非厄米模型中构造该协议，模型等效于耗散Transmon EP实验中使用的有效哈密顿量（相差迹偏移和基矢约定），其中失谐和驱动幅度为实控制参数，相对损耗不平衡固定非厄米尺度。

Result: 平滑实波形仅重塑失谐和驱动幅度两个控制参数，即可复现分支交换态转移。该协议在校准误差、EP位置不确定性和有限带宽滤波条件下仍保持准确性，且额外开销适中。

Conclusion: 受约束的绝热捷径原理为EP编织提供了一种在原生控制约束下的实用态转移方案，避免了慢速环绕的不完全传输和传统STA对外部控制的需求，在两模模型中验证了可行性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Exceptional points define branch-exchange state transfers through holomorphic continuation of non-Hermitian eigenmodes, but realizing these transfers dynamically remains difficult. Slow encircling does not generally transport the full set of instantaneous eigenstates, while shortcuts to adiabaticity can require controls outside the native experimental manifold. Here, we introduce a constrained shortcut-to-adiabaticity principle for exceptional-point braiding using native controls. In a dressed instantaneous-eigenstate frame, the available controls cancel the accessible transition channels locally, while the residual channels remain active during the evolution but are constrained to have no net accumulated effect over the closed loop. The protocol therefore targets the endpoint state transfer selected by ideal adiabatic branch exchange, rather than enforcing complete local cancellation or adiabatic following throughout the trajectory. We demonstrate the construction in a minimal two-mode non-Hermitian model equivalent, up to a trace shift and a basis convention, to the effective Hamiltonian used in dissipative-transmon exceptional-point experiments, where detuning and drive amplitude provide real controls and the relative loss imbalance fixes the non-Hermitian scale. Smooth real waveforms reshape only these controls, reproduce the branch-exchange transfer, and remain accurate under calibration errors, exceptional-point uncertainty and finite-bandwidth filtering with modest overhead.

</details>


### [97] [Agentic AI for Scientific Reasoning in Autonomous Quantum Sensing Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25145)
*Takuya Isogawa,Ryotaro Okabe,Nutdech Phadetsuwannukun,Mingda Li,Paola Cappellaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了一个基于大语言模型智能体的自主实验工作流，用于金刚石中氮空位（NV）中心的量子传感实验，并引入了两个离线基准来评估智能体的推理能力，结果表明应将科学假设与定量工具交由智能体处理，而硬件控制和安全约束则由确定性代码负责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV中心实验涉及大量计算机可控的测量，是自主工作流的天然应用场景，但现有方法缺乏对智能体推理能力与实验执行分离的系统评估。作者旨在构建一个结合持久项目记录、定量计算工具和确定性实验控制的自主工作流，并建立离线基准来独立评估智能体的推理表现。

Method: 作者围绕LLM构建了自主实验智能体工作流：智能体负责选择NV中心、校准共振频率、执行Ramsey测量测量T₂*，并在发现弱特征后追加CPMG测量排查邻近¹³C的影响。此外，设计了两个离线基准——Ramsey校验点基准和脉冲ODMR数据评估基准，在GPT-5.4、GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6 Sol上评估不同推理强度对残余共振校准偏移识别及假阳性率的影响，并引入预期信号计算作为辅助约束。

Result: 在Ramsey校验点基准中，提高推理强度普遍改善了对残余共振校准偏移的识别。在pODMR数据评估基准中，仅依赖脉冲序列信息时，推理强度越高越易产生假阳性共振判断；但引入预期信号计算后，三种模型在各推理设置下的假阳性率均保持较低水平。

Conclusion: 自主实验应明确分工：LLM智能体负责形成科学假设并使用定量工具评估数据，而确定性代码负责硬件控制和安全约束的执行。这种分工兼顾了智能体的灵活推理与实验运行的安全可靠性。

Abstract: We implement an agentic AI workflow built around a large language model (LLM) agent for autonomous experiments with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond. NV centers are a widely used platform for quantum sensing, and the ability to control many measurements from a computer makes NV experiments a natural setting for autonomous workflows. We make two main contributions. First, we demonstrate an autonomous NV experiment workflow that combines persistent project records, quantitative calculation and data analysis tools, and deterministic experiment control. In one autonomous experiment, the agent selected a single NV center, calibrated its resonant frequency, measured \(T_2^\ast\) with Ramsey measurements, and added a Carr--Purcell--Meiboom--Gill (CPMG) measurement to check a weak feature that could be related to nearby \(^{13}\mathrm{C}\). Second, we introduce two offline benchmarks that evaluate the agent's reasoning separately from laboratory execution. We evaluated both benchmarks with GPT-5.4, GPT-5.5, and GPT-5.6 Sol. In the Ramsey checkpoint benchmark, greater reasoning effort generally improved recognition of a residual resonance calibration offset. By contrast, in the pulsed optically detected magnetic resonance (pODMR) data evaluation benchmark, pulse sequence information alone produced more false positive resonance judgments at higher reasoning effort. Requiring an expected signal calculation kept false positive rates low across all three models and reasoning settings. The results suggest a clear division of labor for autonomous experiments. The agent forms scientific hypotheses and uses quantitative tools to evaluate data, while deterministic code controls the hardware and enforces safety constraints.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Transformer BSDE Solver via Multi-Layer Fully-Connected Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25162)
*Howard Su,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Chi-Sheng Chen,Lance Bai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于全连接变分量子电路（FC-VQC）的量子Transformer BSDE求解器，通过因果自注意力机制学习高维抛物型偏微分方程中BSDE梯度过程的时间-坐标交互关系。在三个d=36维PDE基准上，QTransformer优于无注意力的FC-VQC基线，并在紧凑隐藏宽度下超越经典Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维抛物型偏微分方程在工程、物理和随机控制中至关重要，但面临维度灾难。深度BSDE方法将其转化为BSDE问题，允许从已知随机动态生成轨迹，并用可训练模型学习梯度相关的控制过程。现有方法缺乏对轨迹时间序列中交互关系的有效建模，论文旨在探索量子架构与注意力机制结合能否提升BSDE求解性能。

Method: 提出Quantum Transformer BSDE求解器：将归一化状态轨迹作为时间-坐标token，应用因果自注意力学习BSDE梯度过程中的时序交互。所有可训练参数均置于FC-VQC模块（嵌入、投影、前馈网络、解码器）中，注意力机制和结构操作保持经典且无参数。

Result: 在三个d=36维PDE基准实验上：QTransformer一致优于无注意力FC-VQC基线；在紧凑隐藏宽度设置下超越经典Transformer；但较宽隐藏宽度的经典Transformer仍取得最佳整体精度。

Conclusion: 因果注意力与FC-VQC的结合为高维BSDE轨迹学习提供了一种有效的量子架构，在紧凑模型宽度下展现优势，但经典Transformer在足够宽度下仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Solving high-dimensional parabolic partial differential equations (PDEs) is important in engineering, physics, and stochastic control. Deep BSDE methods reformulate semilinear PDEs as backward stochastic differential equations and admit a model-based reinforcement learning interpretation, where trajectories are generated from known stochastic dynamics while a trainable model learns the gradient-related control process. We propose a Quantum Transformer BSDE solver based on Multi-Layer Fully-Connected Variational Quantum Circuits (FC-VQC). The method treats the normalized state trajectory as time--coordinate tokens and applies causal self-attention to learn interactions in the adapted BSDE gradient process. All trainable model parameters are contained within the FC-VQC embedding, projection, feed-forward, and decoder modules, while attention and structural operations remain classical and parameter-free. Experiments on three d=36 PDE benchmarks show that QTransformer consistently improves over the non-attentive FC-VQC baseline and outperforms the classical Transformer at compact hidden widths, while the wider classical Transformer achieves the best overall accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining causal attention with FC-VQC provides an effective quantum architecture for high-dimensional BSDE trajectory learning.

</details>


### [99] [QUBO-Based Optimization of Social Indicator Configurations for Working-Age Population Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25170)
*Hayate Wada,Seiya Miyamoto,Tatsumasa Ogawa,Kazuki Uehara,Masaru Hitomi,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于QUBO（二次无约束二元优化）的框架，用于探索与劳动年龄人口增长相关的社会指标配置。利用日本市町村数据，将人口增长率回归到十个离散化社会指标上，得到可解释的二次代理模型，并通过量子退火、模拟退火和Gurobi进行优化求解，识别出最优指标配置及次优方案，为政策讨论提供探索性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 劳动年龄人口下降是老龄化社会（如日本）面临的重大区域可持续性挑战。现有方法难以系统性地捕捉社会指标之间的非线性交互作用，且缺乏将统计建模与组合优化相衔接的框架，以探索促进人口增长的指标配置方案。

Method: 首先使用日本市町村数据，将2010-2020年劳动年龄人口增长率回归到十个离散化的社会指标上，构建二次代理模型（测试集相关系数0.84，平均R²为0.76）。然后将拟合模型转化为带独热约束的QUBO公式，分别使用量子退火、模拟退火和Gurobi三种优化方法求解，同时通过退火采样器生成可行次优配置。此外还进行了单指标敏感性分析。

Result: 三种优化方法均识别出相同的最优可行配置；退火采样器还生成了具有不同预测增长率的可行次优配置。单指标分析表明，改变一个指标对预测增长率的影响方向取决于其他指标的取值，体现了指标间的交互效应。

Conclusion: 该框架将市政社会统计数据、非线性交互作用和基于模型的场景生成相连接，具有可解释性和优化就绪性。但应将其视为政策讨论的探索性工具，而非政策干预的因果估计。

Abstract: The decline of the working-age population is a major challenge for regional sustainability, particularly in ageing societies such as Japan. We present a methodological demonstration of a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO)-based framework for exploring social-indicator configurations associated with working-age population growth. Using Japanese municipal data, we regressed the 2010-2020 working-age population growth rate on ten discretized social indicators. The resulting quadratic surrogate model showed reasonable predictive performance, with a test-set correlation coefficient of 0.84 and an average R-squared value of 0.76. Its coefficient matrix provides an interpretable representation of individual indicator-level contributions and pairwise associations. We converted the fitted model into a QUBO formulation with one-hot constraints and optimized it using quantum annealing, simulated annealing, and Gurobi. All three methods identified the same optimal feasible configuration, while the annealing-based samplers also generated feasible suboptimal configurations with different predicted growth rates. Municipality-level single-indicator analyses showed that changing one indicator can increase or decrease the predicted growth rate depending on the other indicators. The framework provides an interpretable and optimization-ready approach for connecting municipal social statistics, nonlinear interactions, and model-based scenario generation. It should be regarded as an exploratory tool for policy discussion rather than as a causal estimate of policy interventions.

</details>


### [100] [Path integral approach to the truncated Wigner approximation of driven-dissipative spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26029)
*Viktoria Noel,Michael Fleischhauer,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了开放自旋-1/2系统在连续SU(2)相空间中的Keldysh路径积分表述，证明截断至二阶量子涨落等价于截断Wigner近似(TWA)，且正确处理耗散需将算符乘积映射到弯曲自旋相空间，所得随机方程与连续TWA一致，可精确再现单耗散自旋的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将TWA的路径积分基础从玻色系统推广到开放自旋-1/2系统，为耗散自旋动力学的半经典近似提供统一的场论基础，并为超越半经典水平的系统扩展提供出发点。

Method: 采用连续SU(2)相空间上的Keldysh路径积分形式，将作用量在量子涨落中截断至二阶，并特别关注算符乘积到弯曲自旋相空间的正确映射以处理耗散。

Result: 推导出的随机方程与连续TWA公式一致，且能精确重现单个耗散自旋的精确动力学，验证了该路径积分框架在处理开放自旋系统时的自洽性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为耗散自旋动力学的截断Wigner近似提供了统一的场论基础，并指明了超越半经典近似的系统扩展路径。

Abstract: Phase-space approaches such as the truncated Wigner approximation (TWA) provide an efficient semiclassical framework for performing approximate simulations of the dynamics of open quantum many-body systems outside the reach of exact numerical methods but beyond the mean-field level. For bosonic systems, TWA is known to be equivalent to a Keldysh path-integral formulation truncated at second order in the so-called quantum fluctuations. This semiclassical approach provides an alternative transparent route towards approximate stochastic equations of motion, which can be efficiently solved. Here we establish the corresponding path-integral formulation for interacting open spin-$1/2$ systems using the continuous $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ phase space. We show, in particular, that a consistent treatment of dissipation requires correctly mapping operator products onto the curved spin phase space, leading to stochastic equations that coincide with those obtained from the continuous TWA formulation and thus reproduce the exact dynamics of a single dissipative spin. Our results provide a unified field-theoretic foundation for the TWA to dissipative spin dynamics and offer a systematic starting point for extensions beyond the semiclassical approximation.

</details>


### [101] [Dissipation Enables Strongly Detuning-Dependent Interference in Pulsed Dynamical Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25176)
*Kenneth DeRose,Jonah Glick,Kefeng Jiang,Hans Johnson,Tanay Roy,Sharika Saraf,Anya Abraham,Hardeep Singh,Tim Kovachy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在耗散（激发态衰减）存在下，脉冲动力学去耦对失谐误差的抑制特性被颠覆，耗散反而产生强烈的失谐依赖干涉信号。作者通过解析与数值模型、自由空间锶原子干涉仪和超导transmon qubit实验验证了这一现象，并基于此提出一种新型光谱技术——耗散Carr-Purcell光谱（DCPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为脉冲动力学去耦（PDD）能够抑制驱动量子比特对静态失谐误差的敏感性，但该工作揭示：当引入耗散（激发态衰减）后，这种抑制特性会被完全改变。作者旨在探索耗散在相干控制中不仅是退化因素，而可能重塑系统对失谐的响应，并利用这一新机制开发新的光谱学工具。

Method: （1）建立解析模型和数值模型，捕捉耗散诱导失谐依赖的底层机制——即驱动演化过程中的衰减及其后量子比特保留的相干性；（2）在自由空间锶原子干涉仪和超导transmon qubit两个实验平台上观测该现象；（3）基于该机制发展Dissipative Carr-Purcell Spectroscopy (DCPS) 技术，并与传统Ramsey序列进行对比。

Result: （1）理论上证明耗散（激发态衰减）在脉冲动力学去耦序列中可产生强烈的失谐依赖干涉信号，颠覆了传统PDD抑制失谐敏感性的认知；（2）在锶原子干涉仪和超导transmon两个截然不同的实验系统中均成功观测到该耗散诱导的失谐依赖现象；（3）所提出的DCPS技术作为一种新型光谱方法，与传统Ramsey序列相比具有独特优势。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一个新机制：在脉冲动力学去耦中，耗散并非仅退化相干控制，而是重塑了系统行为。这一耗散诱导的失谐依赖动态有望广泛适用于各类量子系统，并为基于耗散的相干光谱技术开辟了新方向。

Abstract: One of the defining features of pulsed dynamical decoupling is its suppression of a driven qubit's sensitivity to static detuning errors between the drive field and qubit resonance. In this paper, we show that dissipation, in the form of excited-state decay, can change this behavior entirely, producing an interference signal with a strong detuning dependence. This signal arises from decay during driven evolution and relies on coherence retained by the qubit after such a decay event. We develop analytical and numerical models that capture the underlying mechanism and observe this same dissipation-induced detuning dependence experimentally in both a free-space strontium atom interferometer and a superconducting transmon qubit system. We also use this dissipation-induced detuning dependence as the basis for a new spectroscopic technique called Dissipative Carr-Purcell Spectroscopy (DCPS) and compare it with a traditional Ramsey sequence. Our results establish a regime of pulsed dynamical decoupling in which dissipation reshapes, rather than merely degrades, coherent control, and we expect these dynamics to be relevant to a wide range of quantum systems.

</details>


### [102] [Co-transmission of classical data and continuous-variable entanglement over a single quantum channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25179)
*Nicholas Zaunders,Timothy C. Ralph*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用经典调制量子通信（CMQC）协议解决位移型同时量子-经典通信（SQCC）与纠缠分发等量子通信方案不兼容的问题，实现了连续变量高斯纠缠与经典信息的同步分发，并分析了其在点对点及中继网络中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统位移型SQCC协议中，对经典信号的直接测量会同时测量并破坏量子态，导致SQCC仅能兼容点对点高斯量子密钥分发，而无法用于纠缠分发或基于中继器的量子密钥分发等更广泛的量子通信方案。

Method: 采用Zaunders和Ralph提出的经典调制量子通信（CMQC）协议，该协议通过在量子态上叠加经典调制来避免直接测量导致的量子态破坏。基于此，演示了连续变量高斯纠缠与经典信息的同时分发，表征了分发纠缠态的质量，并计算了点对点场景下高斯CMQC方案的秘密密钥生成率，与等效SQCC协议进行了比较。

Result: 成功实现了连续变量高斯纠缠与经典信息的同时分发；所提方案适用于基于中继器的量子网络；计算了点对点高斯CMQC方案的秘密密钥生成率，并与点对点SQCC协议进行了对比分析。

Conclusion: CMQC协议有效克服了传统SQCC协议的应用局限性，能够同时支持纠缠分发和经典通信，并具备在基于中继器的量子网络中部署的潜力，为同时量子-经典通信提供了更通用的实现路径。

Abstract: Displacement-based simultaneous quantum-classical communications (SQCC) protocols, as originally proposed, are generally incompatible with the majority of useful quantum communication schemes, such as entanglement distribution or repeater-based quantum key distribution: direct measurement of the classical signal also measures and destroys the quantum state, leaving point-to-point Gaussian quantum key distribution as the only quantum communication scheme amenable to integration with SQCC. In this work, we apply the classically-modulated quantum communication protocol proposed by Zaunders and Ralph [arXiv:2606.03181v3] to circumvent this issue and demonstrate the distribution of continuous-variable Gaussian entanglement simultaneously with classical information. We characterise the quality of the distributed entangled state and outline how the scheme is suitable for use in repeater-based networks. Lastly, we compute the secret key generation rate of the Gaussian CMQC scheme in the point-to-point case and compare it to the equivalent point-to-point SQCC protocol.

</details>


### [103] [Loss-induced anomalous generalized bunching in multiphoton interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25306)
*Rikizo Ikuta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了被动线性光学电路中，内部损耗与生存条件化可激活反常的广义聚束效应。作者引入条件聚束概率，并证明双光子输入下该概率始终单调，而三光子输入在最小三模损耗干涉仪中出现非单调异常——部分可区分光子条件下聚束概率最大化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义光子聚束效应在损耗条件下的行为，特别是生存条件化（后选择）如何改变聚束的层次结构。传统认知中，光子不可区分性越高，聚束效应越强，但损耗与条件化的联合作用可能打破这一单调关系，需要系统探究。

Method: 在被动线性光学电路框架下，定义条件聚束概率——即给定所有光子存活条件下，所有光子占据目标输出模式区域的概率。对双光子输入，给出任意电路规模和损耗配置下的严格证明；对三光子输入，在最小三模损耗干涉仪中进行具体分析与数值验证，发现非单调异常。

Result: 双光子情形：条件聚束概率对光子不可区分度始终单调，但多模目标区域可反转单调方向。三光子情形：在最小三模损耗干涉仪中，条件聚束概率呈现非单调行为，部分可区分光子时达到最大值；而无条件目标区域概率不存在此异常。这表明生存条件化损耗改变了广义聚束的最小层次结构。

Conclusion: 生存条件化与损耗的相互作用可在被动线性光学电路中诱发反常的广义聚束行为，打破光子不可区分性与聚束强度之间的单调关系。这一发现对理解损耗条件下的多光子干涉具有基础意义，并可能影响量子光学实验中对聚束效应的解释与应用。

Abstract: We show that internal loss and survival conditioning can activate anomalous generalized bunching in passive linear optical circuits. We introduce a conditional bunching probability that all photons occupy a target region of accessible output modes, given that all photons survive. For two-photon inputs, we prove that this probability is always monotonic for any circuit size and loss configuration, although a multimode target region can reverse the monotonic direction. For three-photon inputs in a minimal three-mode lossy interferometer, we find a nonmonotonic anomaly in which the conditional bunching probability is maximized for partially distinguishable photons. This behavior is forbidden for the corresponding unconditioned target-region probability, demonstrating that survival-conditioned loss changes the minimal hierarchy of generalized bunching.

</details>


### [104] [Actualization, Records, and the Emergence of Entropic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25307)
*Lionel Martellini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于记录的量子力学内在时间理论，将稳定记录的形成表示为对已实现信息的条件化，每条历史中累积的记录代数按包含关系排序。若某个结果的持续时间仅依赖于其条件玻恩概率、在序贯条件化下满足可加性、且连续并经过校准，则每个结果的实际化贡献的时长等于其惊奇度（负对数概率），从而确定性结果不贡献时长，而低概率结果贡献更大。累积时钟的系综均值是记录过程的香农熵，其矩母函数由Rényi熵谱决定，实现时钟允许Doob分解为可预测的熵补偿子与时钟涨落鞅。记录由可区分性、退相干性和稳定性的分级判据刻画。文章还澄清了多时钟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子力学中内在时间的物理基础，寻求将时间流逝与量子事件的实际化（如测量结果的出现）联系起来，而非将其视为外部经典参数。通过记录（records）来定义时间，使时间的推进与信息获取（消除不确定性）相关联。

Method: 采用基于记录代数的序贯条件化框架：将稳定记录的形成建模为对已实现信息的条件化，记录代数沿历史按包含关系排序。假设持续时间仅依赖于条件玻恩概率、满足可加性、连续性和校准性，推导出持续时间等于惊奇度（surprisal，即负对数概率）。利用Shannon熵、Rényi熵、Doob分解等工具分析时钟的统计性质，并建立记录的分级判据（可区分性、退相干性、稳定性）。通过局域上下文中的概率因子分解分析多时钟问题。

Result: （1）每个量子结果的实际化贡献的时长等于其惊奇度（负对数条件玻恩概率）；（2）累积时钟的系综均值等于记录过程的香农熵，矩母函数由Rényi熵谱决定；（3）实现时钟可做Doob分解：漂移部分为可预测的熵补偿子，涨落部分为鞅；（4）每个时钟增量即为对应实际化的信息增益；（5）记录由可区分性、退相干性、稳定性三级判据刻画；（6）单一语境下两惊奇时钟的可加性等价于该语境下玻恩分布的因子分解；纯二分态下所有局域语境对的可加性等价于联合态的秩一分解及所有2×2子式为零。

Conclusion: 该记录驱动的内在时间框架将量子力学中的时间流逝诠释为信息获取过程：每一量子事件的实际化贡献的时长等于其信息增益（惊奇度）。该理论统一了时间的概率性、信息论性质和鞅表示，并通过记录的分级判据和多时钟可加性条件提供了自洽的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a record-based account of internal time in quantum mechanics, where the formation of a stable record is represented as conditioning on actualized information, and along a history the accumulated record algebras are ordered by inclusion. If the duration of a realized outcome depends only on its conditional Born probability, composes additively under sequential conditioning, and is continuous and calibrated, then the actualization of each outcome contributes an internal duration equal to its surprisal, the negative logarithm of that probability, so that a certain outcome contributes no duration, whereas less likely outcomes contribute larger increments. The ensemble mean of the accumulated clock is the Shannon entropy of the record process, its moment-generating function is fixed by the Rényi entropy spectrum, and the realized clock admits a Doob decomposition into a predictable entropic compensator and a martingale of clock fluctuations, so that each increment is the information gain of the corresponding actualization. Records are characterized by graded criteria of distinguishability, decoherence, and stability. We also clarify the multiple-clock problem: in one fixed context, additivity of two surprisal clocks is equivalent to factorization of the Born distribution in that context, whereas for a pure bipartite state, additivity in every pair of local contexts is equivalent to rank-one factorization of the joint state and to the vanishing of all its $2\times2$ minors.

</details>


### [105] [Entanglement-based quantum key distribution with data in hollow-core fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25331)
*Yue Luo,Sheng Liu,Yun-Ru Fan,Da-Wei Ge,Zhi-Yang Liu,Hao Li,Si Shen,Zi-Chang Zhang,Hai-Zhi Song,Li-Xing You,Tao Zhou,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在18公里空芯光纤（HCF）链路上实现基于纠缠的时间编码高维量子密钥分发（HD-QKD）与经典数据信号共存，获得了10.56 kbps的平均密钥率，并理论上预测使用低损耗HCF可在200公里以上距离实现超过135 kbps的密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯硅芯光纤（PSCF）因高非线性效应而产生严重的拉曼散射和四波混频噪声，限制了量子与经典信号共存。空芯光纤（HCF）光主要在空气中传输，具有本征超低非线性，可大幅抑制非线性噪声，为量子-经典共纤传输提供了更优平台。

Method: 在18公里空芯光纤链路上，利用纠缠光子对实现时间编码的高维量子密钥分发（HD-QKD），同时承载双向接收功率为0 dBm的经典数据信号（理论容量达2.3 Tbps），并进行24小时连续运行测试。进一步结合理论分析，预测采用最先进低损耗HCF在更长距离下的性能。

Result: 在18公里HCF上，24小时连续运行实现了平均密钥率10.56 kbps，经典信号双向接收功率0 dBm对应理论数据容量2.3 Tbps。理论分析预测，使用当前最先进低损耗HCF，在超过200公里传输距离下密钥率可达135 kbps以上。

Conclusion: 与基于PSCF的系统相比，HCF方案在量子-经典共纤传输中展现出显著提升的性能，凸显了HCF在可扩展量子-经典共存网络中与现有光纤网络架构兼容的巨大潜力。

Abstract: The coexistence of quantum information and classical signals in a single fiber is essential for future quantum networks that leverage the well-established optical fiber infrastructure. Although multiplexing technologies can separate quantum and classical signals, pure silica core fibers (PSCFs) remain fundamentally limited by the high nonlinearity, which generates substantial Raman scattering and four-wave mixing noise. Hollow-core fibers (HCFs), guiding light predominantly in air, offer an attractive solution with intrinsically ultra-low nonlinearity and strongly suppressed nonlinear noise. In this work, we demonstrate the entanglement-based key coexisting with data over an 18-km HCF link. We achieve time-encoded high-dimensional quantum key distribution (HD-QKD) carrying 0 dBm of bidirectional received power, corresponding to a theoretical data capacity of up to 2.3 Tbps. During 24 hours of continuous operation, an average secret key rate (SKR) of 10.56 kbps is obtained. Theoretical analysis further predicts SKRs above 135 kbps over transmission distances exceeding 200 km using state-of-the-art low-loss HCFs. These results show significantly improved performance compared with PSCF-based systems and highlight the potential of HCFs for scalable quantum-classical coexistence compatible with the architectures of established fiber-optic networks.

</details>


### [106] [Remote entanglement of massive oscillators via wire-mediated Coulomb interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25341)
*Lorenzo Papa,Onur Hosten,Carlos Gonzalez-Ballestero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在机械振子附近放置导体结构，利用镜像电荷增强带电宏观振子间的库仑相互作用，使耦合距离标度从自由空间的 $1/D^3$ 改善为 $1/(D\ln^2 D)$，可将稳态纠缠距离提升一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电宏观机械振子间的库仑相互作用随距离急剧衰减（$1/D^3$），严重限制了利用中心力实现远距离量子纠缠的能力。因此需要一种方案来增强长距离耦合，以拓展宏观量子控制与基础力纠缠探索的实验边界。

Method: 利用宏观量子电动力学（macroscopic QED）推导在导体结构存在下两个带电振子的有效运动动力学；以圆柱形导线为具体模型，分析镜像电荷如何修正静电相互作用的距离依赖关系；结合连续位置测量方案，计算稳态运动纠缠的生成条件与可达距离。

Result: 耦合标度从自由空间的 $1/D^3$ 转变为渐进 $1/(D\ln^2 D)$，且对低频振子引入的退相干可忽略。对毫克级振子，纠缠距离可达数百微米，比自由空间提升一个数量级以上；未来系统有望接近两个数量级的改善。

Conclusion: 导体辅助库仑相互作用为宏观物体的量子控制和利用基本中心力生成纠缠提供了有效资源，显著拓展了带电机械振子间量子关联的可达距离。

Abstract: We propose a method to enhance Coulomb interaction between charged macroscopic mechanical oscillators by placing a conducting structure in their vicinity. We derive the effective motional dynamics of the two oscillators using macroscopic quantum electrodynamics and show that image charges induced in the conductor fundamentally modify the range of the electrostatic interaction. For the specific case of a cylindrical wire, we predict that the coherent motional coupling changes from the free-space scaling $1/D^3$ to an asymptotic $1/(D\ln^2 D)$ dependence on the separation $D$ between the oscillators, at the cost of only negligible additional decoherence for low-frequency oscillators. We further show that, when combined with continuous position measurements, the enhanced interaction enables the generation of steady-state motional entanglement between the oscillators over significantly larger distances than achievable in free space. For experimentally realistic milligram-scale oscillators, we predict observable entanglement at separations of several hundred microns -- more than an order of magnitude beyond free-space capabilities -- with improvements approaching two orders of magnitude in future systems. These results identify conductor-assisted Coulomb interactions as a resource for quantum control of massive objects and for the exploration of entanglement generated by fundamental central forces.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum teleportation over a field-deployed hollow-core fibre network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25352)
*Ri-Yao Song,Ya-Zhou Zhao,Yun-Ru Fan,Yang-Bin Ma,Yan-Yu Wei,Si Shen,Hao Li,Li-Xing You,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在成都部署的空芯光纤（HCF）城域网络中，实现了与经典数据共传情况下的量子隐形传态。HCF凭借极低的拉曼噪声（比传统固态光纤低约三个数量级）和无需主动稳定的光子不可区分性保持能力，在高达160 mW经典发射功率下仍能维持高保真度隐形传态，为量子互联网提供了一条与经典通信兼容的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隐形传态在实际部署中受限于量子信道损伤（损耗、噪声、波动），尤其在经典数据密集或背景光强烈的场景中，退相干和保真度下降问题更为严重。因此，亟需能够在共享基础设施中同时支持高保真量子操作和大容量经典通信的新型信道架构。

Method: 利用部署于成都的三节点空芯光纤城域网络，在共传经典通信流量的条件下，通过联合投影到一个贝尔态测量（BSM），实现光子量子态到纠缠光子对中远端成员的隐形传态。重点考察了HCF在无主动稳定下保持光子不可区分性的长期稳定性，以及拉曼噪声的抑制水平。

Result: HCF链路在无主动稳定条件下可长期保持光子量子比特的不可区分性；拉曼噪声比标准固态纤芯光纤低约三个数量级；即便在经典信道发射功率高达160 mW的情况下，仍能实现鲁棒的量子隐形传态。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个经典数据兼容的量子网络框架，可在已部署光纤基础设施上运行，且具有波长无关、即插即用、免主动锁定的特性，为迈向量子互联网提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: When a photon and one member of an entangled photon pair are jointly projected onto a Bell-state measurement (BSM), the quantum state of the photon can be transferred to the distant partner of the pair without physically transmitting this information carrier. In real-world deployment, however, teleportation performance is fundamentally bottlenecked by quantum channel impairments, such as loss, noise, and fluctuations, which induce severe decoherence and degrade fidelity. This vulnerability is further exacerbated in scenarios with intense classical data traffic or background light. Realizing scalable quantum networks, therefore, hinges on developing advanced channel architectures capable of supporting both high-fidelity quantum operations and high-capacity classical communications within a shared infrastructure. Towards this end, hollow core fibre (HCF) offers a promising quantum channel resource by combining free-space-like weak light-matter interaction with the stability of fibre-based systems. Here, utilizing a field-deployed metropolitan HCF network spanning three spatially separated nodes in Chengdu, we achieve quantum teleportation with an intermediate BSM under co-propagating classical traffic. Crucially, the HCF links preserve the long-term indistinguishability of photonic qubits without active stabilization, and exhibit a Raman noise approximately three orders of magnitude lower than that of standard solid-core counterparts. This noise suppression enables robust quantum teleportation even alongside classical launch powers up to 160 mW. Our findings establish a classical-data-compatible framework for quantum networking over deployed fibre infrastructure and offer a wavelength-agnostic, plug-and-play, and free-running pathway toward the quantum internet.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Teleportation toward the Quantum Internet: A Concise Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25395)
*Yang-Bin Ma,Yun-Ru Fan,Ri-Yao Song,Ya-Zhou Zhao,Yi-Ye Liu,Si Shen,Zi-Hao Zhan,Yan-Yu Wei,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 这篇综述回顾了量子网络和量子隐形传态在不同物理平台上的代表性实验进展，讨论了迈向可扩展、实用化量子互联网的当前挑战、开放问题和未来展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络是未来全球量子互联网的基础设施，而量子隐形传态是实现量子资源远距离分发的关键技术。量子中继器协议是实现任意节点间量子通信最有前景的方案。本文旨在系统梳理该领域的实验进展与面临的挑战。

Method: 本文采用简明综述的方式，系统整理了不同物理平台上用于构建量子网络的量子隐形传态的代表性实验演示，并沿着这一技术轨迹展开讨论。

Result: 综述展示了多个物理平台上量子隐形传态的实验进展，明确了当前量子网络构建中的技术瓶颈和开放性问题，并指出了向量子互联网实用化迈进的关键方向。

Conclusion: 量子隐形传态与量子中继器协议是实现全球量子互联网的核心使能技术。尽管已有显著实验进展，但在可扩展性和实用性方面仍面临诸多挑战，需要进一步突破。

Abstract: Quantum networks play a pivotal role in quantum information science, which not only provide a secure communication platform for remote access to quantum computers but also serve as the strategic core for achieving large-scale quantum information processing, forming the foundational infrastructure for the future global-scale quantum internet. Quantum teleportation, which enables the transmission of unknown quantum states over long distances by employing quantum entanglement together with classical communication, is essential for the distribution of quantum resources in the construction of the global-scale quantum internet. To realize a global-scale quantum internet, quantum repeater protocols represent one of the most promising approaches for enabling quantum communication between any nodes. This concise review presents representative experimental demonstrations of quantum teleportation for constructing quantum networks across different physical platforms. Along this trajectory, the review discusses current challenges, open issues, and future perspectives toward scalable and practical quantum internet.

</details>


### [109] [A General First- and Second-Order Numerical Solver for Non-Markovian Quantum State Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25427)
*Zhenning Cai,Quanhui Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非马尔可夫量子态扩散（NMQSD）方程的通用数值框架，通过解析求解线性NMQSD方程揭示非马尔可夫随机动力学的三种基本结构（随机传播、泛函导数插入和记忆配对），并据此构建了适用于任意浴关联函数的辅助态方法，给出了显式的一阶和二阶数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于NMQSD方程的非马尔可夫开放量子系统数值模拟受限于对随机过程的泛函导数处理，现有方法依赖浴关联函数的预设分解，缺乏直接处理泛函导数的通用数值框架。

Method: 作者首先推导了线性NMQSD方程的解析解，识别出三种基本动力学结构；随后构建了通用的辅助态框架，将数值构造分解为时间离散化、记忆积分求积和层级截断三部分；最后构造了一阶和二阶数值格式，并提供了显式实现的图解跃迁规则。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提方法达到预期的时间精度，并证明了该方法对不同浴关联函数和多能级量子系统的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了不依赖浴关联函数预设分解的通用NMQSD数值求解，为非马尔可夫开放量子系统的模拟提供了系统化的工具。

Abstract: The numerical simulation of non-Markovian open quantum systems based on the non-Markovian quantum state diffusion (NMQSD) equation is complicated by functional derivatives with respect to the stochastic process. A general numerical framework that directly treats these functional derivatives without relying on prescribed decompositions of the bath correlation function is still lacking. In this work, we derive an analytical solution of the linear NMQSD equation that reveals three elementary structures of the non-Markovian stochastic dynamics: stochastic propagation, functional-derivative insertion, and memory pairing. Based on this structure, we construct a general auxiliary-state framework for arbitrary bath correlation functions. The framework separates the numerical construction into time discretization, memory quadrature, and hierarchy truncation. We then construct first- and second-order schemes and provide diagrammatic transition rules for their explicit implementation. Numerical results verify the expected temporal accuracy and demonstrate the applicability of the proposed methods to different bath correlation functions and multi-level quantum systems.

</details>


### [110] [Asymmetric information scrambling and eigenstate thermalization in inhomogeneous XXZ spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25496)
*Shivam Mishra,Ravi Prakash*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀XXZ自旋链中相互作用梯度对信息扰频的影响，发现有限梯度会导致OTOC出现明显的左右不对称性，并通过ETH分析和解析推导揭示了该不对称饱和值的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性空间非均匀性在实验可操控的量子多体系统中日益重要，特别是相互作用梯度对非平衡动力学的显著影响。然而，非均匀系统中OTOC的行为及其与ETH的联系尚不清楚，这驱动了本研究。

Method: 采用确定性空间变化相互作用轮廓的XXZ自旋链模型；通过数值计算OTOC量化信息扰频的左右不对称性；结合ETH框架分析OTOC可观测量在能量本征基下的对角矩阵元；推导基于哈密顿量与OTOC可观测量重叠的有限尺寸饱和值解析表达式。

Result: 有限相互作用梯度（δ>0）导致OTOC出现显著的左右不对称信息扰频，即使在系统表现出量子混沌谱特征时依然存在，强相互作用侧的算符扰频受到抑制。解析推导的饱和值表达式与数值结果完全一致，给出了梯度诱导不对称性的微观解释。

Conclusion: 确定性相互作用梯度会在量子多体系统中产生OTOC的持久左右不对称性，该不对称性可通过哈密顿量与OTOC可观测量之间的重叠来解析描述，即使在量子混沌区域也不消失。

Abstract: Deterministic spatial inhomogeneity has become increasingly relevant in experimentally engineered quantum many-body systems, where interaction gradients can strongly influence nonequilibrium dynamics. Motivated by this, we investigate out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) and their connection to the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) in inhomogeneous XXZ spin chains. Using a deterministic spatially varying interaction profile, we show that finite interaction gradients ($δ>0$) induce a pronounced left--right asymmetry in information scrambling, as quantified by OTOCs. This asymmetry persists even when the system exhibits spectral signatures of quantum chaos, with operators on the strongly interacting side exhibiting suppressed scrambling. To elucidate the origin of the asymmetric finite-size long-time saturation value of OTOCs, we employ two complementary approaches. First, we analyze the diagonal matrix elements of the OTOC observables in the energy eigenbasis within the ETH framework. Second, we derive an analytical expression for the finite-size saturation value based on the overlap between the Hamiltonian and the OTOC observables, which explicitly incorporates the spatial interaction profile. The analytical prediction is fully consistent with the numerical results and provides a microscopic explanation for how deterministic interaction gradients generate the observed asymmetry in the long-time saturation of OTOCs.

</details>


### [111] [Automated discovery of high-probability heralded schemes for path-entangled states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25501)
*Marcello Armezzani,Colin P. Lualdi,Xuemei Gu,Paul G. Kwiat,Mario Krenn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用AI技术发现用于路径纠缠态的预告式线性光学方案，并将结果提升为一个全新的可扩展族，该族在效率上相对已知方案有指数级甚至超指数级改进，为实验可及的大规模多光子纠缠提供了理论和实践路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子间相互作用极弱，使得确定性组装大多光子纠缠态成为量子光学中的核心挑战。现有预告式线性光学方案效率受限，亟需发现更高效的、可扩展的生成方案。

Method: 采用AI自动发现技术，搜索预告式线性光学电路方案，针对路径纠缠态进行优化；将搜索得到的个体电路方案进一步抽象和推广，构造出一个全新的可扩展方案族。该族包含已知构造为特例，并自然延伸至更广泛的目标态类别。

Result: AI发现的方案族在效率上相较此前已知方案实现了指数级乃至超指数级的提升；给出了面向大型路径纠缠态的紧凑实验方案，并将方法推广至更广泛的目标态类别，展示了自动发现可揭示可迁移的物理理解。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了预告式线性光学领域的理论突破，并通过AI驱动的自动发现为实验上实现大规模多光子纠缠提供了可行路线，有望推动光子量子技术在分布式通信、量子增强测量和信息处理中的应用。

Abstract: Entangled states of light lie at the heart of photonic quantum technologies, from distributed quantum communication to quantum-enhanced measurement and information processing. Their practical generation, however, remains constrained by the weak interactions between photons, which make the deterministic assembly of large multiphoton entangled states a central challenge in quantum optics. In this work, we use AI techniques to discover heralded linear-optical schemes for path-entangled states and show that the resulting solutions can be elevated from individual circuits to a new scalable family. This family contains previously known constructions as special cases while generally providing exponential and super-exponential improvements over those, and its extension to broader classes of target states shows how automated discovery can reveal transferable physical understanding. By presenting compact experimental proposals for large path-entangled states, our results provide both a theoretical advance in photonic heralding and a route towards a substantial leap in experimentally accessible multiphoton entanglement.

</details>


### [112] [Collective states of multi-level emitters: The role of multi-level interferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25547)
*Nathan E. Rahat,Christiane P. Koch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了当充分考虑量子发射体的多能级特性时，光与物质的集体态如何与传统的二能级近似模型产生差异。研究发现，在发射体间距较近时，近共振跃迁间的干涉会彻底改变集体态的性质，并出现超辐射的间距下界；而在较大间距下，结果则趋近于二能级近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究光与物质集体态（如超辐射）时，普遍将量子发射体简化为二能级系统。然而真实量子发射体（如分子）具有丰富的多能级结构，近共振跃迁之间的干涉效应可能从根本上改变集体行为。作者旨在揭示这种多能级效应何时以及如何显著偏离二能级近似。

Method: 作者采用全量子理论框架，对紧密排列的多能级量子发射体与光场的耦合进行建模，重点分析近共振跃迁间的干涉效应。通过对比多能级模型与二能级近似下的集体态特征，识别出两个不同的物理区域（小间距与大间距），并针对光学晶格中囚禁的分子体系提出具体的候选分子和实验探测方案。

Result: 研究发现两个关键结果：(1) 在发射体间距较小时，多能级跃迁干涉彻底改变集体态性质，并出现超辐射的间距下界，这一下界在二能级近似中不存在；(2) 在间距较大时，多能级模型的集体态退化为与二能级近似一致的结果。两种区域均可通过光学晶格中的分子实现。

Conclusion: 多能级量子发射体的近共振跃迁干涉在紧密排列时对集体态有决定性影响，二能级近似在此区域失效。该工作为在分子—光学晶格平台上观测多能级干涉效应提供了理论指导和实验路线。

Abstract: We explore how collective states of light and matter differ when the multi-level nature of the quantum emitters is fully taken into account. For closely spaced emitters, interferences between near-resonant transitions completely change the character of the collective states compared to two-level approximations. In particular, we find a lower bound on the emitter separation for superradiance to occur which does not exist for two-level emitters. By contrast, for larger separations between the emitters, the collective states resemble those obtained within the widely used two-level approximation of the emitters. Both regimes may be realized by molecules trapped in optical lattices. We therefore propose molecular candidates and describe experimental signatures of emerging multi-level interference.

</details>


### [113] [Theory of Cubic-Phase Dynamics in the Linear Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25627)
*Maximilian L. D. D. Pellner,Georgi Gary Rozenman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了线性势中量子波包积累的立方相位（cubic-in-time phase）。该相位由三项贡献组成：内禀项、力致项和交叉项。力致项是形状无关的，而Airy本征态使形状相关项保持非色散。文章引入"本征力"（eigenforce）作为自然参数，揭示了立方系数在两个零线（静态Airy本征态和非平凡零点）上的因子化形式。该相位空间均匀但不可直接测量，仅能通过两个在不同势中演化的波包的相对相位获取。通过外差解调模拟的双Airy波包干涉，验证了理论预测达到亚百分精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究线性势（如重力）下量子波包的普适立方时间相位结构，揭示其物理起源和可测量性，并将其推广至超冷原子凝聚体、傍轴光学和表面重力水波等多个薛定谔型平台。

Method: 基于本征态无量纲化方法，引入"本征力"概念；将立方相位分解为内禀、力致和交叉三项贡献；通过对双Airy波包干涉的外差解调模拟提取相对立方系数，并与理论预测进行对比验证。

Result: (1) 立方相位系数可解析地因子化为沿两条零线的形式；(2) 力致贡献单独是形状无关的，Airy本征态使形状相关贡献非色散；(3) 本征力作为外加力的有效对抗者，影响焦散自加速和相位，但不影响质心运动（符合Ehrenfest定理）；(4) 相对立方系数在相同波包时被对称性禁戒，由制备不对称性激活；(5) 模拟验证与理论预测在拟合不确定度内一致，达到亚百分精度。

Conclusion: 线性势中波包的立方时间相位具有普适的三项分解结构，本征力是理解该相位的自然参数。该相位无法直接测量，但可通过不对称制备的双波包干涉的相对相位获取，为超冷原子、光学和水波等系统中的相位设计和测量提供了理论框架和可设计信号。

Abstract: A quantum wave packet in a linear potential, i.e., under a constant force such as gravity, accumulates a cubic-in-time phase that is universal across Schrodinger-type platforms and naturally realized by Airy eigenstates. Because the classical action is quadratic in the force, this phase comprises exactly three contributions: intrinsic, force-induced, and a cross term. The force-induced contribution alone is shape-independent, whereas the Airy eigenstate renders the shape-dependent contributions non-dispersing. An eigenstate-based nondimensionalization identifies the eigenforce, namely the intrinsic force underlying the packet's acceleration in the absence of an applied force, as a natural parameter. As a function of both forces, the cubic coefficient takes an analytically closed and physically interpretable form that factors along two zero lines: the static Airy eigenstate and a nontrivial zero at which the phase cancels without stationarity. This exposes the eigenforce as an effective antagonist to the applied force, not only in the caustic's self-acceleration but also within the phase, while leaving the centroid unaffected in accordance with Ehrenfest's theorem. Spatially uniform within each packet, the phase cannot be measured directly and is accessible only through the relative phase of two colliding packets, each evolving in its own potential. The general relative cubic coefficient, forbidden by symmetry for identical packets and activated by preparation asymmetry, therefore provides a designable signal. Extracted through heterodyne demodulation of the simulated interference between two Airy packets, its central value agrees with the prediction to sub-percent accuracy within the fitting uncertainty. The analysis spans ultracold-atom condensates, paraxial optics, and surface-gravity water waves.

</details>


### [114] [Coherent control of subradiant excitations in atomic rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25638)
*Milena Djatchkova,Igor Lesanovsky,Beatriz Olmos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了环形有序亚波长原子阵列中的亚辐射集体激发，展示了局域激发的绝热传输、相邻环间的相干转移以及基于相互作用的条件相移，揭示了该系统作为退相干保护的光子量子信息处理平台的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序亚波长原子阵列中的亚辐射态因相邻原子散射光干涉而具有极低的辐射衰变率，可用于存储和操控光子激发。然而，如何在这些系统中实现局域俘获、相干传输以及激发间的相互作用操控仍待探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，展示环形阵列几何结构结合偶极取向动态控制在量子信息处理中的具体能力。

Method: 作者采用理论分析，基于环形有序亚波长原子阵列构型，利用原子偶极取向的动态调控实现亚辐射激发的局域化。通过绝热演化方法实现单环上局域激发的传输；利用几何结构调控选择性实现相邻环间的相干激发转移；并通过激发间相互作用在相邻环中产生条件相移，等效于受控相位门操作。

Result: 研究实现了三项关键操作：(1) 单环上周域亚辐射激发的绝热传输；(2) 相邻双环间单激发的高选择性相干转移；(3) 相邻环中两个同时俘获的激发间相互作用诱导的条件相移，可解释为存储激发间的等效受控相位操作。

Conclusion: 有序原子阵列凭借其亚辐射集体激发可实现退相干保护，结合几何设计与偶极动态控制，能够实现局域激发传输、环间转移和条件相移等基本量子信息处理操作，是相干光子量子信息处理的有前景的平台。

Abstract: Collective excitations in ordered subwavelength atomic arrays can exhibit strongly suppressed radiative decay due to interference between light scattered by neighboring emitters. These so-called subradiant states make these systems a promising platform for storing and manipulating photonic excitations. The external geometry of the array, combined with dynamical control of the atomic dipole orientation, enables localized trapping and coherent transport of these subradiant excitations. Here, we theoretically demonstrate these capabilities in ring-shaped atomic arrays. Specifically, we show adiabatic transport of a localized excitation around a single ring, coherent transfer of a single excitation between two neighboring rings with geometry-controlled selectivity, and interaction-induced conditional phase shifts between two simultaneously trapped excitations in neighboring rings. The latter can be interpreted as effective controlled-phase operations between stored excitations. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of ordered atomic arrays as a platform for coherent photonic quantum information processing with dissipation-protected collective excitations.

</details>


### [115] [Landau Levels on the Surface of a Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25684)
*Almila Kura,Bayram Tekin,Mehmet Özgür Oktel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究带电粒子在包裹磁单极子的立方体表面上的量子力学行为。通过两片规范拼片保证波函数一致性，自然导出狄拉克量子化条件。利用规范修正的转动算符对态进行分类：偶数磁荷对应立方转动群 O 的不可约表示，奇数磁荷对应二元八面体群 2O 的旋量表示。采用规范协变的有限差分法计算能谱，发现类朗道能级流形受立方对称性劈裂。此外还研究了离散化立方体上的 Hofstadter 紧束缚模型，发现除磁子带结构外，立方体角点处出现额外的局域间隙态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平凡几何与拓扑背景下（立方体表面 + 磁单极子）带电粒子的量子力学，研究离散对称性对朗道能级的影响，以及连续模型与紧束缚模型之间的对应关系。

Method: 1. 用两片规范拼片覆盖立方体表面，由波函数一致性导出狄拉克量子化条件；2. 构造规范修正的转动算符，用群论（O 群与 2O 群）分类本征态；3. 采用规范协变有限差分法数值计算连续谱；4. 建立离散化立方体上的 Hofstadter 紧束缚模型进行分析。

Result: 1. 狄拉克量子化条件从波函数一致性自然得出；2. 偶数磁荷对应 O 群不可约表示，奇数磁荷对应 2O 群旋量表示；3. 连续谱呈现出类朗道能级流形，简并被立方离散对称性劈裂；4. Hofstadter 谱除磁子带结构外，在立方体角点处出现局域间隙态。

Conclusion: 立方体表面上的磁单极子量子系统成功将球面 Haldane 模型推广到非光滑流形。离散立方对称性以群论可预测的方式劈裂朗道能级简并，角点缺陷在紧束缚模型中诱导出局域态，揭示了几何拓扑与离散对称性之间的丰富相互作用。

Abstract: We study the quantum mechanics of a charged particle confined to the surface of a cube enclosing a magnetic monopole. The magnetic field is chosen to have a constant magnitude on each face and to point along the outward normal, preserving the rotational symmetry of the cube. We formulate the continuum problem using two gauge patches on the cube surface and show that consistency of the wavefunction gives the Dirac quantization condition. Since an explicit vector potential does not remain invariant under ordinary rotations, we construct gauge-modified rotation operators and use them to classify the eigenstates. Even monopole charges are described by the irreducible representations of the cubic rotation group O, while odd monopole charges require the spinorial representations of the binary octahedral group 2O. We compute the spectrum with a gauge-covariant finite-difference discretization and find Landau-level-like manifolds whose degeneracies are split by the discrete cubic symmetry. We also study the corresponding tight-binding Hofstadter problem on the discretized cube. The resulting spectrum contains the usual magnetic subband structure together with additional gap states localized near the cube corners.

</details>


### [116] [Nonlocal Magnonic Cat States in Hybrid Magnon-Qubit Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25643)
*Urmimala Dewan,Roson Nongthombam,Sampreet Kalita,Amarendra K. Sarma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于纠缠交换的协议，在由超导transmon量子比特与YIG磁振子模式耦合的混合架构中，通过对量子比特执行贝尔态测量，将初始的磁振子-量子比特纠缠转移至远程磁振子模式，生成空间分离的双模磁振子猫态，并通过Wigner函数重建进行量子态层析表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色模式（如微波腔、光学腔、磁振子、机械谐振器）中的相干态量子叠加为量子信息处理提供了超越传统量子比特编码的大Hilbert空间。然而，要实现可扩展的量子通信和分布式量子计算，必须建立能够支持此类玻色态的长距离量子网络，而当前缺乏在空间分离的磁振子系统间生成非局域猫态的有效方案。

Method: 1. 构建混合架构：每个节点由超导transmon量子比特与YIG球（支持磁振子模式）耦合组成。2. 先在每个节点内建立磁振子-量子比特纠缠。3. 通过两个节点量子比特之间的投影贝尔态测量（BSM），利用纠缠交换机制将纠缠相干地转移至两个远程磁振子模式。4. 采用联合位移宇称测量重建磁振子模式的Wigner函数，实现量子态层析。 

Result: 成功生成空间分离子系统的双模磁振子猫态（非局域磁振子纠缠态），并通过Wigner函数重建完成了生成态的量子态层析表征，验证了协议的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方案为实现长距离磁振子纠缠提供了一条可行路径，推动了混合量子网络架构的发展，为基于玻色模式的分布式量子信息处理奠定了基础。

Abstract: The quantum superpositions of coherent states offer an alternative to the conventional qubit-based encodings by harnessing the large Hilbert space available in bosonic modes, including those realised in microwave and optical cavities, magnons, and mechanical resonators. Beyond their advantages for local information processing, establishing long-distance quantum networks for such bosonic states is crucial for scalable quantum communication and distributed quantum computation. In this work, we propose an entanglement-swapping-based protocol to generate a bipartite magnonic cat state shared between spatially separated subsystems. Each subsystem comprises a hybrid architecture consisting of a superconducting transmon qubit coupled to a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) sphere that supports magnon modes. By performing a projective Bell-state measurement on the qubits, the initially established magnon-qubit entanglement is coherently transferred to the remote magnon modes, resulting in a nonlocal magnonic cat state. For experimental characterisation of the gener- ated states, we perform quantum state tomography through reconstruction of the Wigner function using joint displaced parity measurements of the magnon modes. Our scheme provides a feasible route towards realising long-distance magnonic entanglement and contributes to the advancement of hybrid quantum network architectures.

</details>


### [117] [Engineering a Quantum Thermal Diode with Floquet Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25678)
*Aqsa Rehman,M. Tahir Naseem,Adam Zaman Chaudhry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在最小量子热二极管中通过Floquet调控实现强热整流的新机制。系统由两个纵向调制Ising耦合的量子比特组成，分别连接独立热库。单侧驱动可打破互易结构，在抑制一个偏置方向热流的同时保持另一方向有限输运，并存在精确的阻塞条件。双侧驱动则引入Floquet泵浦贡献，完全阻塞需二者抵消。该工作建立了接触选择性Floquet修饰作为可控热流非对称性的设计原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热器件中，强热整流往往以抑制传输热流为代价，这种权衡在静态二极管配置中普遍存在。研究动机在于寻找一种能在不牺牲热流大小的前提下实现强整流的新机制，突破传统静态方案的局限。

Method: 作者构建了一个由两个纵向调制Ising耦合量子比特组成的最小量子热二极管模型，每个量子比特耦合独立热库。从微观系统-热库模型出发，推导了Floquet-LGKS主方程以解析驱动辅助的跃迁通道。分别分析了无驱动互易基准、静态失谐整流参考、单侧Floquet驱动和双侧Floquet驱动四种构型，得到了单侧驱动下的紧凑稳态电流公式和精确阻塞条件。

Result: 单侧Floquet驱动通过创建Floquet修饰接触与纯热接触的不对称配对，打破了互易结构。在弱正弦驱动下，整流效应随调制幅度的平方增长。双侧驱动时，Floquet泵浦贡献叠加到相互作用介导的电流上，完全阻塞需要两种贡献相互抵消。研究给出了精确的阻塞条件。

Conclusion: 接触选择性Floquet修饰是实现最小量子热器件中可控热流非对称性的有效设计原理，可克服传统静态二极管中整流与热流之间的权衡困境，为量子热管理器件提供了新的调控途径。

Abstract: Controlling heat flow in small quantum systems is a central goal of quantum thermodynamics and nanoscale transport. A key challenge is to achieve strong thermal rectification without suppressing the transmitted heat current, a tradeoff that often arises in static diode configurations. We establish a Floquet-control mechanism in a minimal quantum thermal diode formed by two longitudinally modulated Ising-coupled qubits, each coupled to an independent thermal reservoir. From a microscopic system--bath model, we derive a Floquet--LGKS master equation that resolves the drive-assisted transition channels. The resonant undriven device with left--right symmetric bath couplings serves as the reciprocal benchmark, while static detuning provides a rectifying reference with reduced heat current. Single-side driving breaks this reciprocal structure by creating a Floquet-dressed contact opposite a purely thermal contact. For this configuration, we obtain a compact steady-state current formula and an exact blocking condition for suppressing one bias direction while retaining finite transport in the opposite direction. In the weak sinusoidal-drive regime, rectification begins quadratically in the modulation amplitude. Dual-side driving adds a Floquet pumping contribution to the interaction-mediated current, so complete blocking requires cancellation of both contributions. These results establish contact-selective Floquet dressing as a design principle for controllable heat-flow asymmetry in minimal quantum thermal devices.

</details>


### [118] [Counterfactual Quantum Sensing: What Interaction-Free Measurement Can and Cannot Buy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25699)
*Christoph F. Wildfeuer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从估计论角度审视无相互作用测量，发现对于测量物体透明度，Elitzur-Vaidman干涉仪相比直接透射探测并无优势，Fisher信息量仅为后者的一半，且每吸收光子的Fisher信息相同。真正的优势在于二元鉴别任务——当备择假设是物体完全缺失时，每吸收光子的Chernoff信息随Zeno循环次数近似线性增长；但若在两个部分透明度之间鉴别则无增益。系统内寄生损耗是限制无相互作用传感性能的关键品质因数。此外，作者证明对于任意固定光学装置下单光子多次探测的方案，其可达量子Fisher信息不会超过将相同入射通量用于独立单次探测的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无相互作用测量被广泛描述为一种实现"最小侵入式传感"的途径。作者旨在用量子估计论的语言严格回答：无相互作用测量在传感任务中究竟带来了什么优势？厘清其在参量估计与二元鉴别两类任务中的真实增益，并找出限制其性能的关键物理因素。

Method: 将无相互作用测量建模为信道估计问题。以Elitzur-Vaidman干涉仪为分析起点，分别计算其在透明度估计任务中的Fisher信息、在二元鉴别任务中的Chernoff信息，并与直接透射探测方案对比。引入Zeno多循环方案，分析寄生损耗对性能的影响，推导最优循环次数与每吸收光子最大确定性探测次数的标度关系。最后，针对单光子通过无记忆、非色散物体经任意固定光学的多次相互作用，利用量子Fisher信息的凸性论证其一般上界。

Result: （1）在透明度估计中，干涉仪的Fisher信息仅为直接探测的一半，每吸收光子的Fisher信息两者相同。（2）在二元鉴别中，当备择假设为物体缺失时，每吸收光子的Chernoff信息约按Zeno循环次数N乘以其对数增长，即使对弱吸收物体亦然；但在两个部分透明度之间鉴别时无增益。（3）寄生损耗每循环记为η，最优循环次数N_opt ∝ 1/η，每吸收光子最大确定性探测次数 ∝ 1/η。（4）一般性负结果：任意固定光学下单光子多次探测的可达量子Fisher信息 ≤ 相同通量下独立单次探测，恢复了Massar-Mitchison-Pironio界，并指出其紧致条件。

Conclusion: 无相互作用测量的真正价值不在于连续参量估计（如测透明度），而在于"有/无"类二元鉴别任务。其性能最终受限于每循环的寄生损耗，损耗越低，可堆叠的Zeno循环越多，优势越大。更一般地，在无记忆、非色散物体和固定光学的设定下，多次相互作用并不带来优于独立单次探测的信息论增益，这一负结果为无相互作用传感的能力边界提供了清晰的理论限定。

Abstract: Interaction-free measurement infers the presence of an absorbing object from a photon that, in the counterfactual sense, never interacted with it, and is widely described as a route to minimally invasive sensing. We ask what it actually buys, in estimation-theoretic terms. Written as a channel-estimation problem, the Elitzur-Vaidman interferometer carries exactly half the Fisher information about the object's transmissivity that direct transmission probing does, and the two schemes deliver identical Fisher information per absorbed photon. For measuring how transparent something is, the interferometer buys nothing. The advantage lies in discrimination, and we show that what it requires is not that the object be opaque but that the competing hypothesis be the object's absence.
