Against empty space the Chernoff information per absorbed photon grows almost linearly, as the number of Zeno cycles times its logarithm, even for a weakly absorbing object; between two partial transparencies it does not grow at all. Parasitic loss in the cycle caps the advantage. The number of conclusive interrogations per absorbed photon reaches a maximum inversely proportional to the loss per cycle, at an optimal cycle number that is likewise inversely proportional to it, which identifies the loss per cycle as the figure of merit governing how far interaction-free sensing can be pushed. Finally, the negative result is not special to the interferometer. For a single photon meeting a memoryless, non-dispersive object any number of times through arbitrary fixed optics, the accessible quantum Fisher information never exceeds that of the same incident flux spent on independent single-pass probes, and is generically far below it. This recovers the bound of Massar, Mitchison and Pironio for this class, by a short argument that also identifies when it is tight.

</details>


### [119] [Benchmarking Optical Receivers for Quantum Communication and Randomness Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25702)
*Hamid Tebyanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文比较了连续变量、光子计数和混合光接收机在同一制备-测量框架下的性能，以半设备无关随机性生成为主要案例，利用基于能量重叠约束的Gram矩阵来描述源，通过半定规划从接收机统计中界定了最小熵，揭示了不同接收机在不同能量区间的优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光接收机的选择决定了传输态的哪些属性在观测数据中保持可见，进而影响不同量子协议的性能。然而，目前缺乏在同一框架下系统比较连续变量、光子计数和混合接收机的研究，尤其是在半设备无关场景下对随机性生成等任务的影响尚未明晰。

Method: 作者在制备-测量框架下，将测量设备保持非表征状态，通过三种方式描述源：能量推导的重叠约束、幅度-Gram基准、以及经认证的相干相移键控星座的完整复Gram矩阵。利用观测到的接收机统计数据，通过精确的半定规划（SDP）界定条件最小熵 H_min(B|X,Λ)，其中经典边信息与测量设备相关但与输入独立。复数多输入情况以块实数形式处理，并通过对偶证书验证。

Result: 纯光子计数对固定模长的相位编码是"相位盲"的，因此无法认证最坏情况随机性。连续变量接收机在中等能量下给出最高的认证熵；而在标称源校准下，当输出中保留信标区域标签时，混合接收机在低能量下表现更优。此外，相同的接收机统计数据还可用于离散调制连续变量QKD、量子读取、隐蔽通信和量子签名验证的接收机层面比较。

Conclusion: 不同接收机架构在半设备无关随机性生成中存在显著性能差异，连续变量接收机在中等能量占优，混合接收机在低能量且保留信标信息时更佳，而光子计数因相位盲性无法单独认证随机性。该框架为多种量子协议提供了统一的接收机比较基准。

Abstract: The choice of optical receiver determines which properties of the transmitted states remain visible in the observed data and therefore affects the performance of different quantum protocols. We compare continuous-variable, photon-counting and hybrid receivers within the same prepare-and-measure framework, using semi-device-independent randomness generation as the main case study. The measurement device is left uncharacterised, while the source is described by the Gram matrix of its pure signal states using an energy-derived overlap constraint, a magnitude-Gram benchmark or the full complex Gram matrix of a certified coherent phase-shift-keyed constellation. Within this framework, the observed receiver statistics are used to bound $H_{\min}(B|X,Λ)$ against classical side information correlated with the measurement device but independent of the input. For a fixed Gram matrix, this bound is obtained from an exact semidefinite program, with complex multi-input cases treated in block-real form and checked through the corresponding dual certificate. Photon counting alone is phase blind for fixed-modulus phase encoding and therefore certifies no worst-case randomness. Continuous-variable receivers give the highest certified entropy at moderate energy, while under the nominal source calibration a hybrid receiver performs better at low energy when the beacon-region label is retained in the output. The same receiver statistics also provide receiver-level comparisons for discrete-modulated continuous-variable quantum key distribution, quantum reading, covert communication and quantum-signature verification, without replacing the full security analysis required for each protocol.

</details>


### [120] [Verifiable blind probabilistic error cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25704)
*Bo Yang,Elham Kashefi,Harold Ollivier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了可验证盲概率误差消除协议（VBPEC），这是首个在完全恶意敌手模型下、将量子误差缓解（QEM）与安全验证相结合的协议。VBPEC 在抽象密码学框架下，将委托式误差缓解形式化为密码学资源，以完美盲性和指数级小的安全误差执行概率误差消除（PEC），且不增加量子空间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子误差缓解（QEM）是抑制硬件噪声的关键工具，但其可靠性依赖于建模、校准和实现，在不可信的量子硬件上缺乏端到端安全保障。现有的验证量子计算协议仅能容忍低于固定阈值的诚实噪声，无法主动对抗恶意敌手。因此，需要一种将 QEM 与安全验证深度整合的方案，以在不牺牲安全性的前提下提升验证的实用性。

Method: 作者在抽象密码学框架中将委托式误差缓解形式化为密码学资源，提出 VBPEC 协议。该协议将基于陷阱的验证从确定性通过/失败检查扩展为受益于 QEM 的统计测试，并开发了新的证明技术来整合额外的偏差来源。协议以完美盲性运行，安全误差呈指数级小，唯一的额外开销来自 QEM 过程所需的额外重复次数。

Result: VBPEC 实现了：（1）在完全恶意敌手下可组合安全的委托式概率误差消除；（2）完美盲性；（3）指数级小的安全误差；（4）无量子空间开销；（5）主动消除噪声，使正确缓解后的估计能以高概率被接受，而非仅容忍低于固定阈值的诚实噪声。

Conclusion: VBPEC 建立了通往近未来量子硬件上安全、可靠且实用的委托量子计算的关键路径，从根本上了提升验证的实用性。该框架首次将 QEM 纳入可组合安全范畴，弥合了误差缓解与安全验证之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation (QEM) is an essential tool for mitigating hardware noise without incurring space overhead. Yet, its reliability depends on modeling, calibration, and implementation, leaving end-to-end security on untrusted quantum hardware unresolved. We address this problem by introducing verifiable blind probabilistic error cancellation (VBPEC), the first secure verification protocol against a fully malicious adversary that integrates QEM. VBPEC brings probabilistic error cancellation (PEC), a widely studied QEM technique, within the scope of composable security by formalizing delegated mitigation as a cryptographic resource in the abstract cryptography framework. The protocol performs PEC with perfect blindness and an exponentially small security error. VBPEC retains the absence of quantum-space overhead from recent statistically-secure verified quantum computation protocols and from PEC. The only overhead takes the form of additional repetitions due to the QEM procedure. To achieve this, we extend trap-based verification from deterministic pass/fail checks to statistical tests that benefit from QEM and develop a new proof technique that integrates the corresponding additional deviation sources. Rather than merely tolerating honest noise below a fixed threshold, VBPEC actively cancels it, enabling correctly mitigated estimates to be accepted with high probability without compromising security. Our framework thus establishes an essential route towards secure, reliable, and practical delegated quantum computation on near-future quantum hardware: VBPEC fundamentally improves the practicality of verification.

</details>


### [121] [Classically Augmented Zero-Noise Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25746)
*Timon Scheiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种经典增强的零噪声外推混合误差缓解方法，用经典模拟替代高噪声外推节点，在最优shot分配下可实现指数级的方差缩减，并通过数值模拟验证了均方误差的降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子误差缓解中，零噪声外推（ZNE）方法在高噪声节点处采样方差极大，限制了整体精度。为此，探索利用经典模拟替代高噪声节点，以降低统计方差的混合量子-经典方案。

Method: 提出Classically Augmented Zero-Noise Extrapolation方法：将Richardson外推中的高噪声节点替换为经典模拟估计值（引入确定性偏差但采样方差可忽略），推导最优shot分配下的方差缩减公式，并针对线性节点间距和固定指数截断给出系数层面的理论分析。

Result: 理论推导表明，在线性节点间距和固定指数截断下，方差可呈指数级缩减；Pauli传播数值模拟验证了该预测，并在截断偏差足够小时实现了均方误差的降低。

Conclusion: 该方法在截断偏差可控的条件下，能有效降低零噪声外推的统计误差，为混合量子-经典误差缓解提供了一种有前景的新思路。

Abstract: We investigate a hybrid quantum-classical approach to quantum error mitigation. We propose Classically Augmented Zero-Noise Extrapolation, a hybrid error-mitigation method in which high-noise Richardson extrapolation nodes are replaced by classically simulated estimates. These classical nodes have negligible sampling variance but introduce deterministic simulation bias. We derive the resulting variance reduction under optimal shot allocation and show that, for linear node spacings and fixed index cutoff, the coefficient-level reduction can be exponential. We validate the prediction numerically using Pauli-propagation simulations and demonstrate a reduction in mean-squared error when the truncation bias is sufficiently small.

</details>


### [122] [Exponential de Finetti Theorems for Fermionic Gaussian States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25779)
*Jędrzej Burkat,Michał Studziński,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了指数形式的Gaussian de Finetti定理，将置换不变自由费米子Gaussian态的子系统逼近为几乎i.i.d. Gaussian态的凸组合，误差界呈指数衰减且维度惩罚为多对数级别（指数级优于标准de Finetti定理），并将结果从Gaussian对称态推广到更广泛的Gaussian不变态类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Gaussian de Finetti定理推广到指数形式的误差界，并扩展到更广泛的Gaussian不变态类，以克服现有工作中只考虑Gaussian对称态（即tensor matchgate表示的平凡不可约表示上的态）的局限性。

Method: (1) 证明了一个指数形式的Gaussian de Finetti定理：对于置换不变的自由费米子Gaussian态，其子系统可以被几乎是i.i.d.的态（在子部分上为Gaussian）的凸组合很好地逼近；(2) 将Gaussian对称态扩展为更广泛的Gaussian不变态，证明Gaussian不变态恰好是局部扩大副本上Gaussian对称态的部分迹，且始终允许纯化到更大的Gaussian对称态中。

Result: (1) 原始态与逼近态之间的误差界随无约束部分数量呈指数衰减，在子系统较小时变为超指数衰减；(2) 误差界的维度惩罚在局部Hilbert空间维度上仅为多对数级别，相比[Nat. Phys. 3, 645-649]的标准de Finetti定理有指数级改进；(3) 在完全i.i.d.极限下，该界恢复[arXiv:2603.12392]的Gaussian de Finetti定理；(4) 将de Finetti定理扩展到完整的Gaussian不变态集合，误差界的维度惩罚仅为多项式开销。

Conclusion: 该工作显著改进了Gaussian de Finetti定理的误差界（从多项式提升到指数衰减），并将适用范围从Gaussian对称态扩展到完整的Gaussian不变态集合，维度惩罚仅有多项式开销。这一推广意味着任何Gaussian不变态都可以通过局部扩大副本系统来纯化为Gaussian对称态。

Abstract: We prove an exponential variant of the Gaussian de Finetti theorem: the subsystems of permutation-invariant, free-fermionic Gaussian states are well-approximated by convex combinations of almost-i.i.d. states that are Gaussian on subsets of their parts. Our result provides an error bound between the original state and its approximants that decays exponentially in the number of unconstrained parts, becoming super-exponential when the subsystem under consideration is small. The dimensional penalty of our bound is polylogarithmic in the local Hilbert space dimension, an exponential improvement over the standard de Finetti theorem of [Nat. Phys. 3, 645-649]. In the fully i.i.d. limit, our bound recovers the Gaussian de Finetti theorem of [arXiv:2603.12392]. Previous works considered Gaussian-symmetric states, which are supported on the trivial irrep of the tensor matchgate representation. We extend these to a broader class of Gaussian-invariant states containing, for example, i.i.d. copies of single-replica mixed Gaussian states. We show that Gaussian-invariant states are precisely the partial traces of Gaussian-symmetric states on locally enlarged replicas, and always admit a purification into a larger Gaussian-symmetric state. This extends de Finetti theorems to the full set of Gaussian-invariant states, with only a polynomial overhead in the dimensional penalty of the error bound.

</details>


### [123] [Efficient Lindbladian Learning from Constant-Time Pauli Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25795)
*Jiaxing Song,Yukun Zhang,Xiao Yuan,Yusen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的Lindblad算符学习框架，通过将局域Pauli响应视为涵盖相干和耗散项的线性系统，解决了开放量子系统中相干-耗散模糊性问题。框架利用固定短时间窗口内的局域响应数据，通过反演分离两类贡献，并开发了两种算法：Chebyshev–Lobatto响应插值（对数级短演化时间）和单时间投影响应收缩（单个固定演化时间），均以$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(M/ε^2)$的样本和经典后处理复杂度估计$M$个候选系数至$ε$精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放多体系统的Lindblad生成元学习面临一个核心困难：局域响应在封闭系统中可直接揭示相干相互作用项，但在开放系统中可能同时包含耗散贡献，导致相干-耗散模糊性问题。现有方法难以有效分离这两类贡献。

Method: 研究者将局域Pauli响应建模为同时覆盖相干项和耗散项的线性系统，通过反演该响应系统分离两类贡献。在此框架下，提出两种算法：（1）Chebyshev–Lobatto响应插值，使用对数级短演化时间，后处理成本与$M$线性相关；（2）单时间投影响应收缩，使用单个固定演化时间并全局反演截断响应函数。理论分析假设候选生成元字典已知、耗散支持有界、且满足有界对偶相互作用图度或有界未加权局域强度条件。

Result: 两种算法均能以$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(M/ε^2)$的样本复杂度和经典后处理复杂度，将$M$个候选Lindbladian系数估计至逐项精度$ε$。理论结果建立了局域响应反演作为可扩展范式的地位。

Conclusion: 局域响应反演为从实验可及的短时间数据中学习、校准和诊断复杂量子系统提供了一条可扩展的路径，成功解决了开放系统生成元学习中的相干-耗散模糊性难题。

Abstract: Learning the generator of an open many-body system is more challenging than Hamiltonian learning: local responses, which can directly reveal coherent interaction terms in closed-system dynamics, may also contain dissipative contributions in open-system dynamics. In this paper, we address this challenge by developing an efficient Lindbladian learning framework for a known local candidate generator dictionary with bounded dissipative support and either bounded dual-interaction-graph degree or bounded unweighted local strength. The framework resolves the coherent-dissipative ambiguity by treating local Pauli responses as a linear system over both types of generator terms. Inverting this response system separates their contributions and makes the individual Lindbladian coefficients accessible from local response data in a fixed short-time window. Within this framework, we develop two efficient learning algorithms: Chebyshev--Lobatto response interpolation, which uses logarithmically many short evolution times and has a post-mean cost linear in $M$, with the stated dependence on $ε$, and Single-time projected response contraction, which uses a single fixed evolution time and globally inverts a truncated response function. Both procedures estimate $M$ candidate coefficients to entrywise accuracy $ε$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(M/ε^2)$ sample and classical post-processing complexity. Our theoretical results establish local response inversion as a scalable paradigm for learning, calibrating, and diagnosing complex quantum systems from experimentally accessible short-time data.

</details>


### [124] [On some potentials based on exponential functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25805)
*Francisco M. Fernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单且系统化的归一化方法，证明了一些看似不同的基于指数函数的势能实际上是等价的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不同指数型势能之间的内在联系，消除因形式差异造成的误解，建立统一的势能分类框架。

Method: 采用一种简单且系统化的归一化方法，仅要求势能具有一个极小值且在径向变量趋于无穷大时保持有限。

Result: 证明了若干基于指数函数的、表面上不同的势能之间是等价的。

Conclusion: 所提出的归一化方法简洁有效，能够揭示指数型势能之间的等价关系，该方法适用条件宽松，具有良好的普适性。

Abstract: By means of a simple and systematic normalization method we show that some apparently different potentials based on exponential functions are equivalent. Present normalization method only requires that the potential exhibits a minimum and is finite when the radial variable tends to infinity.

</details>


### [125] [Optimal Quantum Eigenvalue Transformation via Linear Combinations of Hermitian Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25812)
*Yanqiao Wang,Yixuan Liang,Hongjia Chen,Jin-Peng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种线性组合厄米矩阵（LCHM）公式，用于实现一般非正规矩阵的特征值变换 g(A)。基于此构建的量子特征值变换（QET）算法在电路深度和后处理重复次数上均达到最优，并可统一多种量子线性代数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子算法中，对非正规矩阵进行一般性特征值变换缺乏统一且高效的框架。作者旨在构建一个能统一表达 g(A) 的数学公式，进而设计出在电路深度和后处理代价上均达最优的量子算法，以解决包括驱动常微分方程、迭代方法、预解式、特殊函数变换等在内的多种量子线性代数问题。

Method: 提出两种互补的 LCHM 公式：(1) 普通 LCHM：对 A = L + iH，将 g(A) 表示为 g(i(H + kL)) 的核积分，内含 LCHS 作为矩阵指数的特例；(2) Weyl LCHM：通过角厄米矩阵 X_θ = cosθ L + sinθ H，将 g(A) 表达为 g(e^{iθ}(X_θ ± i(I-X_θ²)^{1/2})) 的积分。特别地，对矩阵幂 A^m，导出基于切比雪夫多项式的离散精确公式，仅需 N>m 个采样点，无截断和角积分误差，且后选择权重为 O(1)。基于 LCHM 构建 QET 算法，对 d 次多项式 p_d(A) 可实现最优 Θ(d) 电路深度和最优后选择重复次数。

Result: 矩阵幂的离散公式对任意 N>m 精确成立，无截断和角积分误差。QET 算法对 d 次多项式实现最优 Θ(d) 电路深度和 O(||p_d||_∞/||p_d(A)|ψ⟩||) 后选择代价。算法使用 Õ(d log(d/ε)) 个 Clifford+T 门，可统一处理驱动 ODE（归结为标准 LCHS）、迭代方法、预解式、log(I+A)、(λI+A)^ν、Sign 和 ReLU 变换以及非圆域 Faber 逼近等问题。

Conclusion: LCHM 公式为非正规矩阵特征值变换提供了统一的数学框架，由此衍生的 QET 算法在多项指标上达到最优，并以前所未有的统一性覆盖了广泛的量子线性代数应用场景。

Abstract: We discover two complementary linear-combination-of-Hermitian-matrices (LCHM) formulations to achieve a general non-normal matrix eigenvalue transformation $g(A)$. Firstly, for $A=L+\mathrm{i} H$ with Hermitian $L$ and $H$, the vanilla LCHM formula represents $g(A)$ as a kernel integral of $g(\mathrm{i}(H+kL))$, and it contains linear-combination-of-Hamiltonian-simulation (LCHS) [An, Liu, Lin, Phys. Rev. Lett. 2023] as the special case for matrix exponentials. Secondly, for the angular Hermitian $X_θ= \cosθL+\sinθH$, the Weyl LCHM formula expresses $g(A)$ via integrating $g(\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}θ} (X_θ\pm\mathrm{i}(I-X_θ^2)^{1/2}))$. For the matrix power $g(A)=A^m$, the Fourier projection of Weyl LCHM gives \[ A^m=\frac{2}π\int_0^π\text{e}^{\text{i} mθ}T_m(X_θ) \text{d}θ= \frac{2}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1} \text{e}^{\text{i} mθ_j}T_m(X_{θ_j}),\quadθ_j=\frac{πj}{N},\quad \text{for every } N>m \] with Chebyshev polynomial of Hermitian $T_m(X_θ)$ and $N$ samples. The discrete formula is exact, introduces no truncation and angular quadrature error, and offers $\mathcal{O}(1)$ post-selection weights.
  LCHM formulas lead to new quantum eigenvalue transformation (QET) algorithms. For a degree-$d$ polynomial $p_d(A)$ on $|ψ\rangle$, our QET algorithm can achieve optimal $Θ(d)$ circuit depth and optimal $\mathcal{O}(||p_d||_{\infty}/||p_d(A)|ψ\rangle||)$ post-selection repetitions. LCHM-based QETs unify various quantum linear algebraic problems with near-optimal $\mathcal{\widetilde O}(d\log(d/ε))$ Clifford$+T$ gates, including driven ODEs (reduced to standard LCHS), iterative methods, resolvents, $\log(I+A)$, $(λI+A)^ν$, Sign and ReLU transforms, and Faber approximation on noncircular domains.

</details>


### [126] [Lowering the implementation barrier of neutral-atom quantum computing with agentic workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25834)
*Constantin Dalyac,Alexandre Dauphin,Loïc Henriet,Christophe Jurczak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种智能体工作流，可自动将理论量子协议转化为中性原子量子处理器（Pasqal QPU）上的硬件实验，在保持研究人员关键验证环节的同时，实现了从论文/专利到量子处理单元实验的自动化流程。通过三个案例研究和对633篇Rydberg阵列论文的分类分析，展示了该方法的实用性，同时揭示了人类专家审查对于确保科学有效性的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算正从实验室走向工业级云端机器，但理论量子协议转化为硬件实验的过程仍存在重大瓶颈，涉及协议设计、编译、仿真和云端执行等多个专业领域，流程复杂且依赖多领域专家协作。作者旨在通过智能体工作流自动化这一管线，降低量子实验的门槛，使更广泛的科学界能够接触量子硬件实验。

Method: 引入一个智能体工作流系统，部署于两台云端可访问的Pasqal中性原子量子处理单元上，自动化执行从协议设计到QPU实验的全流程，同时保留研究人员的环路验证机制（human-in-the-loop）。此外，使用第二个智能体对633篇Rydberg阵列arXiv论文进行语料分类，评估其在当前硬件上的可实现性及所需硬件升级。

Result: 在三个来自多体物理和优化领域的案例研究中，智能体在一夜之间完成了从已发表论文或专利到QPU实验的转化。但人类干预至关重要：一个实验中智能体选择了不恰当的观测量，另一个实验中构建了看似合理但错误的硬件诊断，两处失败均仅通过领域专家审查才被发现。论文分类结果显示，近半数Rydberg阵列论文可在现有QPU上实现，其余则需要特定硬件升级。

Conclusion: 智能体工作流为理论思想与物理硬件之间架起了实用桥梁，显著降低了量子实验的准入门槛。然而，人类领域专家在关键验证环节仍不可或缺。该框架有望将量子实验开放给更广泛的科学界。

Abstract: Quantum computers are moving from research laboratories to industrial machines accessible via the cloud and integrated into high-performance computing facilities. However, translating theoretical quantum protocols into hardware experiments remains a major bottleneck, requiring expertise across protocol design, compilation, simulation, and cloud execution. Here, we introduce an agentic workflow that automates this pipeline on neutral-atom quantum processors (here two Pasqal QPUs available on the cloud) while keeping the researcher in the loop for critical validation. In three case studies from many-body physics and optimization, the agent went from published paper or patent to a QPU campaign run overnight. In particular, human intervention was crucial to ensure scientific validity: the agent selected an inadequate observable in one experiment and constructed a plausible but incorrect hardware diagnosis in another, with both failures detected only through domain-expert review. Finally, we use a second agent to classify a corpus of 633 Rydberg-array arXiv papers and show that nearly half are implementable on present-day QPUs while identifying specific hardware upgrades needed for the rest. Together, these results demonstrate that agentic workflows provide a practical bridge between theoretical ideas and physical hardware, opening quantum experimentation to a much broader scientific community.

</details>


### [127] [Bipartite Bound Information Exists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25838)
*Jef Pauwels,Nicolas Gisin,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了“束缚信息”（bound information）的存在性：存在一种由两方与窃听者共享的概率分布，其创建需要消耗密钥比特，但却无法从中提取任何密钥。作者给出了一个显式构造（两个比特加一个三态），并澄清了束缚纠缠态的标准基测量并非束缚信息的实例，但其纯化态的某些测量（包括可分离态）确实会产生束缚信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受量子理论中“束缚纠缠”（bound entanglement）现象的启发——某些量子态需要纠缠才能制备，却无法蒸馏出任何单态。Gisin 和 Wolf 在25年前提出：经典密码学中是否存在类似现象，即是否存在需要消耗密钥比特才能生成、却无法从中提取密钥的相关性分布？本文旨在正面回答这一悬而未决的猜想。

Method: 作者通过构造一个显式的概率分布（两比特加一个三态），证明束缚信息的存在。证明的核心在于揭示两种比较窃听者方式的差异：一个窃听者在所有互信息比较中都可以优于另一个，却仍然无法模拟对方的数据。此外，作者还分析了束缚纠缠 qutrit 态的标准基测量及其纯化态的其他测量，以厘清束缚纠缠与束缚信息之间的类比关系。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 给出了束缚信息的显式构造，确证其存在；(2) 证明了束缚纠缠 qutrit 态的标准基测量并非束缚信息的实例——只要其生成消耗了保密性，就能从中提取密钥；(3) 相反，纯化态的某些测量（包括可分离态）确实产生束缚信息，表明该类比是资源层面的，而非单个态及其测量结果层面的。

Conclusion: 束缚信息确实存在，经典密码学中存在与量子束缚纠缠相对应的不可逆现象。然而，这种类比是资源理论层面的对应，而非具体的量子态与经典分布之间的直接对应。这一发现深化了对经典与量子信息资源不可逆性的理解。

Abstract: Bound entanglement is an extreme irreversibility of quantum theory: certain states cost entanglement to create, yet no singlet can be distilled from them. Twenty-five years ago, Gisin and Wolf asked whether classical cryptography admits the same phenomenon. Are there correlations, shared by two parties and an eavesdropper, that cost secret bits to create although none can be extracted? We show that such bound information exists and give an explicit example, a distribution of two bits and a trit. The proof exploits a gap between two ways of comparing eavesdroppers: one can be better informed than another in every mutual-information comparison and nevertheless unable to simulate the other's data. We further prove that the distributions which motivated the conjecture, standard-basis measurements of bound-entangled qutrit states, are not themselves examples: a secret key is extractable from them whenever their creation costs any secrecy. Other measurements of their purifications, in contrast, do yield bound information, even for the separable states among them. The analogy is thus one of resources, not of individual states and their measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [128] [OmniQEC: discovering practical quantum error-correcting codes by an AI scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25865)
*Ge Yan,Shanchuan Li,Pengyue Ma,Qixin Zhang,Pingchuan Ma,Jianping Wang,Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: OmniQEC 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI科学家框架，通过"慢-快"协同工作流迭代发现适用于现代量子处理器的量子纠错码（QEC）。它在代码-电路-解码器协同设计框架下，发现了在98和240物理量子比特预算下分别优于BB码的硬件友好型QEC码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码的发现极具挑战，因为逻辑性能取决于代码结构、硬件、综合征提取与解码之间的复杂相互作用，这些因素往往相互制约。现有方法难以有效协同优化这些环节，亟需一种能够整合代码-电路-解码器协同设计的自动化发现方法。

Method: OmniQEC 将QEC设计建模为迭代发现过程，由LLM充当编排器（orchestrator），协调四个阶段：代码生成、代码级筛选、综合征提取合成、以及基于解码器的电路评估。核心创新在于自演化推理机制与"慢-快"协同工作流——快速循环利用低成本代码级代理指标探索候选码，慢速循环执行物理层面的电路级评估并将证据反馈回搜索过程。评估覆盖4个qLDPC构造族、3种LLM后端、每个后端14种物理量子比特预算。

Result: 发现的QEC码随着物理量子比特预算增加展现出持续改善的逻辑错误抑制能力。在98和240物理量子比特的完整实现预算下，分别优于[72,12,6]和[144,12,12]的BB码。发现的码具备硬件友好特性，对实际QEC部署具有独立价值。

Conclusion: OmniQEC 验证了LLM辅助、基于物理信息的代码-电路-解码器协同设计在QEC发现中的可行性，为可扩展容错量子计算中的纠错码自动化发现开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is indispensable for scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing. However, discovering QEC codes that remain effective is challenging, as logical performance depends on the interplay between code structure, hardware, syndrome extraction, and decoding, which often impose competing requirements. Here we introduce OmniQEC, an efficient AI scientist for discovering QEC codes suited to deployment on modern quantum processors. OmniQEC formulates QEC design as an iterative discovery process in which an orchestrator, implemented by advanced large language models (LLMs), coordinates code generation, code-level screening, syndrome-extraction synthesis, and decoder-based circuit evaluation. At its core, OmniQEC combines a self-evolving reasoning mechanism with a slow--fast synergistic workflow: a fast loop explores candidates using inexpensive code-level proxies, whereas a slow loop performs physically grounded circuit-level evaluation and feeds the resulting evidence back into the search. We evaluate OmniQEC across four qLDPC construction families, three LLM backends, and $14$ total-physical-qubit budgets per backend. The discovered codes show steadily improving logical-error suppression with increasing physical-qubit budgets and outperform the BB codes with $[\![72,12,6]\!]$ and $[\![144,12,12]\!]$ under complete-implementation budgets of 98 and 240 physical qubits, respectively. The discovered codes are hardware-friendly and may be of independent interest for practical QEC implementation. These findings pave the way towards LLM-assisted QEC discovery grounded in physically informed code--circuit--decoder co-design.

</details>


### [129] [Consistent Quantum States over Spacetime without a Common Quantum Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25899)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子时空态（QSOSTs）的一致性问题：多个记录条件下的量子描述是否能由单一底层过程实现。作者结合分支分辨QSOST干涉测量与正过程提升技术，证明每个正权重的QSOST分支具有唯一最小正提升，并将可实现性归约为确定性过程支配。通过构造最小反例（两个记录设置、两个结果的确定因果序双量子比特系统），作者展示了即使各设置分别可实现且共享相同的无条件QSOST，仍不存在联合实现它们的单一确定性过程，分离可见度阈值为η=1/√2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子时空态（QSOSTs）和关系量子描述的核心一致性问题在于：多个记录条件下的量子描述能否共有一个底层过程。但因果不可知干涉测量只能访问低维QSOST投影，遗留了未解决的过程自由度。本文旨在刻画这一"粘合问题"的操作性边界。

Method: 作者将分支分辨的QSOST干涉测量与正过程提升（positive-process lifting）和半定凸对偶性相结合。具体技术路线包括：(1) 证明每个正权重QSOST分支存在唯一最小正提升；(2) 将设置方式可实现性（settingwise realizability）和公共过程可实现性（common-process realizability）归约为确定性过程支配（deterministic-process domination）；(3) 利用该判据构造最小反例——两个记录设置、两个结果、确定因果序A≺B的双量子比特系统。

Result: 构造了最小反例：每个记录设置分别可实现，且两者共享相同完备无条件QSOST，但不存在单一确定性过程同时实现两者。分离可见度阈值精确为η=1/√2，存在涉及八个干涉正交分量的稀疏见证，且该分离在全秩过程中依然成立。

Conclusion: 建立了QSOST一致性与全局过程一致性之间的操作性边界，为检验多个量子视角能否共享一个标准量子过程提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Determining whether multiple record-conditioned quantum descriptions admit a single underlying process is a central consistency problem for quantum states over spacetime (QSOSTs) and relational quantum descriptions. Yet causally agnostic interferometry accesses only a lower-dimensional QSOST projection, which inevitably leaves some process degrees of freedom unresolved. We combine branch-resolved QSOST interferometry with positive-process lifting and semidefinite convex duality to characterize this gluing problem. We prove that every positive-weight QSOST branch has a unique least positive lift and reduce both settingwise and common-process realizability to deterministic-process domination. Using this criterion, we construct a minimal bipartite-qubit example with two record settings and two outcomes in the definite causal order $A\prec B$: each setting is separately realizable, and both share the same complete unconditional QSOST, yet no single deterministic process realizes them jointly. The separation has the exact visibility threshold $η=1/\sqrt{2}$ , admits a sparse witness involving eight interferometric quadratures, and persists for full-rank processes. These results establish an operational boundary between QSOST consistency and global process consistency, and provide a practical framework for testing whether multiple quantum perspectives can share one standard-quantum process.

</details>


### [130] [Effect-valued measurement models and contextuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25900)
*Samson Abramsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Abramsky-Brandenburger的层论上下文性框架推广到取值于凸效应代数A的分布，统一了概率、确定性和量子测量结构。文章区分了内部上下文性与可观测上下文性，证明非上下文性等价于一个锥规划的可行性，引入效应值上下文分数，并利用锥对偶导出Bell型见证。还分析了sharp可实现性与均匀膨胀，以及资源索引效应结构如何诱导分级单子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Abramsky-Brandenburger层论方法仅处理概率分布，无法统一刻画概率、确定性和量子测量结构中的上下文性。作者旨在用一个更一般的数学框架——凸效应代数——来统一这些不同的测量模型，从而在一个框架内研究上下文性的内部结构与可观测表现之间的关系。

Method: 1. 将分布的值域从概率替换为任意凸效应代数A，定义A值测量模型。2. 通过态σ: A→[0,1]诱导出普通经验模型，由此区分A模型的内部上下文性和经态评估后的可观测上下文性。3. 利用效应模的有序向量空间表示，将非上下文性刻画为关联锥规划的可行性，推广概率情形下的线性规划公式。4. 引入效应值上下文分数，利用锥对偶导出Bell型上下文性见证，作为Bell不等式的直接推广。5. 分析sharp可实现性和均匀膨胀，利用资源索引效应结构构造分级单子。

Result: 1. 建立了统一的A值测量模型框架，涵盖概率、确定性和量子测量。2. 明确了内部上下文性与可观测上下文性之间的区别与联系，给出两者在相干条件下的装配关系。3. 非上下文性等价于一个锥规划的可行性，推广了经典线性规划刻画。4. 效应值上下文分数与可观测上下文性见证之间存在明确联系。5. 锥对偶自然导出Bell型上下文性见证。6. 澄清了一般POVM与投影测量之间的关系，并得到资源索引效应结构诱导的分级单子，推广了量子单子。

Conclusion: 通过将分布取值推广到凸效应代数，本文成功地将Abramsky-Brandenburger层论上下文性框架扩展为一个同时容纳概率、确定性和量子结构的统一框架。内部与可观测上下文性的区分揭示了上下文性的层级结构，锥规划刻画为检测非上下文性提供了可计算方法，锥对偶给出Bell型见证则将上下文性与非定域性的统一理解推进了一步。分级单子构造则为此框架的范畴化提供了基础。

Abstract: We generalize the Abramsky--Brandenburger sheaf-theoretic treatment of contextuality by replacing probability distributions with distributions valued in a convex effect algebra \(A\). This yields a notion of \(A\)-valued measurement model encompassing probabilistic, deterministic, and quantum measurement structures within a single framework.
  States \(σ:A\to[0,1]\) induce ordinary empirical models, allowing observable behaviour to be viewed as arising from effect-valued measurement data. This leads to a distinction between internal contextuality of \(A\)-models and observable contextuality after state evaluation. We analyze the relationship between these notions and identify coherence conditions under which observable classical explanations assemble into internal ones.
  Using the ordered-vector-space representation of effect modules, we show that non-contextuality is characterized by feasibility of an associated cone program, generalizing the linear-programming formulation of contextuality in the probabilistic case. We also introduce an effect-valued contextual fraction and study its relation to observable contextuality witnesses. We show how cone duality leads to a notion of Bell witnesses for contextuality as a direct generalization of Bell inequalities.
  Finally, we analyze sharp realizability and uniform dilation for measurement models, clarifying the relationship between general POVMs and projective measurements, and show how resource-indexed effect structures induce graded monads generalizing the quantum monad.

</details>


### [131] [Sampling hard circuits with verifiably high fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25941)
*Simon Martiel,Jay-U Chung,Alireza Seif,Soumik Ghosh,Ian Hincks,Abhinav Deshpande,Bill Fefferman,Jay M. Gambetta,Ali Javadi-Abhari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出「结构化电路」，将采样型量子优越性方案的复杂度理论保证与量子纠错码编码相结合，在70量子比特、深度70的Clifford电路中掺杂468个T门，利用97个物理量子比特的时空编码，经综合征后选择将门错误率抑制10倍，最终以95%置信度获得保真度下界0.284的量子态，同时实现了错误抑制与可验证的量子计算认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样型量子优越性方案难以同时实现三个关键能力：(1) 超越经典计算的复杂度理论硬度保证；(2) 抑制硬件错误以实现可扩展量子计算；(3) 对量子计算本身进行可验证的认证。本文旨在弥合这三者之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者提出「结构化电路」(structured circuits)，其核心创新在于：在保留可证明的经典采样硬度（基于掺杂T门的Clifford电路）的同时，使电路结构天然适配量子纠错码编码。具体方案为：使用深度70的Clifford电路掺杂468个T门，在70个逻辑量子比特上运行，并通过时空码（spacetime codes）编码到97个物理量子比特上。验证方法利用电路结构与编码综合征测量，提供一种设备依赖但噪声假设显著弱于现有保真度代理基准的认证方案。通过综合征后选择（syndrome post-selection）抑制误差。

Result: 在97个物理量子比特上实现时空编码后，门错误率经综合征后选择被有效抑制了10倍。最终生成的量子态在95%置信度下具有0.284的保真度下界。该构造提供了一种系统性的方法，可将稳定子态（stabilizer state）提升为魔术态（magic state），同时保持错误检测的保真度证书。

Conclusion: 结构化电路实现了采样型量子优越性方案与量子纠错编码的有机统一，首次同时满足复杂度理论硬度、错误抑制和可验证性三个可扩展量子计算的关键需求。该方法为在带噪声的中等规模量子设备上展示超越经典计算能力提供了一条更具实用性的路径，尤其是在验证认证方面显著降低了对噪声模型的依赖强度。

Abstract: Sampling-based proposals are prominent candidates for demonstrating quantum computations beyond the reach of classical supercomputers. However, it has been difficult to combine their complexity-theoretic hardness with two capabilities needed for scalable quantum computing more generally: suppressing hardware errors, and verifying the quantum computation itself. Here we address both issues by introducing structured circuits, which, in addition to provable hardness guarantees, admit an encoding in a quantum code. This allows us to simultaneously reach high fidelities at high circuit depths, and to certify an experimental fidelity via the circuit structure and measurement of code syndromes. The resulting certificate is device dependent, but requires substantially weaker noise assumptions than existing fidelity proxy benchmarks. We demonstrate our proposal with a $70$-qubit, depth-$70$ Clifford circuit doped with $468$ $T$ gates. We use a total of $97$ physical qubits to encode this computation in spacetime codes, effectively suppressing gate error rates by $10\times$ after syndrome post-selection, and yielding a state with a fidelity lower bound of $0.284$ with $95\%$ confidence. Our construction is a systematic method for promoting a stabilizer state to a magic state while keeping an error-detected fidelity certificate.

</details>


### [132] [Equi-Entropic Maps for Four-Partite Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25954)
*Wojciech Bruzda,Zahra Raissi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究四体量子系统中一种弱化的纠缠均匀性概念，要求三个平衡二分具有相等（但不必最大）的线性熵。作者引入线性映射 Ξ，通过迭代平均的渐近极限实现熵的精确相等，并分析了其代数结构、不动点及与双酉矩阵和正交拉丁方的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对最大纠缠态（AME）是多方纠缠中高度受限的形式。作者试图放宽条件，探索四体系统中三个平衡二分具有相等（但不一定最大）线性熵的较弱纠缠均匀性概念，从而在更广泛的量子态空间中寻找具有实用价值的纠缠结构。

Method: 引入线性映射 Ξ，通过重排（reshuffling）和部分转置（partial transposition）操作强制熵的精确相等。该映射作为迭代平均过程的渐近极限导出，并具有基于张量指标置换的群论描述。对 Haar 随机酉输入进行主矩分析，并辅以数值模拟验证。

Result: 对 Haar 随机酉输入，主矩分析和数值模拟预测映射输出高度纠缠态，其共同熵随局部维度增长趋近最大值。刻画了映射的代数结构、不动点、渐近行为，并建立其与双酉矩阵（two-unitary matrices）和正交拉丁方（orthogonal Latin squares）的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种系统构造纠缠均匀态的新方法，扩展了 AME 态的理论框架。映射 Ξ 的群论结构和不动点分析为理解多方纠缠的代数基础提供了新视角，在高维量子信息处理中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled states represent a highly constrained form of multipartite entanglement and play an important role in quantum information theory. We investigate a weaker form of uniformity of entanglement for four-party systems of local dimension $d>2$ that requires the three balanced bipartitions to have equal but not necessarily maximal linear entropy. We introduce a linear map $Ξ$ that enforces exact equality of entropies under reshuffling and partial transposition. The transformation arises as the asymptotic limit of an iterative averaging procedure and admits a group-theoretic description in terms of permutations of tensor indices. For Haar-random unitary inputs, a leading-moment analysis supported by numerical simulations predicts highly entangled outputs whose common entropy approaches the maximal value as the local dimension grows. We characterize the algebraic structure, fixed points, and asymptotic behavior of this map and its relation to two-unitary matrices and orthogonal Latin squares.

</details>


### [133] [Observable Estimation in the Absence of Classical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25998)
*Samantha V. Barron,Bradley Mitchell,Vinay Tripathi,Francesco Grieco,Ilan Rosen,Francesca Pietracaprina,Davide Materia,Alireza Seif,Darvin Wanisch,Ramón L. Panadés-Barrueta,Ewout van den Berg,Jay-U Chung,Andrew Eddins,Sam Ferracin,Guillermo García-Pérez,John Goold,Luke C. G. Govia,Holger Haas,Ian Hincks,Jesse C. Hoke,Zoë Holmes,Su-un Lee,Youngseok Kim,Swarnadeep Majumder,Sabrina Maniscalco,Simone Montangero,Daniel Puzzuoli,Tomaž Prosen,James Raftery,Ricardo Rivera Cardoso,Max Rossmannek,Manuel Rudolph,Brendan Saxberg,Liran Shirizly,Karthik Siva,Joshua Skanes-Norman,Ilaria Siloi,Kevin C Smith,Boris Sokolov,Maika Takita,Yanting Teng,Mao Tian Tan,Joseph Tindall,Zoltán Zimborás,Matteo A. C. Rossi,Minh C. Tran,Sergei N. Filippov,Abhinav Kandala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套独立验证量子计算结果可信度的框架，在缺乏经典基准解的情况下，通过系统性设计量子启发式实验来检验底层假设，并引入算子Loschmidt回波这一新工具。将该框架应用于半置乱动力学模型，证明量子计算机提供的可观测估计在多种方法中最具可信度，并进一步展示了通过器件噪声表征与操控将可观测估计验证问题转化为噪声模型验证问题的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模相互作用量子系统的精确模拟超出经典计算能力，而现有数值近似方法虽在实践中有效却缺乏形式化保证。当量子模拟推进到这些近似方法失效的领域时，核心挑战在于：当可靠的经典基准解不可得时，如何信任量子计算结果？

Method: 作者提出了一个独立验证框架：首先引入"算子Loschmidt回波"作为新工具，将其应用于半置乱动力学物理模型；其次系统性地设计一系列量子启发式实验，综合检验量子计算的底层假设；最后通过器件噪声的精细表征与操控，将可观测估计的验证问题转化为噪声模型的验证问题，从而为量子估计赋以精度界限。

Result: 该框架成功应用于一个同时挑战多种主流经典模拟方法但实验上可行的物理模型。结果表明，在缺乏直接基态真值解的情况下，量子计算提供的可观测估计在多种考虑方法中展现出最高的可信度。进一步展示了通过噪声表征可将验证问题转化为噪声模型验证，从而为量子估计建立精度边界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条不依赖经典验证、通向可信量子计算以服务于科学发现的路径，为量子模拟在经典计算无法提供基准的领域中获得可信结果奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: The predictive success of quantum mechanics underpins many areas of modern science, even as the exact simulation of large, interacting quantum systems remains beyond the reach of classical computation. This success has been enabled by the remarkable advancement of scalable numerical approximation methods, which often demonstrate practical accuracy despite the absence of formal guarantees. As quantum simulation pushes into regimes where these approximations struggle, a fundamental challenge arises: How can quantum outcomes be trusted when reliable classical benchmarks are unavailable? Here, we establish a framework for the independent validation of quantum estimates in this setting and present evidence that they provide the most credible result among several considered methods, in the absence of an immediately accessible ground-truth solution. We apply our framework to the semi-scrambling dynamics of a physical model that strains several leading classical simulation methods yet remains experimentally accessible, in part through our introduction of the \textit{operator Loschmidt echo}. We systematically design a series of experiments using quantum heuristics that, taken together, test the underlying assumptions and provide strong confidence in the observable estimates obtained from the quantum computer. We then show how this framework can be extended to place accuracy bounds on quantum estimates via careful characterization and manipulation of the device noise, transforming the problem of validating the observable estimation to validating the noise model. These results establish a route towards trusted quantum computation for scientific discovery, independent of classical verification.

</details>

### [134] [Critical Ripples and Dirac Fermions in Crystalline Membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25767)
*Sebastián Bahamondes,Rodrigo Soto-Garrido,Enrique Muñoz,Vladimir Juričić*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文建立了狄拉克费米子与弯曲声子耦合的低能场论，揭示了在电荷中性点，旋转对称性如何使标量应变-密度耦合在长波平相中呈对数衰减，而弯曲模的反馈以幂律消失，保证了平相的稳定性。当弹性相互作用或电子软化在有限波长触发失稳时，体系进入波纹相，其临界行为取决于对称性：孤立波矢对下是玻色子Wilson-Fisher普适类，而当狄拉克质量型双线性与波纹共享动量和量子数时，涌现出杂化的手征XY Gross-Neveu-Yukawa临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索狄拉克费米子（相对论性）与弯曲声子（非相对论性）这两类动力学截然不同的低能自由度如何耦合，以及这种动力学失配如何决定耦合体系在电荷中性点的长波行为和相变物理。

Method: 构建低能有效场论，利用重正化群（RG）分析标量应变-密度顶点耦合的尺度演化；在有限波长失稳处，分析序参量对称性，将相变映射到已知的Wilson-Fisher或手征XY Gross-Neveu-Yukawa普适类，并利用单圈临界指数进行表征。

Result: 在长波平相中，耦合的无量纲强度对数衰减，费米子反馈以幂律消失，平相稳定；孤立波矢对的波纹转变由玻色子Wilson-Fisher固定点描述，狄拉克费米子仅为旁观者；当狄拉克质量型双线性可共享波纹的动量和水平反射宇称时，出现杂化临界点，属于手征XY Gross-Neveu-Yukawa普适类，费米子与玻色子速度在各向同性连续极限下锁定。

Conclusion: 动力学失配通过旋转对称性深度控制了狄拉克费米子-弯曲声子耦合的物理：它使平相免于微扰失稳，并在波纹转变中区分出纯弹性和杂化电子-结构临界行为，后者由手征XY GNY理论描述，具有可计算的临界指数和速度锁定现象。

Abstract: Crystalline membranes hosting Dirac fermions, with graphene as the paradigmatic example, combine two low-energy sectors with sharply different dynamics: nonrelativistic flexural phonons and relativistic-like Dirac quasiparticles. We develop the low-energy field theory of this coupled system at charge neutrality and determine how this dynamical mismatch controls the coupling between the two sectors. In the long-wavelength flat phase, rotational symmetry ties the renormalization of the leading local scalar strain--density coupling to the scale-dependent bending rigidity, causing its dimensionless strength to decrease logarithmically. At the same time, flexural modes become parametrically slower than the Dirac fermions, so the resulting fermionic feedback vanishes as a power law.The flat phase is therefore stable against this perturbation. The physics changes when elastic interactions or electronic softening destabilize the membrane at a finite wavelength, selecting a ripple pattern formed by modes at $\pm\mathbf{Q}$. For an isolated pair of ordering wavevectors, provided that commensurability-induced phase pinning is irrelevant, the transition is governed by the bosonic Wilson--Fisher fixed point, while the Dirac fermions remain spectators. A genuinely hybrid electronic--structural critical point arises instead when symmetry permits a mass-type Dirac bilinear to share the ripple's momentum and quantum numbers, including horizontal-reflection parity. The transition is then described by the chiral-XY Gross--Neveu--Yukawa universality class. Using the known one-loop critical exponents, we characterize this transition, determine the induced secondary elastic distortion, and show that the fermionic and bosonic velocities lock in the isotropic continuum limit.

</details>

### [135] [QCOEM: Quantum Cloud Orchestration with Evolutionary Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25358)
*Tam N. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Quan Le-Trung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: QCOEM 是一个量子云编排框架，利用进化算法（NSGA-II/NSGA-III）对量子任务调度进行多目标优化，联合最小化平均完成时间、执行错误率和负载不均衡，并采用增强成就标量化函数（AASF）从非凸 Pareto 前沿中选出符合用户偏好的调度方案。实验表明，相比噪声无关启发式方法，QCOEM 实现了零任务重调度和约 30% 的平均保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子云平台编排器采用噪声无关的启发式方法，忽略了后端特定的噪声特性、量子比特拓扑和队列状态，导致执行保真度下降、负载不均衡和频繁重调度。因此需要一种能感知后端噪声、兼顾多目标优化的智能调度框架。

Method: 提出 QCOEM 框架，核心包括：(1) 采用 NSGA-II 和 NSGA-III 两种进化算法对平均完成时间、执行错误率和负载不均衡进行联合多目标优化；(2) 使用增强成就标量化函数（AASF）作为基于偏好的决策规则，将非凸 Pareto 前沿映射为单一可调度方案，以对齐用户优先级。

Result: 在异构量子云环境中的广泛评估显示：QCOEM 实现零任务重调度，相比噪声无关启发式方法平均保真度提升约 30%，同时调度开销保持在可控范围内。

Conclusion: QCOEM 框架能够为量子云计算提供稳定、高保真度的任务执行和轻量级资源管理，验证了进化多目标优化在量子云编排中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Quantum cloud platforms need to dynamically orchestrate workloads across heterogeneous quantum computation backends whose noise profiles, qubit topologies, and queues vary over time. Existing orchestrators use noise-agnostic heuristics that ignore backend-specific errors, causing reduced execution fidelity, load imbalance, and frequent rescheduling. To address these challenges, we propose QCOEM - a Quantum Cloud Orchestration framework that leverages Evolutionary algorithms for Multi-objective optimization of quantum task scheduling. We compare NSGA-II and NSGA-III for jointly minimizing mean completion time, execution error rate, and load imbalance. To select schedules from a non-convex Pareto front, we apply an Augmented Achievement Scalarization Function (AASF) as a preference-based decision rule that maps the Pareto set to a single dispatchable schedule aligned with user priorities. Our extensive performance evaluation in a heterogeneous quantum cloud environment shows zero task rescheduling and about 30% higher mean fidelity than noise-agnostic heuristics, while maintaining bounded scheduling overhead. The experiment results indicate that our QCOEM framework can deliver stable, high-fidelity execution and lightweight resource management for quantum cloud computing.

</details>

### [136] [Repositories, Contributors, and Continuity: An Empirical Study of Foundational Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25437)
*Vincent Gierisch,Nicole Hoess,Ralf Ramsauer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文运用经验软件工程方法，对量子软件生态系统中的基础代码仓库进行分析，通过跨仓库活动与贡献者关系研究社区演化，揭示了贡献者迁移模式、跨项目知识传递现象，以及项目发展的三种路径：形成持续社区、融入成熟生态系统、或通过新软件制品延续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态系统发展迅速，许多新概念通过学术论文的软件制品或社区持续开发而涌现，但大量代码仓库仅在有限时间内获得开发投入，这些概念是否能跨越单个仓库边界而持续存在，是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 采用成熟的经验软件工程技术，对一组具有代表性的基础量子软件仓库进行实证分析，结合跨仓库活动数据与贡献者关系网络，追踪社区演化轨迹，识别贡献者迁移模式与知识传递现象。

Result: 发现三种典型发展路径：(1) 项目演化为持续活跃的社区；(2) 贡献者将概念整合到已有成熟生态系统中；(3) 活动通过新建或衍生软件制品得以延续。同时提供了贡献者迁移和跨项目知识传递的实证证据。

Conclusion: 该研究首次从实证角度揭示了量子软件生态系统中概念和影响力如何跨越仓库边界而持续存在，为理解开源量子软件社区的长期演化提供了初步的经验视角。

Abstract: Driven by contributions from academia, industry, and open-source communities, the quantum software ecosystem is rapidly growing. Across this ecosystem, new concepts often emerge through software artefacts accompanying scientific publications as well as through sustained development in larger communities. However, many repositories receive development efforts only over a limited period of time, raising the question whether their concepts persist beyond individual repositories. In this paper, we apply established empirical software engineering techniques to analyse a set of foundational quantum software repositories. We combine cross-repository activity with contributor relationships to study the evolution of communities. Our analysis provides empirical evidence of contributor migration patterns and indications of cross-project knowledge transfer. We observe multiple development paths: projects may evolve into sustained communities, contributors may integrate concepts into established ecosystems, or activity may continue through new and follow-up software artefacts. Our observations provide an initial empirical perspective on how concepts and influence persist across repository boundaries in quantum software ecosystems.

</details>


### [137] [KQFuzz: Knowledge-Guided Fuzzing for Quantum Libraries via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25647)
*Fuyuan Xia,Qixin Zhang,Chenhao Ying,Haojin Zhu,Shuai Wang,Yuan Luo,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KQFuzz 是一种知识引导的量子库模糊测试工具，它结合代码库知识、LLM 测试生成、适应度引导评估和两级变异策略，在 Qiskit、PennyLane 和 Cirq 三个量子库上实现了最高 18.44% 的覆盖率提升，并发现 13 个已确认 bug（其中 12 个已修复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的持续发展，量子库的可靠性和正确性变得至关重要。现有基于 LLM 的模糊测试方法存在灵活性不足和效率低下的问题，限制了量子计算领域的进展，因此需要一种更高效、更灵活的量子库模糊测试方案。

Method: 提出 KQFuzz，一种知识引导的模糊测试工具。核心包括三部分：(1) 针对量子程序定制的提示方案，策略性地融入代码库知识，高效生成高质量量子种子程序；(2) 适应度引导的评估策略，用于筛选和管理生成的测试用例；(3) 两级变异策略，在模糊测试执行过程中进一步丰富测试用例的多样性，以探索复杂执行路径并触发潜在 bug。

Result: 在 Qiskit、PennyLane 和 Cirq 三个主流量子库上进行实验，KQFuzz 相比其他 SOTA 方法覆盖率提升最高达 18.44%。共发现 13 个 bug，全部被开发者确认，其中 12 个已被修复。

Conclusion: KQFuzz 通过知识引导的 LLM 种子生成与适应度评估、两级变异相结合，显著提升了量子库模糊测试的覆盖率和 bug 发现能力，为量子软件的可靠性保障提供了有效手段。

Abstract: As quantum computing continually improves, ensuring the reliability and correctness of quantum libraries has become increasingly critical. To this end, many LLM-based fuzzing approaches towards quantum libraries have been proposed to uncover potential bugs. However, these methods still suffer from limitations such as insufficient flexibility and low efficiency, which hinder the progress of the quantum computing field. To address these challenges, we propose KQFuzz, a novel knowledge-guided fuzzer for quantum libraries. It leverages comprehensive codebase knowledge to ground LLM-based test generation, synergizing this with fitness-guided evaluation and two-level mutations to explore complex execution paths and trigger potential bugs. Firstly, KQFuzz introduces a novel prompting scheme tailored to quantum programs, which strategically incorporates knowledge of the codebase to efficiently generate high-quality quantum seed programs. Moreover, we develop evaluation and mutation strategies to handle the generated seed programs, facilitating efficient fuzzing execution while further enriching the diversity of the resulting test cases. We implement KQFuzz and conduct fuzzing on three popular quantum libraries, including Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, with coverage improved by up to 18.44%. During the development of KQFuzz, we discovered 13 bugs, all of which have been confirmed and 12 have already been fixed by the developers.

</details>

### [138] [An Investigation into the Effect of Mobile Ions on the Steady State Performance of Perovskite Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25951)
*Matthew V. Cowley,Kjeld O. Jensen,Matthew J. Wolf,Alison B. Walker,Petra J. Cameron*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 通过漂移扩散建模与因子分析，对1202个钙钛矿太阳能电池进行模拟，系统研究移动离子对稳态性能的影响。结论是：在高效器件中，移动离子对稳态性能影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清移动离子在钙钛矿太阳能电池中究竟是"有益"还是"有害"这一争议性问题。以往研究对此结论不一，需要系统性地解耦各关键参数的影响。

Method: 采用漂移扩散建模结合因子分析，模拟601对（共1202个）钙钛矿太阳能电池，涵盖广泛的物理相关材料参数。每对中，一个电池允许离子自由移动，另一个则完全不含移动离子，从而对照研究。模拟覆盖n-i-p和p-i-n两种结构，以及有机和无机接触层，并解耦离子密度、复合速率、能带偏移等关键参数的贡献。

Result: 在高效器件中，移动离子对稳态性能仅产生很小的影响。通过因子分析解耦了离子密度、复合速率、能带偏移等参数在各器件构型中的贡献。

Conclusion: 移动离子的存在与否并不会显著改变高效钙钛矿太阳能电池的稳态性能，这为调和此前关于移动离子利弊的争议提供了系统性的量化证据。

Abstract: In perovskite solar cells, the interplay between mobile ions and photo-excited charge carriers is complex,with recent studies suggesting mobile ions can be either beneficial or detrimental to cell efficiency depending on the cell properties. In this study we use drift diffusion modelling and factorial analysis to simulate 601 pairs of perovskite solar cells (1202 total cells) across a broad range of physically relevant materials parameters. In each pair of devices, one cell contains mobile ions that can move freely. The second paired cell is identical but has no mobile ions. This approach allows us to systematically investigate the impact of mobile ions on cell performance. We deconvolve the contributions of key cell parameters including ion density, recombination rate and band offsets, for n-i-p and p-i-n devices with both organic and inorganic contact layers. Importantly, we find that in efficient devices, mobile ions have only a small impact on steady-state performance.

</details>


### [139] [Bosonic Condensed Phase Real-time Dynamics from Ring Polymer Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25892)
*Yotam M. Y. Feldman,Sourav Karmakar,Sutirtha Paul,Jacob Higer,Adrian Del Maestro,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种玻色子环聚合物分子动力学（bosonic RPMD）方法，用于近似计算玻色凝聚相的实时关联函数和量子输运系数，克服了路径积分中的动力学符号问题，并首次实现了大规模玻色凝聚相有限温度实时动力学的直接模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子实时关联函数的直接计算面临严重的动力学符号问题，虚时关联函数的解析延拓是病态问题，而现有RPMD方法虽能准确近似凝聚相实时关联函数，却忽略了玻色子的交换效应。因此需要一种能纳入交换对称性的玻色子RPMD方法。

Method: 作者发展了玻色子RPMD方法，该方法在谐振子极限、短时极限和高温极限下是精确的。关键洞察在于：只有当关联观测量在交换下对称时，玻色子的Kubo变换关联函数才是实的。方法在谐振和非谐振模型系统上进行了基准测试，并用于直接计算Lieb-Liniger气体在不同温度和动量转移下的密度-密度实时关联函数。

Result: 该方法在谐振和非谐振模型系统上验证了准确性，并成功获得了Lieb-Liniger气体在不同温度和动量转移下的密度-密度实时关联函数，首次实现了大规模玻色凝聚相的有限温度实时动力学直接模拟。

Conclusion: 玻色子RPMD方法解决了传统RPMD忽略交换效应的局限，为大规模玻色凝聚相的有限温度实时动力学模拟开辟了新途径，在谐振子、短时和高温三个极限下严格精确。

Abstract: We present a method for approximating real-time correlation functions and quantum transport coefficients of bosonic condensed phases. The direct evaluation of quantum real-time correlation functions in the path integral formulation is impossible due to a severe dynamical sign problem. Evaluating imaginary-time correlation functions and inverting them using analytic continuation is famously ill-posed. Ring polymer molecular dynamics (RPMD) provides an alternative approach resulting in accurate approximations for real-time correlation functions of condensed phases but neglects exchange effects. In this work, we develop a bosonic RPMD method, which is exact in the harmonic, short time, and high temperature limits. A critical enabling observation is that the Kubo-transformed correlation function for bosons is real only when the correlated observables are symmetric under exchange. We benchmark the method on harmonic and anharmonic model systems and then use it to directly obtain the Lieb-Liniger gas density-density real-time correlation functions at various temperatures and momentum transfers. This work enables the direct simulation of finite-temperature, real-time dynamics of large bosonic condensed phases using RPMD for the first time.

</details>


### [140] [Structured High-Angular-Momentum Coulomb Tensors from Real and Complex Solid-Harmonic Integral Engines: A Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25245)
*Bo Peng*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文论述了基于实/复固态谐函数的电子排斥积分直接算法。相比传统的笛卡尔高斯函数路线（从d壳层起产生冗余函数），固态谐函数引擎直接在目标球谐空间工作，生成更小的库仑张量并保留角动量结构信息。该结构可桥接低秩分解、局域哈密顿构造、量子模拟等后续计算，实现了高效积分生成与结构化多电子计算的直接衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电子排斥积分算法采用笛卡尔高斯函数生成，再变换到球谐基。但从d壳层开始，笛卡尔表示包含的函数多于球谐空间实际所需，造成冗余。作者旨在论证直接使用固态谐函数引擎的优势：在目标空间内直接工作，保留角动量量子数标签与相位结构，从而与下游多电子计算方法无缝衔接。

Method: 采用实或复固态谐函数引擎直接生成电子排斥积分，避免笛卡尔高斯中间表示。通过简单的解析计数方法，量化张量规模、角动量块、径向Slater-Condon参数以及对空间工作量。分析固态谐函数张量的角动量结构如何与低秩分解、局域哈密顿构造、量子模拟、旋量或有效模型基组变换等算法建立直接联系。

Result: 固态谐函数积分引擎产生的库仑张量规模更小，且天然保留磁量子数标签、排序和相位信息。解析计数清晰给出了张量大小、角动量块划分、Slater-Condon参数数目以及对空间工作量，这些量可直接指导后续的低秩分解、局域哈密顿构造、量子模拟等结构化计算。

Conclusion: 固态谐函数积分引擎在高效积分生成与结构化多电子计算之间架起了直接桥梁，避免了笛卡尔路线的冗余，并将积分输出的角动量结构信息完整传递至下游算法，是一种更为紧凑且信息保持的积分策略。

Abstract: Electron-repulsion integrals describe the Coulomb interaction between charge distributions built from orbital basis functions. Most integral algorithms generate these quantities through Cartesian Gaussian functions, whose angular shapes are written as powers of $x$, $y$, and $z$, and then transform the result to spherical functions. This route is effective, but from $d$ shells onward the Cartesian representation contains more functions than the spherical space required by the calculation. Direct real or complex solid-harmonic engines work in that target space from the beginning. They therefore produce a smaller final Coulomb tensor while preserving the ordering, phase, and magnetic-quantum-number labels that describe its angular structure. Following this structure beyond integral evaluation reveals direct connections to the algorithms that use the tensor. Simple analytical counts quantify tensor size, angular blocks, radial Slater--Condon parameters, and pair-space work. These quantities guide low-rank factorization, local Hamiltonian construction, quantum simulation, and transformations to spinor or effective-model bases. In this way, solid-harmonic integral engines provide a direct bridge between efficient integral generation and structured many-electron computation.

</details>

### [141] [Parametrized beyond-Teukolsky framework in the time domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25311)
*Ciro De Simone,Sebastian H. Völkel,Kostas D. Kokkotas,Salvatore Capozziello*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次在时域中实现了参数化超越Teukolsky框架，通过(2+1)维高斯波包散射实验，研究了广义相对论修正对黑洞扰动响应的完整线性演化，包括初始响应、铃荡和晚期尾迹，并分析了形变参数对铃荡振幅、相位及幂律衰减起始时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前参数化超越Teukolsky框架的铃荡分析仅局限于频域本征值问题，缺乏时域演化研究，无法完整刻画从初始响应到晚期尾迹的全过程，也难以评估线性近似的适用边界。

Method: 在(2+1)维时域中实现参数化超越Teukolsky框架，采用高斯波包进行散射实验，获得扰动的完整线性演化。以频域本征值结果为基准验证精度，并在线性化修正势模型中估计二次项与混合项系数，用于诊断线性近似的有效范围。

Result: 低方位角模数与频域基准吻合良好；高方位角模存在模式混合，限制模提取精度。形变参数显著影响铃荡振幅与相位，趋势可由修正势的近视界结构解释。近视界形变不改变晚期尾迹幂律指数，但显著偏移幂律衰减的起始时刻。二次项与混合系数提供了线性近似有效性的定量判据。

Conclusion: 时域演化方法为黑洞微扰理论中检验广义相对论的参数化偏离提供了互补且灵活的计算框架，能够全面揭示从铃荡到晚期尾迹的形变效应。

Abstract: Modifications to General Relativity can significantly alter the perturbative response of black holes, leaving imprints on quasinormal-mode spectra, waveform amplitudes and phases, and late-time tails. The parametrized beyond-Teukolsky framework was introduced to capture possible deviations from Kerr dynamics, but the ringdown has so far only been explored as an eigenvalue problem. We present the first time-domain implementation of this framework and perform (2+1)-dimensional scattering experiments with Gaussian wave packets. This approach provides the full linear evolution of the perturbation, from the initial prompt response through the ringdown and into the late-time regime. Using frequency-domain eigenvalue results as benchmarks, we find excellent agreement for low azimuthal numbers, while higher azimuthal numbers are affected by mode mixing, which limits the precision of the extracted modes. We further use the time-domain waveforms to estimate quadratic and mixed coefficients within the linearized modified-potential model, providing a diagnostic for the regime of validity of the linear approximation. Beyond mode frequencies, we show that the deformation parameters can strongly affect the ringdown amplitude and phase, with trends that can be understood from the near-horizon structure of the modified potential. We also analyze the late- time behavior, finding that near-horizon deformations leave the tail exponent unchanged but can substantially shift the onset of the power-law decay. These results demonstrate that time-domain evolutions provide a complementary and flexible framework for testing parametrized deviations from General Relativity in black-hole perturbation theory.

</details>


### [142] [Memory Effect for deformed gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25452)
*P. -M. Zhang,M. Elbistan,P. A. Horvathy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了夹层引力波的形变。通过纯位移参数压缩波至脉冲波时，位移记忆特性丢失，仅剩速度效应。对于每个固定的形变参数p，可通过增大振幅恢复位移记忆，但p→∞时振幅发散，无法得到脉冲波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究夹层引力波在形变过程中的性质变化，特别关注位移记忆（DM）和速度记忆（VM）在脉冲极限下的行为，试图理解形变参数与振幅之间的制约关系。

Method: 对夹层引力波施加形变操作，以纯位移参数将波压缩至脉冲波极限，分析DM和VM的保持与丢失情况；进一步考察在不同形变参数p下，通过增加振幅恢复DM的可能性。

Result: （1）用纯DM参数压缩波至脉冲波时，DM性质丢失，仅保留VM效应。（2）对于每个固定p值，DM可通过进一步增大振幅恢复。（3）当p→∞时，所需振幅发散，因此无法获得脉冲波。

Conclusion: 夹层引力波的形变揭示了DM与VM之间的非平凡转换关系：脉冲极限下DM自然丢失，虽可通过调幅恢复，但在形变参数无穷大极限下振幅发散，意味着不存在同时保持DM的脉冲引力波构型。

Abstract: Deformations of sandwich gravitational waves are considered. Squeezing a wave with pure displacement (DM) parameters to an impulsive wave, the DM property is lost, leaving us with the velocity effect (VM). For each fixed value of the deformation parameter $p$, DM can be restored by further increasing the amplitude which however diverge when $p\to\infty$, and no impulsive wave is obtained.

</details>


### [143] [Gravitational wave constraints on corrections to Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula in classical F(R) gravity and quantum GR : implications for theory parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25513)
*Parthasarathi Majumdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）引力波数据对黑洞面积定理的验证结果，约束了经典F(R)引力中的熵修正以及来自圈量子引力与纠缠熵的量子修正，进而对超出标准模型的粒子谱（包括暗物质候选者）的自旋与种类数给出严格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVK近期观测（GW250114）以5σ精度验证了霍金面积定理，这为从观测上约束黑洞熵对贝肯斯坦-霍金面积公式的修正提供了前所未有的契机。作者旨在利用这一观测事实，约束经典修正引力理论和量子引力理论中黑洞熵的领头阶修正项。

Method: 采用Wald熵函数形式，推导F(R)引力中大面积球对称黑洞的熵修正（领头阶为面积倒数项）；结合圈量子引力（非微扰、无物质）和纠缠熵（微扰、含物质）两种量子广义相对论修正带来的对数面积修正；引入“绝对一致性”判据，要求理论修正与面积定理的5σ观测验证相容，从而导出对理论参数的限制。

Result: （1）绝对一致性条件关联了F(R)引力的参数；（2）结合两种量子修正的对数项并施加绝对一致性后，对超出标准模型粒子的自旋和种类数产生了显著限制，其中部分粒子常被视为暗物质候选者。

Conclusion: 引力波天文学对黑洞面积定理的高精度验证，已能对修正引力理论和量子引力模型中的黑洞熵修正施加有意义的观测约束，并可间接限制超出标准模型的新物理，包括暗物质候选粒子的性质。

Abstract: This contribution considers constraints from analyses of gravitational wave data from binary black hole coalescence, on possible corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula for black hole entropy. Most recent analyses of gravitational wave data from the LVK Consortium appear to confirm the Hawking Area Theorem for black holes at a $5 σ$ accuracy, for the `loudest' signal (SNR of the order of $80$) of binary black hole merger inherent in the recent observation GW250114. Amalgamating this result with Bekenstein's ideas of black hole entropy and the generalized second law of thermodynamics, we constrain leading inverse area corrections for large horizon area spherical black hole solutions of classical $F(R)$ gravity, using the Wald entropy function formalism. The implementation of the observational constraints entails the notion of `absolute consistency' which we introduce and contrast with `relative consistency'. This absolute consistency criterion is shown to relate some of the parameters of $F(R)$ gravity. Next we consider leading quantum general relativistic corrections to the Area formula, arising both from the non-perturbative matter-free Loop Quantum Gravity and matter-dependent, perturbative Entanglement entropy approaches. Combining the leading logarithmic corrections in horizon area (for large areas) from both approaches, and imposing absolute consistency with the observational validation of the Area Theorem, is shown to lead to significant restrictions on the spin and number of species of Beyond Standard Model spectrum of elementary particles, some of which are often assumed to be Dark Matter candidates.

</details>


### [144] [Conserved Gravitational Charges as Curvature Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25646)
*Emel Altas,Bayram Tekin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在渐近AdS和渐近平坦时空中，标准守恒引力荷可等价地表示为线性化曲率的通量。对于AdS，该构造再现了Abbott-Deser荷的曲率表示；对于平坦时空，通过引入反对称庞加莱Killing势，得到了统一的曲率-通量公式，其平移部分恢复ADM能量-动量，四维下的洛伦兹部分恢复角动量和质心荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，守恒引力荷（如ADM能量-动量、角动量）表示为度规扰动及其一阶导数的表面积分。作者试图寻找一种等效的曲率通量表示，即仅用线性化曲率（Weyl张量、Ricci张量等）来表达这些荷，从而提供更深层的几何理解，并统一AdS和平坦时空的处理。

Method: 核心工具是一个无散的四阶张量，其迹正比于宇宙学爱因斯坦张量。在最大对称AdS背景下，该构造直接给出Abbott-Deser荷的曲率表示。对于渐近平坦时空，由于平移Killing二形式退化，不能直接取Λ→0极限；作者转而引入反对称庞加莱Killing势，利用与代数Bianchi恒等式适配的Killing势将线性化爱因斯坦流转化为全散度，得到单一曲率-通量公式。在非最大对称爱因斯坦背景上，背景Weyl曲率会产生额外项。

Result: 在渐近AdS情形下，构造成功再现了已知的Abbott-Deser荷曲率表示。渐近平坦情形下，平移部分恢复ADM能量-动量；四维洛伦兹部分在标准Regge-Teitelboim衰减和宇称条件下恢复角动量和质心荷。归一化通过Schwarzschild、boosted Schwarzschild和Kerr数据显式验证。

Conclusion: 标准守恒引力荷可统一地用线性化曲率通量表示。该构造在最大对称背景（AdS和平坦）上成立，平坦情形需通过庞加莱Killing势而非直接取Λ→0极限来实现。然而，在非最大对称爱因斯坦背景上，背景Weyl曲率引入额外贡献，因此纯余二维曲率-通量公式无法直接推广。

Abstract: Conserved gravitational charges are commonly expressed as surface integrals of the metric perturbation and its first derivatives. We show that, in the usual asymptotically AdS and asymptotically flat settings, the standard charges admit equivalent representatives as fluxes of linearized curvature. The construction uses a divergence-free rank-four tensor whose trace is proportional to the cosmological Einstein tensor. On a maximally symmetric AdS background, it reproduces the known curvature representation of the Abbott-Deser charges. The asymptotically flat construction is not obtained by taking a naive $Λ\rightarrow 0$ limit, since the Killing two-form vanishes for translations. We instead introduce an antisymmetric Poincaré Killing potential. A representative Killing potential adapted to the algebraic Bianchi identity converts the linearized Einstein current into a total divergence and yields a single curvature-flux formula. Its translation sector reproduces the ADM energy--momentum, while in four dimensions its Lorentz sector reproduces the angular momentum and boost/center-of-mass charges under the standard Regge-Teitelboim falloff and parity conditions. The normalization is checked explicitly for Schwarzschild, boosted Schwarzschild, and Kerr data. On non-maximally symmetric Einstein backgrounds, background Weyl curvature generates additional terms, so the maximally symmetric construction does not directly extend to a pure codimension-two curvature-flux formula.

</details>


### [145] [Taub-NUT as gravitational dyon with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25654)
*Dmitri Gal'tsov,Rostom Karsanov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Taub-NUT度规的Levi-Civita联络在Misner弦上奇异，产生含delta函数平方的爱因斯坦张量。引入挠率可改善此问题，使爱因斯坦张量被解释为具有负变张力的宇宙弦，挠率生成Komar流并贡献渐近Komar荷，Taub-NUT度规由此呈现为双自旋流体束支撑的孤立子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Taub-NUT度规中Levi-Civita联络在Misner弦上的奇异性问题，以及由此产生的爱因斯坦张量包含delta函数平方的物理上不合理的分布源。

Method: 在引力理论中引入挠率（torsion），修改联络结构，使爱因斯坦张量在Misner弦上的奇异性得到正则化。通过挠率生成Komar流，分析渐近Komar荷的起源，并将整体构型重新解释为自旋流体束支撑的孤立子解。

Result: 引入挠率后，爱因斯坦张量可被解释为具有负变张力的宇宙弦；Misner弦中的Komar流完全由挠率产生，挠率成为渐近Komar荷的物理来源；Taub-NUT度规可视为由两束自旋流体支撑的孤立子。

Conclusion: 挠率是理解Taub-NUT时空物理结构的关键要素，它消除了Misner弦上的分布奇异性，并为渐近荷提供了自然的物质来源，揭示Taub-NUT度规本质上是一个有挠引力理论中的孤立子解。

Abstract: We show that the Levi-Civita connection for the Taub-NUT metric is singular on Misner strings and generates an Einstein tensor containing the squares of the delta function. This can be improved by introducing torsion, in which case the Einstein tensor may be interpreted as that of a cosmic string with negative variable tension. Komar flows in Misner strings are generated by torsion, which is therefore responsible for the asymptotic Komar charges. The Taub-NUT metric then appears as a soliton supported by two spin-fluid beams.

</details>


### [146] [Late-Time Cosmological Tests of a Minisuperspace Generalized-Uncertainty-Principle Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25745)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种二次广义不确定性原理（GUP）对FLRW迷你超空间泊松代数的形变，推导了修正的弗里德曼方程，并利用宇宙计时器、Pantheon+超新星和DESI DR2重子声波振荡数据对形变参数进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应在宇宙学中的可观测印记，尤其是能够通过晚期宇宙加速膨胀的观测数据进行检验的效应。

Method: 对FLRW迷你超空间泊松代数引入二次GUP形变（一阶形变参数），推导修正的弗里德曼方程；由于扰动 sector 不唯一确定，仅分析背景观测量，联合31个宇宙计时器测量、1580个Pantheon+哈勃流样本和DESI DR2 BAO似然（声视界作为冗余参数）进行参数约束。

Result: 在基线归一化下，形变参数 β* 在68%置信度下为 -0.086^{+0.040}_{-0.032}，95%置信度区间为 -0.142 到 0.005。

Conclusion: GUP形变模型在背景膨胀历史层面与平坦ΛCDM及CPL参数化模型可比，观测数据对形变参数给出了与零一致的约束（95%水平），表明当前背景观测量尚不支持显著偏离经典弗里德曼方程的量子引力修正。

Abstract: The investigation of possible quantum-gravity effects in cosmology is of considerable interest, especially when such effects can be tested against observations of the late-time expansion. In the present work we study a quadratic generalized-uncertainty-principle (GUP) deformation of the FLRW minisuperspace Poisson algebra. At first order in the deformation parameter, the construction modifies the Friedmann equation and hence the homogeneous expansion history. Since it does not determine a unique perturbation sector, we restrict the analysis to background observables, using 31 cosmic-chronometer measurements, the uncalibrated 1580-object Pantheon$+$ Hubble-flow sample, and the 13-component DESI DR2 baryon-acoustic-oscillation likelihood, while treating the sound horizon as a nuisance parameter. We compare the resulting cosmology with flat \LCDM{} and with the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder parametrization. For the baseline normalization we obtain $\betastar=-0.086^{+0.040}_{-0.032}$ at $68\%$ credibility and $-0.142<\betastar<0.005$ at $95\%$ credibility.

</details>


### [147] [Analytic backreaction of a scalar wig on a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25932)
*Marco de Cesare,Manuel Del Piano,Carlos A. R. Herdeiro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 $r_0\mu \ll 1$ 的小耦合条件下，解析计算了球对称大质量复标量场拟束缚态对 Schwarzschild 黑洞的领头阶反作用，得到了度量扰动、Misner-Sharp 质量及未来外捕获视界的演化，并验证了黑洞质量增长与标量云质量衰减的平衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越稳态近似，在时域中自洽地描述拟束缚态的指数衰减对黑洞的反作用，填补小耦合区间内解析处理的空白，并明确标量小耦合近似与引力微扰展开同时成立的有效范围。

Method: 采用入射 Eddington-Finkelstein 坐标，将复标量场拟束缚态的能量-动量张量代入 Einstein 方程并展开至振幅二阶；通过匹配渐近展开求解析基态 s 波的小质量拟共振频率，并用 Leaver 连分式法进行数值验证；导出度量扰动和 Misner-Sharp 质量的显式表达式，追踪未来外捕获视界的演化。

Result: 得到了度量扰动和 Misner-Sharp 质量的解析表达式；黑洞质量随视界通量单调增长，当有限标量云被完全吸收后饱和；标量云质量的减少恰好平衡视界质量的增长（在当前微扰阶成立）；给出了标量小耦合近似与引力微扰展开同时有效的参数域。

Conclusion: 该工作在时域框架下自洽地处理了拟束缚态衰减对 Schwarzschild 黑洞的反作用，揭示了质量转移的精确平衡关系，并划定了双近似方案的联合适用边界，为超辐射云、玻色子星塌缩等相关天体物理场景提供了解析基准。

Abstract: We analytically determine the leading backreaction of a spherically symmetric massive complex scalar quasi-bound state (with mass $μ$) on a Schwarzschild black hole with (initial) gravitational radius $r_0$. Working in the small-coupling regime, $r_0 μ\ll 1$, we evaluate the stress-energy tensor of the fundamental scalar $s$-wave and solve the Einstein equations through quadratic order in its amplitude in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates. We also determine the small-mass quasi-resonant frequency of the fundamental $s$-wave analytically by matched asymptotic expansions and validate it numerically using Leaver's method. Unlike steady-state treatments, the calculation retains the exponential decay of the quasi-bound state. We obtain explicit expressions for the metric perturbations and Misner-Sharp mass and derive the evolution of the future outer trapping horizon. The black-hole mass grows monotonically with the decaying horizon flux and saturates when the finite scalar cloud has been absorbed, with the decrease of the cloud mass exactly balancing the horizon growth at the perturbative order considered. We also determine the domain in which the scalar small-coupling approximation and the gravitational perturbative expansion are simultaneously valid.

</details>

### [148] [Does a rising tide lift all boats? A wealth exchange model on a dynamic network with economic growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25874)
*Gustavo L. Kohlrausch,Sebastian Gonçalves*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个动态复杂网络上的基于主体的经济模型，探讨经济增长（漂移项μ）和生产率异质性（波动率σ）如何与社会保护因子（f）共同影响财富不平等。结果表明，只有在强有力的社会保护下，经济增长才能使最贫困主体受益，否则波动性加大会加剧不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21世纪以来财富不平等显著加剧，且各国财富与收入分布呈现普遍的两极分化模式，暗示存在根本性机制驱动这些经济差异。作者旨在通过基于主体的复杂网络模型，揭示经济增长、生产率异质性与社会保护政策之间的相互作用如何塑造财富分配格局。

Method: 采用动态复杂网络上的基于主体模型（Agent-Based Model），经济体系通过三个交替过程演化：（1）每个主体独立的随机财富增长，由漂移项μ（经济增长）和波动率σ（生产率异质性）刻画；（2）连接主体间的财富交换；（3）复杂网络中连接的重新布线。引入社会保护因子f，使每次交易偏向较贫困方，系统分析不同参数组合下的财富分配结果。

Result: （1）较高的社会保护因子f放大了经济增长的效应；（2）增大漂移项μ可降低不平等，而增大波动率σ则加剧不平等；（3）只有当存在强有力的社会保护时，经济增长才会惠及最贫困主体；缺乏社会保护下，经济增长反而可能恶化不平等状况。

Conclusion: 经济增长对财富分配的改善作用并非自动实现，而是以强有力的社会保护机制为前提条件。政策层面，单纯追求经济增长不足以解决财富不平等问题，必须配套完善的社会保障制度才能确保增长成果惠及底层群体。

Abstract: Wealth inequality, although an age-old problem, has seen a substantial rise since the early XXI century. The distributions of wealth and income across countries follow a universal pattern, typically manifesting as a two-class division, which suggests that fundamental mechanisms underpin the emergence of these economic disparities. Agent-based models, which allow the rules of interaction between economic agents to be explicitly defined, are particularly well-suited for studying economic systems and analyzing their emergent properties. In this work, we examine a recently proposed dynamic complex network agent-based model within the context of a growing economy. The model evolves via three alternating processes: independent stochastic wealth growth of each agent, wealth exchanges between connected agents, and the rewiring of connections within the complex network. The wealth growth of each agent is governed by a stochastic process characterized by two parameters: a drift term $μ$, representing economic growth, and volatility $σ$, reflecting heterogeneity in productivity. We analyze the outcomes for various values of a social protection factor $f$, which favors the poorer agent in each transaction. Higher values of $f$ amplify the effect of economic growth: while increasing $μ$ reduces inequality, increasing $σ$ has the opposite effect. In this context, economic growth benefits the poorest agents only when strong social protection is in place.

</details>


### [149] [Cumulative suspicion and absorption dynamics in an agent-based Mafia game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24980)
*Eduardo Velasco Stock,Roberto da Silva*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了黑手党/狼人游戏的一个基于智能体的数学模型，用累积的公开嫌疑分数替代统一随机投票，通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究了人口规模、初始黑手党人数和知情侦探对博弈吸收统计的影响，发现了标度律和有限尺寸交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑手党/狼人博弈模型将白天的淘汰简化为均匀随机投票，忽略了玩家间基于历史的、异质性的嫌疑累积过程。本研究旨在构建一个更贴近真实游戏动态的最小化模型，将微观的指控历史与宏观的吸收统计（黑手党灭绝或平局）联系起来。

Method: 提出基于智能体的模型：白天阶段，随机配对的玩家根据各自角色修改对方公开的嫌疑分数；夜晚则按角色执行攻击或侦查。淘汰阶段，存活玩家以与其累积嫌疑分数成比例的概率被处决。采用大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，并引入随机处决近似给出稀释黑手党条件下的解析预测。通过有限尺寸标度分析，提取特征人口规模N_c并检验数据塌缩。

Result: 在无侦探情况下，稀释黑手党早期累积灭绝概率满足F(τ)~(τ/N)^{N_m}，与模拟一致。胜率曲线存在以N_c为特征的有限尺寸交叉，按N_c重标度可使不同初始黑手党数量的曲线近似塌缩。引入完全知情侦探显著缩短黑手党存活时间，并引入额外的人口组成依赖性，单一参数重标度不再充分。

Conclusion: 该模型在隐藏角色博弈中建立了从微观指控历史到宏观吸收统计的最小连接，揭示了标度行为和有限尺寸效应。侦探的引入使系统动力学更加复杂，需要超越单参数标度的理论框架。

Abstract: The Mafia game, also known as Werewolf, describes a competition between a coordinated minority whose identities are concealed and a larger good faction composed primarily of uninformed civilians attempting to identify and eliminate them. We introduce an agent-based formulation in which the daytime decision is not represented by uniform random voting. Instead, randomly paired agents modify player-specific public suspicion scores according to their roles, and one surviving player is subsequently eliminated with probability proportional to their accumulated suspicion. These scores persist throughout the game, producing a history-dependent stochastic process with two competing absorbing outcomes: elimination of the mafia or numerical parity between the mafia and the good faction. We investigate the effects of population size, initial mafia size, and the presence of perfectly informed detectives through extensive Monte Carlo simulations. In the absence of detectives, a random-execution approximation predicts that, in the dilute-mafia and early-time regime, the cumulative probability of mafia extinction behaves as $F(τ)\sim (τ/N)^{N_m}$, in agreement with simulations as $N_m/N$ decreases. The winning-probability curves also exhibit an empirical finite-size crossover characterized by a population scale $N_c$. Rescaling the population by $N_c$ approximately collapses the curves obtained for different initial mafia populations. Perfectly informed detectives substantially shorten mafia-survival times and introduce an additional dependence on population composition for which the same one-parameter rescaling is insufficient. The model provides a minimal connection between microscopic histories of accusation and the macroscopic absorption statistics of hidden-role games.

</details>


### [150] [Open-ended innovation in zero-sum games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25677)
*Matteo Marsili*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了具有开放式创新的零和博弈，其中玩家可不断引入新策略。作者论证了在一般条件下，这种设定会导致永无止境的创新军备竞赛，因为一方引入新技术会提高对手进行技术创新的边际效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统博弈论通常假设策略空间是固定且已知的，但现实中许多竞争场景（如技术竞赛、军事对抗）中，参与者会不断发明新策略。本文旨在建模这种开放式创新过程，并解释为何创新竞赛可能永无休止。

Method: 将创新过程建模为从某个分布中抽取新策略的随机过程；在零和博弈框架下，分析一方引入新策略对对手边际创新效用的影响，并论证在一般（generic）条件下该动态的长期行为。

Result: 在一般条件下，开放式创新的零和博弈会导致持续的创新军备竞赛：玩家A引入新技术会提高玩家B进行技术创新的边际效用，从而促使B也进行创新，形成正反馈循环。

Conclusion: 开放式创新零和博弈具有内在的不稳定性，创新过程会自我强化，导致永无止境的军备竞赛。这为理解技术竞争、军备竞赛等现实现象提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: This note discusses zero-sum games with open-ended innovation, whereby each player may introduce new strategies. The innovation process is modelled as a draw of new strategies form a distribution. It is argued that, under generic conditions, this setting can lead to an everlasting innovation arm race, because the introduction of new technologies of one player increases the marginal utility for technological innovation of the opponent.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [A Spectral Proof of the Hypergraph Moore Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26028)
*Alexander Schmidhuber,Matthew B. Hastings*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Feige于2008年提出的关于超图Moore界的猜想：对于任意k≥3的k-均匀超图，若边数超过某个阈值，则必定存在一个大小受控的偶覆盖（even cover）。证明基于Kikuchi矩阵的锐谱界技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶覆盖（even cover）是图论中围长（girth）概念在超图上的自然推广——每个顶点出现在偶数条超边中。Feige在2008年提出了超图Moore界猜想，将经典的Moore界（关于图的最小环长与边数/顶点数的关系）推广到超图情形。证明这一猜想对于理解超图的组合结构具有重要意义。

Method: 核心方法基于Kikuchi矩阵的锐谱界（sharp spectral bounds）技术。作者通过分析Kikuchi矩阵的谱性质，建立了超图边数与最小偶覆盖大小之间的定量关系，从而推导出Feige猜想的界：当超边数超过C·n^(k/2)/ℓ^(k/2-1)时，必然存在大小不超过A·ℓ·log(en/ℓ)的偶覆盖。

Result: 证明了存在绝对常数A和C（与k无关），使得对任意k≥3和1≤ℓ≤n，任何n个顶点上的k-均匀超图若拥有多于C·n^(k/2)/ℓ^(k/2-1)条超边，则必包含一个大小至多为A·ℓ·log(en/ℓ)的偶覆盖。这一结果完全证实了Feige 2008年的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了Feige提出的超图Moore界猜想，其开发的Kikuchi矩阵谱界技术也具有独立的研究价值。作者在另一篇伴随论文中将这些技术应用于随机约束满足问题的反驳（refutation），展示了该方法在理论计算机科学中的广泛应用前景。

Abstract: A nonempty subfamily of a $k$-uniform hypergraph is an \emph{even cover} if every vertex lies in an even number of its hyperedges; for $k=2$ these are edge-disjoint unions of cycles, so the minimum size of an even cover is the natural hypergraph analogue of girth. We prove Feige's 2008 conjecture on the hypergraph Moore bound: there are absolute constants $A$ and $C$ (independent of $k$) such that for every $k\ge3$ and every $1\le\ell\le n$, any $k$-uniform hypergraph on $n$ vertices with more than $C\,n^{k/2}/\ell^{k/2-1}$ hyperedges contains an even cover of size at most $A\,\ell\log(en/\ell)$. Our proof is based on sharp spectral bounds for Kikuchi matrices, which we expect to be of independent interest; we apply them to the refutation of random constraint satisfaction problems in a companion paper.

</details>

### [152] [The geometry-first formulation of gauge theory is not equivalent to the symmetry-first one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24901)
*Henrique Gomes*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了几何优先与对称优先两种规范理论表述并不等价，从三个方面揭示其差异：有限张量数据下几何优先所容纳的理论更少；即使对称优先理论存在几何优先表述，主丛与物质丛也无法唯一确定生成规范群的结构向量丛；从几何优先生成对象到带物质主丛的函子忠实但非本质满且非满。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示几何优先表述（规范群作为结构化基本向量丛的自同构群产生）与对称优先表述在物理理论构建中的深层差异，澄清二者在何种意义上不等价，尤其关注有限张量生成数据所施加的限制。

Method: 采用范畴论与微分几何工具，构造从几何优先生成对象到主丛带物质结构的函子，通过分析其忠实性、本质满性与满性，结合具体反例（如加法群 ℝ 的荷理论、标准模型对角商群、实定向纤维 ℝ^{2m} 的外自同构）系统论证三种差异。

Result: （1）加法结构群 ℝ 的带电理论在有限张量菜单中无几何优先表述；（2）主丛与物质丛不足以反向确定生成规范群的结构向量丛；（3）所述函子忠实但非本质满（标准模型对角商群及 ℝ 加群在所有有限张量菜单上均失败），且非满（SO(2m) 由非正常正交映射诱导的外自同构无法由保持结构的纤维映射实现）。

Conclusion: 几何优先与对称优先的规范理论表述存在三类不可忽视的差异，二者并不等价。这一结论对规范理论的数学基础以及物理上选择理论构建路径具有重要启示，暗示几何优先路径在有限生成条件下的理论空间更为受限。

Abstract: This paper argues that the geometry-first and symmetry-first formulations of gauge theory are not equivalent. They differ in three respects. First, the geometry-first formulation---in which gauge groups arise as automorphism groups of structured fundamental vector bundles---admits fewer theories when its generating structures are restricted to finite tensorial data. Charged theories with additive structure group $\mathbb{R}$ have no such presentation. Second, even when a symmetry-first theory has a geometry-first presentation, its principal bundle and matter bundles do not determine which structured vector bundles generated the gauge group. Third, the natural functor from geometry-first generating objects to principal bundles with matter is faithful but neither essentially surjective nor full. Essential surjectivity fails for the diagonal quotient of the Standard Model gauge group on what I call `the classical menu', and for the additive-$\mathbb{R}$ examples on every finite tensorial menu. Fullness fails for a real oriented fibre $\mathbb{R}^{2m}$: the principal-bundle category admits the outer automorphism of $SO(2m)$ induced by an improper orthogonal map, but no structure-preserving fibre map induces it.

</details>
