### [153] [Low-Temperature Magnetoresistance Hysteresis in Granular Cr1-xFexO2: Slow Relaxation of Transport-Active Magnetic Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25263)
*E. Yu. Beliayev,V. A. Horielyi,I. G. Mirzoiev,V. V. Andrievskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 重新分析了压制CrO₂粉末在低温下的磁电阻行为，发现当输运由少数自旋依赖的晶粒间隧穿路径主导时，磁电阻回线出现非单调特征。在含Fe样品中观察到电阻先降后升再降的异常磁滞，该特征随磁场扫描速率降低而减弱，对应约3秒的磁输运弛豫时间，归因于晶粒间接触处局域磁矩和畴结构的缓慢演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释掺杂CrO₂粉末中观测到的非单调磁电阻回线现象，特别是超越常规低场磁滞的额外分支交叉行为，揭示其背后的物理机制。

Method: 重新分析未掺杂CrO₂和含Fe的Cr₁₋ₓFeₓO₂压制粉末的低温磁电阻数据；对不同磁场扫描速率下的回线进行数字化处理，提取电阻恢复场与扫描速率的线性关系，估算有效磁输运弛豫时间；将观测到的非单调特征分解为常规负隧穿磁电阻与缓慢电阻上升贡献的叠加。

Result: 含Fe样品电阻随磁场先降后升再降；该非单调磁滞特征随扫场减慢显著减弱；电阻恢复场与扫速近似线性，对应有效弛豫时间约3秒；Fe增加矫顽力和界面磁不均匀性，同时降低整体负磁电阻。

Conclusion: 观测结果支持一种输运加权的磁重构机制：传统负隧穿磁电阻叠加了与瞬态局域磁无序相关的缓慢电阻上升贡献。约3秒的弛豫时间源于晶粒间接触处局域磁矩和畴构型的缓慢演化，而非微观自旋翻转。

Abstract: Low-temperature magnetoresistance loops of compacted CrO2 powders become strongly nonmonotonic when electrical transport is dominated by a small number of spin-dependent intergranular tunnelling paths. We reanalyse previously reported data for undoped CrO2 and Fe-containing Cr1-xFexO2, focusing on additional branch crossings beyond the conventional low-field hysteresis. In the Fe-containing specimen, the resistance decreases, passes through a minimum, rises over an extended field interval, and then decreases again at higher field. This nonmonotonic hysteretic feature is strongly reduced when the magnetic-field sweep is slowed. Digitization of the three fastest sweeps shows that the field at which the resistance starts to recover shifts approximately linearly with sweep rate, corresponding to an effective magnetotransport relaxation time of about 3 s. This scale is attributed not to microscopic spin flips but to the slow evolution of local moment and domain configurations at the intergranular contacts dominating the current. The observations are consistent with conventional negative tunnelling magnetoresistance superimposed on a slower resistance-increasing contribution associated with transient local magnetic disorder. Fe increases coercivity and interfacial magnetic heterogeneity while reducing the overall negative magnetoresistance. The results support a transport-weighted magnetic-reconfiguration mechanism for the unusual hysteresis shape.

</details>


### [154] [Relaxation of quenched structural glasses: descent in a stiffening caging potential over inflection-point 'speed bumps'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25360)
*Ting Qu,Deng Pan,Yuliang Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了淬火玻璃中缓慢能量弛豫的幂律衰减源于一种简单的笼效应——每个粒子感受到由多体约束产生的有效硬化势，该势在特征笼尺寸处发散，解析地给出幂律行为。通过固定邻居的最小单粒子笼模型可定量复现该行为，表明集体弛豫模式并非必要。研究还发现不存在区分不同动力学机制的特征温度，状态跟随在所有初始温度下普遍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火玻璃中缓慢能量弛豫的幂律衰减现象虽广泛存在，但其微观起源仍存在激烈争议——现有假说包括鞍点减速、边缘稳定性、局域激发粗化以及声子动力学等，均无法达成共识。作者旨在通过系统性模拟来甄别甚至排除这些假说，从而建立统一、正确的微观物理图像。

Method: 作者采用梯度下降动力学模拟典型的结构玻璃形成体系，系统追踪能量弛豫过程。在此基础上，构建了一个固定邻居的"最小单粒子笼模型"（minimal single-particle cage model），将多体约束简化为每个粒子所感受到的有效硬化势，该势在特征笼尺寸处发散，从而解析导出幂律衰减。同时分析了能量景观上的拐点（inflection points）对动力学的瞬态扰动效应，并与平均场自旋玻璃理论进行对比。

Result: （1）已有的鞍点减速、边缘稳定性、局域激发粗化、声子动力学等假说均无法解释作者的模拟数据；（2）幂律衰减来自简单的笼效应：各粒子感受到由多体约束产生的有效硬化势，该势在特征笼尺寸处发散，解析地给出幂律行为，且被最小单粒子笼模型定量复现；（3）动力学中拐点作为"减速带"引起涨落，但不影响整体幂律行为；（4）与平均场自旋玻璃理论不同，不存在区分不同动力学机制的特征温度，只要系统足够接近固有结构，状态跟随在所有初始温度下均普遍成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为典型结构玻璃中的梯度下降动力学建立了一个完整的物理图像：幂律能量弛豫的本质是单粒子尺度的笼效应，集体弛豫模式并非必需；动力学机制在温度上具有普适性，不存在特征温度分隔不同动力学区域。这一发现统一并澄清了该领域长期存在的争议。

Abstract: The slow energy relaxation in quenched glasses is a ubiquitous yet poorly understood phenomenon. Despite extensive study, the microscopic origin of the observed power-law decay remains debated, with proposed mechanisms ranging from saddle-point slowdown and marginal stability to coarsening of localized excitations and phonon dynamics. Here, by simulating gradient descent in archetypal structural glass formers, we show that none of these scenarios can account for our data. Instead, the power-law behavior emerges from a remarkably simple caging effect: each particle experiences an effective stiffening potential that arises from many-body confinement and diverges at a characteristic cage size. This mechanism analytically yields the observed power-law decay and is quantitatively reproduced by a minimal single-particle cage model with fixed neighbours, demonstrating that collective relaxation modes are not essential. The dynamics is punctuated by fluctuations as the system rolls through inflection points on the energy landscape, which act as `speed bumps' but do not affect the overall power-law behaviour. In contrast to mean-field spin glass theory, we find no characteristic temperature that separates distinct dynamical regimes; state following within a given glass basin occurs universally for all initial temperatures whenever the system is sufficiently close to the inherent structure. Our results establish a complete physical picture of gradient descent dynamics in typical structural glasses.

</details>

### [155] [Organizing Principles for Moiré Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24944)
*Qiaoling Xu,Yifan Gao,Tao Zhang,Ammon Fischer,Yi Jiang,Hanqi Pi,Zike Fan,Dongdong An,Kun Zhou,Yingjian Li,Yongqing Li,Yuhao Fu,Lei Wang,Lijun Zhang,B. Andrei Bernevig,Dante M. Kennes,Enge Wang,Angel Rubio,Lede Xian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个超越传统Γ、K、M谷机制的完整moiré平带分类框架，通过对600多种共格扭转双层结构的第一性原理计算和对称性分析，揭示了多种实现关联拓扑量子物态的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，范德华双层中的moiré平带仅通过六角晶体的Γ谷、K谷和M谷机制来理解，但这种观点是不完整的。需要建立一个更通用的框架，将单层能带边缘的动量空间位置、有效局域轨道特征、moiré对称性及能带对称性表示作为组织变量，系统揭示涌现低能moiré哈密顿量的全貌。

Method: 作者对覆盖所有二维晶格类型的600多种共格扭转双层进行了完全弛豫的第一性原理计算、能带展开（band unfolding）和对称性表示分析，从中系统分类moiré平带的涌现机制。

Result: 发现了多条超越传统单轨道范式的新路径：(1) 实现了三角、蜂窝、正方、棋盘和kagome型Hubbard模型，涉及单轨道、多轨道和多格点Hilbert空间；(2) 自旋-轨道耦合的多轨道平带展现出超越传统K谷情形的对称性指示拓扑；(3) 非简单空间moiré对称性强制半金属平带连接；(4) M谷六角系统和X谷正方/矩形系统中涌现准一维平带，源于动量空间非简单空间对称性；(5) 在非高对称点能带边缘的系统中识别出kagome型连通性的耦合多谷流形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个“谷-轨道-对称性”框架，将母体材料电子结构与涌现moiré哈密顿量联系起来，为关联、拓扑和对称性强制的moiré物相研究提供了系统性的指导原则。

Abstract: Moiré flat bands in van der Waals bilayers are usually discussed through a small set of mechanisms associated with the $Γ$ and $K$ valleys of hexagonal crystals, and more recently with $M$-valleys systems. Here we show that this view is incomplete. The momentum-space location and effective local orbital character of the monolayer's band edge, in conjunction with the moiré symmetry and the symmetry representations of the resulting bands, provide a general set of organizing variables for the emergent low-energy moiré Hamiltonian. Applying fully relaxed first-principles calculations, band unfolding and symmetry-representation analysis to more than 600 commensurate twisted bilayers spanning all 2D lattice classes, we identify several routes to moiré quantum matter beyond the conventional single-orbital paradigm. The resulting flat bands realize trigonal, honeycomb, square, checkerboard and kagome-like Hubbard models with single-orbital, multi-orbital and multi-site Hilbert spaces; spin-orbit-coupled multi-orbital flat bands exhibit symmetry-indicated topology beyond the conventional $K$-valley setting; and nonsymmorphic moiré symmetries enforce semimetallic flat-band connectivity. Analogous quasi-one-dimensional flat-band structures are found in $M$-valley hexagonal systems and $X$-valley square or rectangular systems resulting from emergent momentum-space nonsymmorphic symmetries. Separately, coupled multi-valley manifolds with kagome-like connectivity are identified in several systems whose parent band edges lie at non-high-symmetry points. These results establish a valley-orbital-symmetry framework for connecting parent-material electronic structure to emergent moiré Hamiltonians relevant to correlated, topological and symmetry-enforced moiré phases.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Wald-like entropy and Islands in Dimensionally Reduced Einstein-Hilbert Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24883)
*Krishna Jalan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在维度约化爱因斯坦-希尔伯特（DREH）模型框架下，采用洛伦兹方法计算广义熵，无需借助副本技巧，通过Wald-like Noether荷确定量子极端曲面，从而推导渐近平坦永恒黑洞与蒸发黑洞的岛公式和Page曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究通过欧几里得引力路径积分的副本方法推导了DREH模型中霍金辐射的精细熵。本文旨在补充这一欧几里得方法，提供一个无需副本技巧的洛伦兹框架来计算广义熵，从而以更直接的方式验证和拓展岛公式与Page曲线的结果。

Method: 在DREH模型（四维爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量经s波近似约化为二维伸缩子引力，并引入大中心荷c的Polyakov-Liouville作用量以纳入量子修正）中，作者将广义熵S_gen等同于DREH-PL联合作用量的Wald-like Noether荷，并在洛伦兹框架下直接计算，无需依赖欧几里得副本方法。随后利用该广义熵确定量子极端曲面、Page时间及Page曲线。

Result: 广义熵被证明等价于DREH-PL联合作用量的Wald-like Noether荷。基于此，作者成功确定了永恒黑洞与蒸发黑洞的量子极端曲面、Page时间及Page曲线，结果与先前欧几里得副本方法一致。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹框架下基于Noether荷的广义熵计算方法为岛公式提供了独立且自洽的验证，无需引入外部热浴（辐射存在于黑洞时空自身内部）。文章最后讨论了将该方法推广至包含真空极化修正的完整四维几何的可能性。

Abstract: We study the island formula and Page curve for the asymptotically flat eternal and quasi-stationary evaporating black hole solutions within the dimensionally reduced Einstein-Hilbert (DREH) model. In this model, the four-dimensional Einstein-Hilbert action reduces to a two-dimensional dilaton gravity, on top of which the quantum corrections can be incorporated via the Polyakov-Liouville (PL) action for a two-dimensional conformal field theory with a large central charge $c$. This gravity model arises from the s-wave approximation of four-dimensional Einstein-Hilbert gravity and provides a fully gravitational setting in which we study the islands, i.e., we will not require a non-gravitational bath region to collect the radiation, instead it lives in the black hole spacetime itself. The fine-grained entropy of Hawking radiation in the eternal and evaporating black holes within the DREH model was derived using the island rule in \cite{djordjevic2022eternal, djordevic2025evaporating}. The island rule is based on the replica method using the Euclidean gravitational path integral. In this work, we complement the Euclidean approach by providing a Lorentzian prescription for the generalized entropy $S_{\text{gen}}$, in the island formula without invoking the replica trick to compute $S_{\text{gen}}$. The generalized entropy is shown to coincide with Wald-like Noether charge of the combined DREH-PL action. Using this generalized entropy, we determine the quantum extremal surface, the Page time, and the Page curve for the eternal and the evaporating black hole. We conclude with a discussion of the possible extension to the full four-dimensional geometry incorporating vacuum polarization corrections.

</details>


### [157] [OPE = QNM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24909)
*Paolo Arnaudo,Cristoforo Iossa,Robin Karlsson,Benjamin Withers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了大N CFT热态的双重复表示——热算子积展开(OPE)与准正则模(QNM)——在复时间平面上存在收敛重叠区域，从而建立了OPE与QNM数据间的映射关系，并提出了一种新的热bootstrap方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观尺度下热关联函数由OPE描述，宏观尺度下由QNM描述，但两者之间的关系尚不明确。作者旨在建立这两种表示之间的桥梁，利用彼此的数据相互约束，开辟热bootstrap的新方向。

Method: 在混合时间-空间动量坐标下，分析延迟关联函数的OPE和QNM表示在复时间平面上的收敛域；利用 overtone QNM渐近行为与OPE奇点的关系，以及低overtone QNM在短时到长时解析延拓中的表现；引入Mellin空间中的求和规则；结合光锥OPE分析大空间动量下应力张量关联函数的热化行为。

Result: 1. 证明OPE与QNM表示在复时间平面上存在收敛重叠区域；2. 大overtone QNM渐近与OPE奇点直接相关；3. 从OPE数据数值获得了Schwarzschild-AdS₅黑膜的 low-overtone QNM；4. 通过Mellin空间求和规则揭示了OPE谱与QNM数据的内在联系；5. 论证当共形对撞机界接近饱和时，大空间动量下的应力张量关联函数热化更慢。

Conclusion: OPE与QNM数据可以相互约束，这为发展热bootstrap程序奠定了基础，使得仅从OPE数据即可提取QNM信息，反之亦然。

Abstract: At microscopic scales the linear response of thermal states of large-$N$ CFTs is governed by a thermal operator product expansion (OPE), while at large scales response is governed by collective excitations known as quasinormal modes (QNM). We show that the OPE and QNM representations of the retarded correlator in mixed time and spatial momentum coordinates have an overlapping region of convergence in the complex time plane, giving a map between OPE and QNM data. We show that large-overtone QNM asymptotics are related to OPE singularities, while low-overtone QNM data appear in analytic continuation from short to large times. Using this approach we obtain new analytic results for QNM asymptotics, and numerically obtain low-overtone QNMs from OPE data for the Schwarzschild-AdS$_5$ black brane. We further show that the OPE spectrum is intimately related to QNM data through a set of sum rules which we derive in Mellin space. Finally, using the lightcone OPE, we argue that stress tensor correlators at large spatial momentum thermalise slower as the conformal collider bounds approach saturation. This work points to a new thermal bootstrap programme where OPE and QNM data constrain each other.

</details>


### [158] [Neural Spectral Bias and Conformal Correlators II: Modular and Annulus Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24913)
*Kausik Ghosh,Sidhaarth Kumar,Vasilis Niarchos,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经网络bootstrap框架，利用模不变性和Cardy条件重构二维共形场论的配分函数。将环面模S-不变性映射为四点交叉对称性，将环管开/闭道对偶表述为界面CFT中缺陷算符混合四点函数的交叉对称性。采用锚定bootstrap形式，用轻量前馈神经网络在惰性训练机制下参数化关联函数，利用谱偏差选择特定交叉对称构型，从稀疏数据中高精度重构完整配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维CFT的配分函数重构是核心问题，传统方法依赖模不变性和Cardy条件，但对稀疏谱数据（如仅知能隙和少量锚定点）的重构能力有限。作者希望将环面和环管约束统一到四点交叉对称框架下，利用神经网络在欠定问题中的谱偏差特性，自动选择物理合理的解，从而以更统一、高效的方式从极少量输入数据重建完整配分函数。

Method: 1. 理论映射：环面配分函数通过对称积描述中的扭曲场表示，将模S-不变性映射为对角运动学下的四点交叉方程；环管配分函数通过界面CFT中缺陷改变算符的混合四点函数，将开/闭道对偶表述为交叉对称性。2. 锚定bootstrap框架：将重构问题表述为欠定的锚定bootstrap形式，约束条件为交叉方程，输入为最小谱数据（能隙）和锚定数据。3. 神经网络参数化：采用轻量前馈神经网络参数化关联函数及对应配分函数，在惰性训练（lazy training）机制下利用谱偏差（spectral bias）自动选择特定的交叉对称构型。

Result: 该方法成功将二维标准模约束和环管约束统一到锚定神经bootstrap方法框架中。神经网络在惰性训练下的谱偏差有效选择了满足交叉对称性的物理构型，能够从极稀疏的输入数据（仅能隙和少量锚定点）中以显著精度重构完整的环面和环管配分函数。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的框架，将二维CFT中的模不变性和环管对偶重新表述为四点交叉对称性问题，并通过神经网络bootstrap求解。这一方法揭示了神经网络谱偏差在约束选择物理解中的关键作用，为从稀疏数据重构完整配分函数开辟了新途径，有望推广到更高维或其他类型CFT的bootstrap研究中。

Abstract: We develop a neural network bootstrap framework for reconstructing partition functions of two-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs) based on modular invariance and the Cardy condition, which are recast as crossing equations for four-point correlators. For torus partition functions, we use the twist-field representation in the symmetric-orbifold description to map modular S-invariance to four-point crossing and focus on the diagonal kinematics of four insertions on a line. For annulus partition functions, we formulate open/closed channel duality as crossing symmetry for mixed four-point functions of defect-changing operators in interface CFT. In both cases, the reconstruction problem is formulated in the anchored-bootstrap form, where the crossing constraints are supplemented by minimal spectral input (a gap) and anchor data. We solve this under-determined problem by using lightweight feed-forward neural networks to parametrise the correlators and their corresponding partition functions. A key ingredient of this approach is the spectral bias of the neural networks in the lazy training regime, which selects specific crossing-symmetric configurations. This reformulation unifies standard modular and annulus constraints in two dimensions with the anchored neural approach for CFT correlators, providing a new way to reconstruct full partition functions from sparse data with remarkable accuracy.

</details>


### [159] [Kaluza-Klein Perturbation Theory from Exceptional Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24914)
*Camille Eloy,Olaf Hohm,Camilla Lavino,Henning Samtleben,Yehudi Simon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了十维和十一维超引力在广泛Kaluza-Klein背景上的微扰理论，利用E6(6)例外场论和广义Scherk-Schwarz ansatz，推导了线性化广义微分同胚下规范不变的一阶场方程，并通过同伦传递方法分离物理模式与纯规范模式，最后以Kerr-Newman AdS5黑洞背景为例分析了IIB型超引力的KK谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KK超引力微扰理论在处理包含高阶形式规范场的非平凡背景（如AdS×球面以外的黑洞几何）时存在局限，需要一套系统的方法来推导规范不变的涨落方程，并实现物理自由度与纯规范自由度的清晰分离。

Method: 利用E6(6)例外场论框架，通过广义Scherk-Schwarz ansatz刻画背景，推导线性化广义微分同胚下规范不变的一阶涨落场方程；详细阐述了所有场（包括自旋2场）的Higgs机制；采用基于同伦传递（homotopy transfer）的方法，在微扰论所有阶上将场分离为规范不变的物理模式和纯规范的非物理模式。

Result: （1）得到了广泛KK背景上规范不变的线性化涨落方程；（2）完整给出了高阶形式规范场为零的背景下所有场的Higgs机制细节；（3）以Kerr-Newman AdS5黑洞（近视界极限为AdS2与压扁三维球的纤维积）为例，分析了IIB型超引力十维KK模式的部分谱。

Conclusion: 所发展的基于例外场论和同伦传递的微扰方法，为研究一般KK背景（包括非平凡规范场背景）上的超引力涨落谱提供了强有力的系统框架，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We develop the perturbation theory of ten and eleven-dimensional supergravities on a large class of Kaluza-Klein backgrounds including familiar AdS examples such as AdS$_5\times S^5$, but also more general manifolds such as black hole geometries. Employing ${\rm E}_{6(6)}$ exceptional field theory with the backgrounds characterized by a generalized Scherk-Schwarz ansatz, we determine the first order field equations for the fluctuations that are gauge invariant under linearized generalized diffeomorphisms. We then present the details of the Higgs mechanism for all fields including spin-2 for the subset of backgrounds in which higher-form gauge fields vanish. We use a recently established machinery based on homotopy transfer that allows one to separate the fields into gauge invariant physical modes and pure gauge unphysical modes to all orders in perturbation theory. Finally, as a first application for backgrounds with higher-form gauge fields switched on, we analyze part of the spectrum of ten-dimensional Kaluza-Klein modes of type IIB supergravity around a Kerr-Newman AdS$_5$ black hole that in the near horizon limit becomes a fibered product of AdS$_2$ and a squashed three-sphere.

</details>


### [160] [The Tachyon Chern-Simons action with a generic tachyon field, and baryons in V-QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24915)
*Jean-Loup Raymond,Matti Järvinen,Elias Kiritsis,Francesco Nitti,Edwan Préau*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 holographic V-QCD 模型中，利用超联络形式，显式构造了一般情况下的 Tachyon-Chern-Simons 项，涵盖不同夸克质量的情形，并据此分析了重子解的结构，证明了重子数等于拓扑瞬子数，同时验证了有效四维 π 介子作用量可重现包括 Skyrme 项和 Wess-Zumino-Witten 项在内的手征拉格朗日量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局味反常一致性要求在全息 QCD 模型中引入 Chern-Simons 项，而此前对 Tachyon-Chern-Simons 项的构造在 tachyon 为一般复矩阵（对应不同夸克质量）的情形下尚未完善，亟需推广以处理更实际的 QCD 背景。

Method: 采用超联络（superconnection）形式，在 holographic V-QCD 框架下显式构造一般复矩阵 tachyon 的 Tachyon-Chern-Simons 项；利用所得结果分析非零夸克质量下的重子解结构，推导重子数流与总重子数表达式；约化到四维以得到 π 介子有效作用量。

Result: 成功显式构造了一般情形的 Tachyon-Chern-Simons 项；证明重子数恒等于重子解的拓扑瞬子数；导出的四维有效作用量精确再现了包含 Skyrme 项和 Wess-Zumino-Witten 项的手征拉格朗日量。

Conclusion: 该工作完整推广了 Tachyon-Chern-Simons 项至一般味矩阵情形，为 holographic V-QCD 模型中处理任意夸克质量配比的重子物理和低能手征有效理论提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Consistency with global flavor anomalies requires the presence of Chern-Simons terms in holographic models of QCD. Such terms are analyzed in a setup arising in the holographic V-QCD model, where chiral symmetry breaking is implemented through the condensation of a complex scalar field, the tachyon. Using the superconnection formalism, the Tachyon-Chern-Simons terms are constructed explicitly in the general case, where the tachyon is any complex matrix in flavor space. This general case covers, among other things, backgrounds where different quark flavors have different masses. These new results are used to analyze the structure of the baryon solutions in the presence of nonzero quark masses. Expressions for the baryon number current and the total baryon number are found, and the baryon number is shown to be equal to the topological instanton number of the baryon solution. The effective four-dimensional pion action is analyzed and is shown to reproduce the chiral Lagrangian, including the Skyrme and Wess-Zumino-Witten terms.

</details>


### [161] [Carrollian bosonic supergravity at order $α'$ and the universal cancellation of higher-curvature divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24916)
*Eric Lescano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了包含四导数α'修正项后，玻色子超引力的Carroll极限仍然有限，并明确构造了有效作用量。同时建立了一个普适判据，用于判定由黎曼张量幂次构成的高阶曲率贡献的有限性，并应用于证明纯引力α'²和α'³修正具有有限Carroll极限，且ζ(3)项在α'³阶不对Carroll玻色子超引力产生贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Carroll极限（光速趋于零的极限）下弦论α'修正的有限性，以检验Carroll引力的自洽性及其与弦论框架的兼容性。此前尚不清楚高阶导数修正是否会在Carroll极限下发散。

Method: 对玻色子超引力作用量进行Carroll极限分析，纳入源于α'修正的四导数项；建立基于黎曼张量幂次Riem₁···Riem_N（N>1）的普适有限性判据；利用该判据逐阶分析纯引力α'²和α'³修正的Carroll极限行为。

Result: 1）含四导数α'修正的玻色子超引力Carroll极限有限，有效作用量已显式构造；2）提出通用判据并证明纯引力α'²和α'³修正均具有有限Carroll极限，其作用量贡献已显式导出；3）证明与ζ(3)成正比的项在α'³阶不对Carroll玻色子超引力产生贡献。

Conclusion: Carroll极限在弦论α'修正框架下具有良好行为，直至α'³阶均保持有限，且某些特定项（如ζ(3)项）自然退耦，表明Carroll引力可作为研究弦论非相对论极限的有效理论框架。

Abstract: We prove that the Carrollian limit of bosonic supergravity remains finite after including the four-derivative terms arising from the $α'$-corrections, and we explicitly construct the effective action. We also establish a universal criterion to determine the finiteness of higher-curvature contributions given by powers of the Riemann tensor, ${\rm Riem}_1 \cdots {\rm Riem}_N$, with $N>1$. As applications of this criterion, we prove that the purely gravitational $α'^2$- and $α'^3$-corrections admit a finite Carrollian limit, derive their explicit contributions to the action, and show that the terms proportional to $ζ(3)$ do not contribute to the Carrollian bosonic supergravity at order $α'^3$.

</details>


### [162] [Analytic thermal bootstrap in momentum space: From thermal OPE to QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24919)
*Julien Barrat,Deniz N. Bozkurt,Enrico Marchetto,Alessio Miscioscia,Elli Pomoni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个bootstrap程序，将热算子乘积展开（OPE）中的紫外数据与红外可观测量（低频行为和拟正规模式）联系起来。通过构造热OPE块的热KMS对称完备化形式（类比于Polyakov块），作者得到推迟热关联器的渐近展开，推导出反演公式，将OPE系数与拟正规模式频率关联，产生非平凡求和规则、拟正规谱约束及其在大空间动量下的渐近行为，并在多种理论中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立有限温度下量子场论中紫外线（热OPE数据）与红外物理（推迟关联器的低频行为及拟正规模式）之间的定量联系。此前这一方向缺乏系统的bootstrap方法，尤其是如何在任意空间动量下将热OPE数据与关联器的复频率平面解析结构联系起来。

Method: 1. 从单个热OPE块出发，构造其KMS对称完备化形式（称为“热Polyakov块”）；2. 对完备化的块做傅里叶变换，得到任意空间动量下推迟热关联器的渐近展开；3. 基于该展开推导反演公式，在亚纯性假设下，将OPE系数表达为拟正规模式频率的函数；4. 在自由场论、二维共形场论、O(N)模型的大N极限与ε展开、强耦合N=4 SYM的R-流关联器等具体模型中验证和应用。

Result: 1. 得到了推迟热关联器在任意空间动量下的渐近展开；2. 推导出联系热OPE系数与拟正规模式频率的反演公式及求和规则；3. 给出了大空间动量下拟正规谱的渐近行为约束；4. 推导了重算子热OPE系数的普适渐近公式，明确其自旋依赖性，拓展了先前仅在零空间间隔的结果；5. 在三维Ising CFT中用Monte Carlo数据验证了截断关联器与理论预测的良好吻合。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了一个系统的热bootstrap框架，将紫外热OPE数据与红外观测量（拟正规模式）定量关联。该框架不仅提供了对热关联器解析结构的新约束（求和规则、拟正规谱渐近行为），还在多种具体模型中得到了验证，为有限温度共形场论和热关联函数的研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We initiate a bootstrap program that relates ultraviolet data, encoded in the thermal OPE, to infrared observables, namely, the low-frequency behavior and quasinormal modes. Starting from KMS-symmetric completions of individual thermal OPE blocks, which play the role of thermal Polyakov blocks, we construct their Fourier transform, yielding an asymptotic expansion of retarded thermal correlators valid at any spatial momentum. We use these results to derive inversion formulae and connect thermal OPE data to the analytic structure of retarded correlators in the complex frequency plane. Under the assumption of meromorphicity, the inversion formulae express OPE coefficients in terms of the quasinormal-mode frequencies, leading to nontrivial sum rules, constraints on the quasinormal spectrum, and its asymptotics at large spatial momentum. We illustrate these results in free theories, two-dimensional CFTs, the large-$N$ limit and $\varepsilon$-expansion of the $\mathrm{O}(N)$ model, and the $R$-current correlator of strongly coupled $\mathcal N = 4$ SYM at zero spatial momentum. As a byproduct, we derive universal asymptotic formulae for thermal OPE coefficients of heavy operators, resolving their dependence on spin and extending previous results at zero spatial separation. We test these formulae in the three-dimensional Ising CFT, finding good agreement between the resulting truncated correlators and Monte Carlo data.

</details>


### [163] [Split Heun functions via blown-up surface defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24920)
*Saebyeok Jeong,Tommaso Pedroni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维N=2 SU(2)规范理论（含基本超多重态）的NS极限下，研究了Heun方程及其合流极限的共振解。在共振点处，瞬子展开出现极点，作者推导出仅涉及NS函数的爆破方程，用于重求和这些奇异展开。重求和后的函数揭示了规范理论解在共振点附近的解析结构，并可用于构造谱带边缘的周期/反周期解及其对数伴随解。此外，还识别了控制能隙闭合和半单共振单向性的嵌套质量轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当Coulomb分支参数取共振值（2a/ħ为整数）时，Floquet乘子合并，体NS函数和面缺陷NS函数的瞬子展开出现极点，逐项展开掩盖了真正的解析结构。因此需要一种不依赖于瞬子展开的重求和方法来揭示共振点附近的解析行为。

Method: 推导仅涉及NS函数的"爆破方程"（blow-up equations），利用这些方程对体NS函数和面缺陷NS函数的奇异瞬子展开进行重求和。重求和后的函数揭示了辅助参数（accessory parameter）和Floquet解的支割线结构。在共振点取有限极限，构造谱带边缘的周期/反周期解及其对数伴随解。进一步识别嵌套的质量轨迹：在大轨迹上带边辅助参数合并，在小轨迹上两个独立的共振Floquet解存活。该方法适用于N_f=(n0,n1)且ni≤2的理论，并以N_f=(1,1)理论为例进行演示。

Result: 重求和后的体NS函数和面缺陷NS函数成功揭示了共振点附近的解析结构，包括辅助参数和Floquet解的支割线结构（这些在逐项瞬子展开中被掩盖）。在共振点处，重求和的辅助参数和适当归一化的缺陷波函数具有有限极限，可描述谱带边缘的周期/反周期解，并能构造其对数伴随解。识别出嵌套的质量轨迹：在大轨迹上带边辅助参数合并，在小轨迹上两个独立的共振（反）周期Floquet解存活。给出了N_f=(1,1)理论的完整显式演示。

Conclusion: 爆破方程为处理NS极限下瞬子展开的共振奇点提供了系统框架，重求和技术揭示了被逐项展开掩盖的解析结构。该方法适用于广泛的N_f理论类型，为理解规范理论在共振Coulomb分支参数处的行为、能隙闭合机制以及与Heun方程谱理论的联系提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study resonant solutions of the Heun equation and its confluent limits that arise in the Nekrasov--Shatashvili (NS) limit of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ gauge theories with fundamental hypermultiplets. At the resonant loci $2a/\hbar\in\mathbb{Z}$ in the Coulomb branch parameter $a$, the Floquet multipliers coalesce and the instanton expansions of the bulk and surface defect NS functions develop poles of increasing order. We derive blow-up equations involving exclusively NS functions and use them to resum these singular expansions. The resulting resummed bulk and surface defect NS functions reveal the analytic structure of the gauge-theoretic solutions near the resonant loci, including the branch structure of the accessory parameter and of the Floquet solutions that is obscured by the term-by-term instanton expansion. At resonance, the resummed accessory parameters and suitably normalized defect wavefunctions admit finite limits that describe periodic or antiperiodic solutions at the edges of spectral gaps and allow us to construct their logarithmic companions. We then identify distinct nested mass loci governing gap closure and semisimple resonant monodromy. On the larger locus the band-edge accessory parameters coalesce, while on the smaller locus two independent resonant (anti)periodic Floquet solutions survive. We develop the general resummation procedure for $N_f=(n_0,n_1)$ theories with $n_i\leq 2$ $(i=0,1)$, and demonstrate it explicitly for the $N_f=(1,1)$ theory.

</details>


### [164] [Unitary Dual-Resonance S-matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24922)
*Claudia de Rham,Andrew J. Tolley,Zhuo-Hui Wang,Shuang-Yong Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的完全幺正的双共振振幅构造方法，不同于传统世界面构造，将零宽度共振塔推广为物理吸收谱，保留交叉对称性与Regge组织，同时通过非标准eikonal化机制实现幺正化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双共振模型虽能紧凑实现交叉对称性、Regge行为和无穷多共振，但其树图实现缺乏真正的S矩阵幺正性。需要构造真正幺正且保留双共振优良解析结构的振幅。

Method: 作者将零宽度共振塔提升为具有物理吸收谱的共振态，通过一种区别于标准eikonal化的新机制对振幅进行幺正化，同时保持交叉对称性、Regge组织、局域性和解析性，并可引入可控的第二黎曼面Regge轨迹及显著非弹性度。

Result: 成功构造出完全幺正的双共振S矩阵，该矩阵保持局域性、解析性，具有可控的第二黎曼面Regge轨迹和可观的非弹性度，且保留了交叉对称性和Regge组织。

Conclusion: 该构造为探索一致性强耦合S矩阵空间开辟了新路径，超越了传统世界面方法的局限，对理解强耦合散射的完整理论空间具有重要意义。

Abstract: Dual-resonance models realize crossing symmetry, Regge behavior and infinitely many resonances in a compact analytic form but their canonical tree-level realizations fall short of genuine S-matrix unitarity. Here we explicitly construct fully unitary dual-resonance amplitudes distinct from the conventional worldsheet construction. The zero-width resonance towers are promoted into a physical absorptive spectrum that retains their characteristic crossing and Regge organization, while unitarizing the amplitude through a mechanism distinct from standard eikonalization. The resulting S-matrices are also local and analytic, and can contain controllable second-sheet Regge trajectories as well as substantial inelasticity. More broadly, our construction opens up a new route to charting the space of consistent strongly coupled S-matrices.

</details>


### [165] [Feynman Tree Theorem and the Gelfand--Yaglom Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24923)
*Ipak Fadakar,Guilherme L. Pimentel,Behrang Tafreshi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用费曼树定理为Gelfand-Yaglom公式提供了一个新的图解证明，将单圈行列式表达为树图求和，并推广至一般边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Polyakov建议的启发，作者试图为Gelfand-Yaglom公式提供一个更直观、更结构的图解推导，以揭示单圈行列式与树图展开之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用费曼树定理（Feynman Tree Theorem），通过对单圈行列式进行"切割"，将其重新表达为树图（tree diagrams）之和，这些树图显式地编码了Gelfand-Yaglom初值问题的解。

Result: 成功给出了Gelfand-Yaglom公式的一个全新图解证明，并将该图解框架从Dirichlet边界条件推广到一般边界条件。

Conclusion: 图解方法不仅为Gelfand-Yaglom公式提供了新的理解视角，还指出了向更一般的量子场论推广的可能性。

Abstract: The Gelfand--Yaglom formula equates the one-loop determinant of a Schrödinger operator with Dirichlet boundary conditions to the solution of an initial value problem. Following a suggestion by Polyakov, we provide a new diagrammatic proof of this formula as a direct application of the Feynman Tree Theorem. By cutting the one-loop determinant, we re-express it as a sum of tree diagrams. These trees explicitly encode the solution to the Gelfand--Yaglom initial value problem. We extend this diagrammatic framework to general boundary conditions and comment on a potential generalization to quantum field theory.

</details>


### [166] [Cross-Correlations of Metric and Monopole Perturbations from Holographic Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24989)
*Matheus Cravo,Juliana Z. Finotti,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在3维全息宇宙学玩具模型中计算了能动张量与两个SO(3)全局流的三点函数⟨TJJ⟩。通过全息字典和Sl(2,Z)对偶性，将该QFT三点函数映射到宇宙学中度量扰动与磁单极场的非高斯性关联⟨ξÃÃ⟩和⟨γÃÃ⟩。作者计算了实现压缩极限下精确因子化所需的半局域接触项，并评估了有效非线性参数：标量-单极交叉关联消失(f_NL^{ξÃÃ}=0)，而张量-单极交叉关联非零，符合非高斯关联的振幅层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用全息对偶将边界QFT的关联函数映射到宇宙学可观测的非高斯性，从而为全息宇宙学提供新的可实验检验的理论预言。

Method: 在3维全息宇宙学玩具模型中，对⟨TJJ⟩三点函数进行单圈计算；利用全息字典将QFT的n点函数映射到宇宙学关联函数；通过Sl(2,Z)对偶性将电Noether流映射为磁涡旋流（对偶于宇宙学磁单极）；计算压缩极限下的半局域接触项以保证因子化；最后评估有效非线性参数f_NL。

Result: 标量-单极交叉关联的非线性参数f_NL^{ξÃÃ}=0；张量-单极交叉关联的非线性参数f_NL^{γÃÃ}非零。该结果尊重非高斯关联函数预期的振幅层次结构。

Conclusion: ⟨TJJ⟩三点函数的全息分析揭示了宇宙学中度量扰动与磁单极场之间的非高斯交叉关联特征，其中张量模式的非零信号为全息宇宙学的实验检验指明了新方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the 1-loop calculation of the three-point function $\langle TJJ \rangle$ of the stress-energy tensor and two insertions of $SO(3)$ global currents, using a 3d toy model for holographic cosmology. By applying the holographic dictionary that relates these QFT $n$-point functions to cosmological correlators, together with the $Sl(2,\mathbb{Z})$ duality that maps the electric Noether current to a magnetic vortex current dual to cosmological magnetic monopoles, we relate the $\langle TJJ \rangle$ correlator to the cross-correlations between metric perturbations and the bulk magnetic monopole field, specifically mapping to the non-Gaussianities $\langle ξ\tilde{A} \tilde{A} \rangle$ and $\langle γ\tilde{A} \tilde{A} \rangle$. We calculate the semi-local contact terms necessary to achieve the exact factorization of the cosmological correlators in the squeezed limit, $p_1 \to 0$. Finally, we evaluate the effective non-linear parameters, showing that the scalar-monopole cross-correlation vanishes at leading order, $f_{NL}^{ξ\tilde{A} \tilde{A}} = 0$, while the tensor-monopole cross-correlation yields a non-zero $f_{NL}^{γ\tilde A\tilde A}$. These results respect the expected amplitude hierarchy of the non-Gaussian correlators, while pointing at new directions in which holographic cosmology can be tested experimentally.

</details>


### [167] [Quantum Gravity Corrections to Giant Graviton Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25054)
*Junding Chen,Xi-Er Du,Hynek Paul,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 理论的超引力极限下，计算了应力张量多重态中两个极大巨引力子和两个轻超引力子关联函数的最低阶量子引力修正。将巨引力子视为零维缺陷，利用缺陷版 AdS 幺正性方法从已知树级关联函数重建单圈修正，并在 Mellin 空间和位置空间中给出了闭合形式的单圈结果，同时提取了缺陷通道 OPE 中描述巨引力子-轻超引力子束缚态的算符在最低共形 twist 处的反常量纲量子修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 理论超引力极限中巨引力子关联函数的量子引力修正，以深入理解 AdS/CFT 对偶中重-轻算符混合系统的动力学，特别是巨引力子作为零维缺陷的非微扰效应及其与轻超引力子的相互作用。

Method: 采用缺陷版 AdS 幺正性方法（defect version of the AdS unitarity method），将两个极大巨引力子视为零维缺陷，利用已知的树级关联函数作为输入，通过幺正性切割技术重建单圈图；分别在 Mellin 空间和位置空间中进行解析计算，得到闭合形式的结果。

Result: 成功计算出两个极大巨引力子与两个轻超引力子关联函数的单圈量子引力修正，并在 Mellin 空间和位置空间中给出了闭合解析表达式。进一步从缺陷通道的算符乘积展开（OPE）中提取了巨引力子-轻超引力子束缚态算符在最低共形 twist 处的反常量纲量子修正。

Conclusion: 缺陷版 AdS 幺正性方法是处理 AdS/CFT 中巨引力子关联函数量子修正的有效工具，所得结果为研究重-轻算符混合系统的共形数据提供了新的定量依据，并有助于进一步探索强耦合 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 中缺陷算符的谱和动力学。

Abstract: We compute the leading quantum gravity correction to the correlation function of two maximal giant gravitons and two light supergravitons in the stress tensor multiplet in the supergravity limit of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM theory. By viewing the giant gravitons as a zero-dimensional defect, we employ the defect version of the AdS unitarity method to reconstruct the one-loop correction from the known tree-level correlators. We obtain the one-loop result in closed form in both Mellin and position space. We also extract the quantum correction to the anomalous dimension of operators describing a bound state of a giant graviton and a light supergraviton at the leading conformal twist in the defect channel OPE.

</details>


### [168] [A discrete series gauge field at the late-time boundary of $dS_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25067)
*Manizheh Botshekananfard,Elif Büşra Güraksın,Vasileios A. Letsios,Gizem Şengör*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了dS₄时空中自由麦克斯韦场的正则量子化，从表示论视角分析了单粒子态在SO(4,1)变换下的性质。在晚期极限下识别出Δ=1和Δ=2两个算子，它们均对应SO(4,1)的酉离散序列表示，与AdS情形不同。利用自对偶与反自对偶分解实现了螺旋度±1的分离，并探讨了与三维共形规范场可能的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解dS₄时空中光子场的表示论结构，特别是晚期算子如何实现SO(4,1)的酉离散序列表示，以及其与AdS情形的差异。同时为dS₄高自旋引力的微观描述（涉及三维共形规范场）提供可能的联系。

Method: 在Bunch-Davies真空中对dS₄上的自由麦克斯韦场进行正则量子化；利用无穷小dS变换分析单粒子态的变换性质；在晚期极限下提取Δ=1和Δ=2算子；引入受CFT启发的、dS群不变的内积；利用场强的自对偶与反自对偶分解实现螺旋度分离。

Result: 晚期Δ=1（主导）和Δ=2（次主导）两个算子均能赋予SO(4,1)的酉离散序列表示，这与AdS上Δ=1对应非归一化模的情形截然不同。通过自对偶/反自对偶分解，对应的离散序列表示可拆分为螺旋度+1和-1两个表示的直和。

Conclusion: dS₄上光子的表示论结构展现了与AdS情形的本质差异：Δ=1和Δ=2模均对应酉离散序列表示。该框架为探索Δ=1晚期算子与三维共形自旋-1规范场之间的联系，进而为dS₄高自旋引力的微观描述提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study the free Maxwell field on the planar patch of four-dimensional de Sitter spacetime ($dS_4$). We review its bulk canonical quantization in the Bunch-Davies vacuum, and we give a representation-theoretic viewpoint by studying the transformation properties of single-particle states under infinitesimal dS transformations. By taking the late-time limit, we identify two late-time operators with scaling dimensions $Δ=1$ (leading) and $Δ=2$ (subleading). We introduce CFT-inspired inner products invariant under the dS group ($SO(4,1)$) for states created by late-time operators acting on the Bunch-Davies vacuum. We explain how the unitary discrete series representations of $SO(4,1)$ associated with the photon are furnished by both operators, in contrast with the Maxwell field on $AdS$ where the $Δ=1$ operator corresponds to a non-normalizable mode. We also explain how the corresponding discrete series representations of $SO(4,1)$ split into a direct sum of representations corresponding to two helicities $+1$ and $-1$. This is achieved by taking advantage of the dS invariance of the self-dual and anti-self-dual sectors of the photon field strength. In our outlook, we draw inspiration from a recent proposal for the microscopic description of $dS_4$ higher-spin gravity where conformal gauge fields in 3 dimensions play a central role, and we investigate a possible connection of the $Δ=1$ late-time operator with a conformal spin-1 gauge field in 3 dimensions.

</details>


### [169] [Derivation of the NS5-brane limit of the plane wave matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25116)
*Yuhma Asano,Goro Ishiki,Shinji Shimasaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从规范场论侧验证了BMN矩阵模型中存在非平凡双重标度极限，该极限通过规范/引力对偶对应于IIA型小弦论在R×S⁵上的描述，并推导出一个描述1/4 BPS扇区的特征值积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规范/引力对偶的预测——BMN平面波矩阵模型在非平凡双重标度极限下应对应于IIA型小弦论（LST）在R×S⁵上的描述，作者旨在从规范场论一侧严格证明该极限的存在性，从而为LST的微观描述提供来自矩阵模型的直接证据。

Method: 作者在规范场论框架下，分别对BMN矩阵模型的配分函数和特定1/4 BPS扇区进行分析，通过构造并取适当的双重标度极限，将矩阵模型约化为一个可用于描述LST中1/4 BPS扇区的特征值积分表达式。

Result: 成功证明了该非平凡双重标度极限在配分函数和1/4 BPS扇区中确实存在，并导出了一个具体的特征值积分，该积分被预期可刻画IIA型小弦论的1/4 BPS扇区。

Conclusion: 该工作从规范场论侧证实了由规范/引力对偶预言的BMN矩阵模型与IIA型小弦论之间的对应关系，为小弦论的非微扰研究提供了具体的矩阵模型实现和可计算框架。

Abstract: From the gauge/gravity duality, it was predicted that there exists a nontrivial double scaling limit of the plane wave matrix model (the BMN matrix model), which describes the type IIA little string theory (LST) on $R\times S^5$. We show on the gauge theory side that such a limit indeed exists for the partition function and in a certain 1/4 BPS sector of the matrix model, and consequently derive an eigenvalue integral, which is expected to describe the 1/4 BPS sector of LST.

</details>


### [170] [Monodromy, Hidden Conformal Symmetry and Soft Hair in Kerr-MOG Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25205)
*Parthapratim Pradhan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过热力学、单值性分析和软毛形式三种独立方法，建立了四维Kerr-MOG黑洞与二维共形场论的对偶关系，推导了左右手温度和中心荷，揭示了隐藏共形对称性，并用Cardy公式和灰体因子验证了微观熵与宏观熵的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索修正引力理论（MOG）中旋转黑洞的微观起源，检验Kerr/CFT对偶在超出广义相对论框架下的有效性，并研究Kerr-MOG黑洞熵的微观统计力学解释。

Method: 采用三种互补方法：（1）黑洞热力学分析；（2）单值性（monodromy）分析；（3）软毛（soft-hair）形式。进而分析非极端Kerr-MOG黑洞近区无质量标量场的动力学，揭示隐藏的SL(2,R)_L × SL(2,R)_R共形对称性，并利用Cardy公式计算微观熵，与Bekenstein-Hawking熵比对。

Result: 三种方法导出左右手温度T_L、T_R和中心荷的一致表达式；熵乘积既不普适也不量子化；Cardy公式给出的微观熵与Bekenstein-Hawking宏观熵精确匹配；低频标量吸收截面与对偶2D CFT的有限温度吸收截面吻合；近区标量波动方程具有隐藏的SL(2,R)_L × SL(2,R)_R共形对称性，且该对称性以Vir_L ⊗ Vir_R微分同胚形式非平凡地作用于黑洞视界。

Conclusion: Kerr-MOG黑洞存在稳健的Kerr/CFT型全息对偶，四维Kerr-MOG时空与二维共形场论之间存在一致的对应关系，为修正引力中黑洞微观态计数提供了有力证据。

Abstract: We investigate the two-dimensional conformal field theory (2D CFT) dual of the four-dimensional Kerr-MOG (Kerr-modified gravity) black hole using three complementary approaches: (i) black hole thermodynamics, (ii) monodromy analysis, and (iii) the soft-hair formalism. From these independent methods, we derive consistent expressions for the left- and right-moving temperatures and the central charge of the dual CFT. Finally, we derive the entropy product using these approaches. We show that the product is not universal as well as not quantized. We further explore the hidden conformal symmetry of the non-extremal Kerr-MOG black hole by analyzing the near-region dynamics of a massless scalar field. The resulting Kerr-MOG/CFT correspondence identifies the near-region geometry with a two-dimensional CFT characterized by the temperatures ($T_{L}, T_{R}$) derived in Sec. 3. Moreover, using the Cardy formula, we reproduce the microscopic entropy of the dual CFT and find exact agreement with the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy of the Kerr-MOG black hole. We also show that the low-frequency scalar absorption cross section~(greybody factor) in the near-region Kerr-MOG geometry precisely matches the finite-temperature absorption cross section of the dual 2D CFT. In addition, the near-region scalar wave equation exhibits a hidden $(SL(2,\mathbb{R})_L \times SL(2,\mathbb{R})_R)$ conformal symmetry. These independent results provide strong evidence for the existence of a Kerr/CFT-type holographic duality for the Kerr-MOG black hole, establishing a consistent correspondence between the four-dimensional Kerr-MOG spacetime and a two-dimensional conformal field theory. Furthermore, the hidden conformal symmetry is realized in the form of $\rm {Vir_L} \otimes \rm{ Vir_R}$ diffeomorphisms which act non-trivially on the black hole horizon.

</details>


### [171] [The quantum electrodynamics for dyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25226)
*D. O. R. Azevedo,O. M. Del Cima,T. S. Dias,E. D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于Cabibbo-Ferrari-Salam的双U(1)规范结构，构建了带双荷（电荷和磁荷）费米子（即双子，dyon）的量子电动力学（dQED）。通过BRS代数重整化与BPHZL减法方案，证明该模型无反常且在所有微扰阶上可乘性重整化，从而确立了其量子一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将1960年代Cabibbo、Ferrari和Salam关于带电磁双荷费米子的开创性工作发展为完整的量子场论框架，搁置传统的狄拉克量子化条件，采用U(1)×U(1)规范群来描述光子（矢量场）与亚光子/赝光子（赝矢量场），以构建自洽的量子电动力学理论。

Method: 采用U(1)×U(1)规范群，分别对应矢量场（光子）和赝矢量场（亚光子/metaphoton）。在树图层次分析谱一致性并确立所有连续和分立对称性。量子分析方面，使用与正规化方案无关的BRS代数重整化方法，并在BPHZL重整化框架内引入Lowenstein-Zimmermann减法方案，以处理无质量规范场带来的红外问题。

Result: 验证了所提出的dQED模型无任何反常，且在所有微扰阶上满足乘性可重整性，从而确立了该理论的量子一致性。

Conclusion: 基于Cabibbo-Ferrari-Salam框架构建的dQED是一个量子自洽的理论，它成功地绕开了狄拉克量子化条件，并经受住了严格的重整化检验，为带电磁双荷费米子的量子场论描述提供了可靠的基础。

Abstract: The quantum electrodynamics for massive fermions carrying electric and magnetic charges, the dyons, is proposed based on the 1960's seminal works by Cabibbo, Ferrari, and Salam, with the gauge group being $U(1)\times U(1)$, which is associated to a vector field (photon) and a pseudo-vector field (metaphoton), wherein the Dirac quantization is set aside. At the tree level the spectrum consistency of the model is analyzed, and all continuous and discrete symmetries are established. The quantum analysis is performed by using the Becchi-Rouet-Stora (BRS) algebraic renormalization method, which is independent on any regularization scheme. Moreover, thanks to the presence of massless gauge fields, the Lowenstein-Zimmermann (LZ) subtraction scheme is required within the framework of the Bogoliubov-Parasiuk-Hepp-Zimmermann-Lowenstein (BPHZL) renormalization procedure. Finally, it is verified that the proposed model, the dyon quantum electrodynamics (dQED), is free from any anomaly and is multiplicative renormalizable at all orders in perturbation theory, proving, therefore, its quantum consistency.

</details>


### [172] [String theory mathematics and matrix data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25500)
*Sanjaye Ramgoolam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了置换不变高斯矩阵模型（PIGMM），该模型用有限置换对称性 $S_N$ 替代传统随机矩阵理论中的连续对称性，通过表示论将高维矩阵数据分析问题约化为仅含13个参数的可处理形式，并介绍了其在计算语言学、统计金融和神经网络权重中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机矩阵理论依赖连续对称性处理 $N \times N$ 矩阵，但面对高度多变量的 $N^2$ 变量数据分析问题时计算复杂度过高。作者希望利用有限置换对称性 $S_N$ 的表示论，将复杂的高维矩阵问题转化为参数空间可处理的低维形式。

Method: 采用置换不变高斯矩阵模型（PIGMM），以有限置换群 $S_N$ 的表示论为核心工具，将高度相关的二次矩阵作用量对角化至仅含13个参数的近似对角形式。模型的不变观测量由图（graphs）参数化，其期望值通过算法实现的 Wick 收缩进行计算。

Result: PIGMM 成功应用于计算语言学、统计金融和神经网络权重领域，实现了数据降维与异常检测。模型将原本 $N^2$ 变量的高维问题压缩至13个参数的可控空间，同时保持了置换不变性下的丰富结构。

Conclusion: PIGMM 为矩阵数据分析提供了一种基于对称性的有效降维方法。未来有望拓展至利用强子化算法和对撞机物理数据模块化结构的矩阵数据分析任务中。

Abstract: Inspired by matrix techniques in quantum field theory and string theory, we review Permutation Invariant Gaussian Matrix Models (PIGMM), which replace the continuous symmetries of traditional Random Matrix Theory, for $ N \times N$ matrices, with finite permutation symmetry, $S_N$. This symmetry-driven approach reduces highly multivariate $N^2$-variable matrix data analysis problems to a rich but tractable space of parameters. The representation theory of $S_N$ brings a highly correlated quadratic matrix action to a near-diagonal form with $13$ parameters. The invariant observables are parameterised by graphs and their expectation values are computed with Wick contractions implemented algorithmically. We review the successful application of PIGMM for data reduction and anomaly detection in computational linguistics, statistical finance and neural network weights. We conclude with a brief discussion of potential future applications to matrix data analysis tasks that exploit hadronization algorithms and the modular structure of collider-physics data.

</details>


### [173] [Toward the Structure Constants of $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25662)
*Beatrix Mühlmann,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了二维球面上类空N=2刘维尔理论的结构常数。由于该理论缺少b↔b⁻¹自对偶性，传统解析自举方法失效。作者利用镜像对称性将N=2刘维尔理论与SL(2)_k/U(1)超陪集（2D费米子黑洞）对偶，在超陪集侧获得了绕数守恒与破坏的结构常数显式表达，并通过半经典分析在单圈阶验证了一致性，同时讨论了手征环和1/2-BPS算符的关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N=2刘维尔理论缺少b↔b⁻¹自对偶对称性，导致此前成功求解玻色子和N=1理论的标准解析自举方法无法直接套用，需要寻找新的途径来推导该理论的结构常数。

Method: 利用镜像对称性，将N=2刘维尔理论对偶到SL(2)_k/U(1)超陪集模型（即二维费米子黑洞），在超陪集侧显式计算绕数守恒和绕数破坏的结构常数，再通过半经典分析在刘维尔侧进行单圈阶验证。

Result: 在超陪集侧获得了绕数守恒与绕数破坏两类结构常数的显式表达式；半经典分析表明这些结果在刘维尔侧一致，验证达到单圈阶精度；还给出了理论的手征环描述，并计算了1/2-BPS算符的关联函数。

Conclusion: 通过镜像对称性对偶，成功解决了因缺乏自对偶性而难以用传统自举方法处理的N=2刘维尔理论结构常数问题，结果在半经典水平上自洽，为该理论的完整理解提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We discuss the structure constants of spacelike $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville theory on the two-sphere. Due to the absence of a particular $b \leftrightarrow b^{-1}$ self-dual symmetry, where $b$ is the theory's coupling, the standard analytic bootstrap toolkit that was used to solve the bosonic and $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories cannot be implemented in a straightforward way. Our approach, instead, relies on the fact that $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville theory is dual by mirror symmetry to the $\mathrm{SL}(2)_k/\mathrm{U}(1)$ supercoset, whose target space is the $2$d fermionic black hole. Leveraging this duality, we obtain explicit expressions for the winding number preserving and violating structure constants on the supercoset side, and test them on the Liouville side through a semiclassical analysis finding agreement up to one-loop order. We also discuss the chiral rings of the theory and evaluate correlators of $\frac 12$-BPS operators.

</details>


### [174] [The coupling flow for supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25714)
*Federico Arrighi,Saurish Khandelwal,Olaf Lechtenfeld*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维庞加莱超引力中Nicolai映射的泛函微分流方程，通过超共形表述推导出有效 vierbein 理论中的"流算子"，并论证了BRST规范固定带来的乘性项可通过Wick定理转化为导性项，从而在朗道规范下实现围绕闵氏时空的微扰展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nicolai映射可将超对称场论中的量子关联函数编码，其无穷小逆映射通过"流方程"描述耦合常数变化下的响应。此前构建庞加莱超引力的Nicolai映射面临三大障碍，本文旨在克服其中两个：BRST规范固定破坏流算子的导性性质、以及流方程在κ=0处的奇性问题。

Method: 作者采用四维N=1庞加莱超引力的超共形（superconformal）离壳表述，将完整超共形离壳作用量表达为一个超变分，由此推导出有效vierbein理论中的泛函微分"流算子"。针对BRST规范固定引入的乘性项，提出可借助自由有效理论中的Wick定理将其重写为导性形式。进一步在朗道规范下证明流方程在κ=0处正则，允许围绕闵氏几何进行微扰展开。

Result: 成功推导出超引力的规范不变部分所对应的全阶（逆）Nicolai映射的流算子；论证了BRST乘性项原则上可转化为导性项；证明在朗道规范下流方程在κ=0处正则；由此克服了此前尝试中的三个障碍中的两个。最后与κ领头阶的直接在壳构造进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文通过超共形方法为庞加莱超引力建立了Nicolai映射的流方程框架，解决了BRST规范固定带来的表观障碍和κ=0的奇性问题，但仍遗留一个障碍待解决。该工作为超引力中Nicolai映射的全阶构造奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Quantum correlation functions in supersymmetric field theories can be encoded in the Nicolai map. The infinitesimal inverse map provides their response to a change in a coupling constant through a ``flow equation''. We investigate the flow in the gravitational constant $κ$ for four-dimensional Poincaré supergravity via its superconformal formulation. By expressing the full superconformal off-shell action as a supervariation we derive a ``flow operator'' in the effective vierbein theory, whose exponentiation yields the gauge-invariant part of an all-order (inverse) Nicolai map for supergravity. Alas, the BRST gauge fixing adds to this functional differential flow operator a multiplicative term, which ruins its derivational property and therefore jeopardizes its connection with the Nicolai map. We argue and present an example, however, that such multiplicative flow contributions might be rewritable as derivational ones with the help of Wick's theorem in the free effective theory where the Nicolai-transformed correlators are ultimately evaluated. At least in the Landau gauge, the supergravity flow equation is shown to be regular at $κ{=}0$, allowing for a perturbative expansion around Minkowski geometry. Therewith, we have overcome two of the three obstacles met in an earlier attempt towards a Nicolai map for Poincaré supergravity. Finally, we compare with the direct on-shell construction of a Nicolai map to leading order in $κ$.

</details>


### [175] [Balanced Metrics Know About SYZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25733)
*Per Berglund,Tristan Hübsch,Vishnu Jejjala,Viktor Mirjanić,Challenger Mishra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Donaldson算法变体，在环绕空间全局截面上操作以计算Calabi-Yau流形上的可解释平衡度量。应用于Dwork族和完全交CY流形时，发现在大复结构极限附近度量参数服从新的幂律，并与SYZ猜想预测的Gromov-Hausdorff度量坍缩联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Calabi-Yau流形上的数值Ricci-flat度量虽日益精确，但缺乏可解释性，难以从中提取理论洞见。作者旨在开发一种既能保持数值精度、又能提供解析可解释性的新算法，以便在大复结构极限附近发现度量行为的规律性。

Method: 作者引入Donaldson算法的一种新变体，核心创新在于使用Moore-Penrose伪逆，直接在环绕空间（ambient space）的全局截面上操作，而非在流形本身上操作。这使算法能够利用典范单项式基（canonical monomial basis），即使在较大度数k下也能计算可解释的平衡度量（balanced metrics）。

Result: 将该"环绕算法"应用于多个族（包括Dwork族和完全交Calabi-Yau流形），发现度量参数在大复结构极限（LCSL）附近服从新颖的幂律（power laws）。这些幂律行为被进一步与Strominger-Yau-Zaslow猜想所预测的Gromov-Hausdorff度量坍缩现象建立了联系。

Conclusion: 所提出的环绕空间算法成功地在保持计算可行性的同时，赋予了Calabi-Yau度量以可解释性，揭示了大复结构极限附近度量参数的普适幂律行为，为SYZ猜想的度量坍缩图像提供了直接的数值证据和理论连接。

Abstract: Numerical Ricci-flat metrics on Calabi-Yau manifolds are becoming increasingly accurate. However, they often lack the interpretability required to extract theoretical insights. In this paper, we introduce a novel variant of Donaldson's algorithm based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse that operates on the global sections of the ambient space rather than the manifold itself. This approach allows us to use the canonical monomial basis to compute interpretable balanced metrics even at large degrees $k$. Applying our ambient algorithm to multiple families, including the Dwork family and complete intersection Calabi-Yau manifolds, we discover that the metric parameters obey novel power laws near the Large Complex Structure Limit (LCSL). We connect these to the Gromov-Hausdorff metric collapse predicted by the SYZ conjecture.

</details>


### [176] [Curved momentum space and finite Landau spectrum in $κ$-Minkowski spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25833)
*Adrián Huamán Vargas,Vladislav Kupriyanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从动量空间的de Sitter几何出发，导出κ-Poincaré Casimir算符，并在Poisson规范理论框架下将其作为带电粒子的动力学约束。作者研究了标量粒子和自旋1/2粒子在恒定磁场中的行为，获得了包含形变参数1/κ所有阶的精确能谱。动量空间曲率导致最大不变动量，进而产生有限Landau能谱，并出现最高Landau能级(HLL)。在费米子情形中，截断与自旋相关，导致极化HLL。文章还简要讨论了这种紫外截断的可能影响及其与反常现象的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索动量空间弯曲（κ-Poincaré对称性）对带电粒子在磁场中量子行为的影响，特别是理解紫外截断如何导致有限Landau能谱和最高Landau能级的出现。

Method: 利用动量空间的de Sitter几何导出κ-Poincaré Casimir算符，将其作为Poisson规范理论中的动力学约束；在此框架下分别求解标量粒子和自旋1/2粒子在恒定磁场中的能谱，保留形变参数1/κ的全部阶贡献。

Result: 获得了标量和费米子情形的精确能谱（包含1/κ所有阶）；动量空间曲率引入最大不变动量，导致Landau能谱有限化，出现最高Landau能级(HLL)；费米子的HLL位置与自旋相关，呈现极化特性。

Conclusion: κ-Poincaré形变自然导致Landau能谱的紫外截断，产生最高Landau能级。费米子情形下该截断具有自旋依赖性，形成极化HLL。这一机制可能与反常相关现象有关，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: It is obtained the $κ$-Poincare Casimir from the de Sitter geometry of momentum space and employed as the dynamical constraint governing charged particles within the framework of Poisson gauge theory. The resulting formalism is applied to investigate both scalar and spin-1/2 particles in a constant magnetic field. Exact energy spectra are obtained, including all orders in the deformation parameter $1/κ$. The curvature of momentum space implies a maximal invariant momentum, which in turn leads to a finite Landau spectrum characterized by the existence of a Highest Landau Level (HLL). In the fermionic case, the truncation becomes spin dependent, resulting in a polarized HLL. Possible implications of this ultraviolet truncation and its relation to anomaly-related phenomena are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [177] [Maximal $D=5$ trombone supergravity from M5-branes and $\mathrm{SU}(2)$-flavoured $\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}$ operator spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25952)
*Ritabrata Bhattacharya,Abhay Katyal,Oscar Varela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了同一个 D=5, N=8 规范超引力可以从 M-理论在两类不同内部空间上的相容截断导出：一是 Maldacena-Núñez AdS₅ 解（对应 N=2 的 class S 理论），二是包含 N=1 MN1 解在内的 BBBW 解族。作者进一步计算了 MN1 解上的 KK 谱的普适扇区，并给出提取全局定义子扇区的方法，该谱对偶于 SU(2)-味 N=1 class S SCFT 的轻算符谱的普适扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者构造了一个带有局域伸缩对称性的 D=5 N=8 规范超引力，但仅与 N=2 的 AdS₅ 解关联。本文旨在将该框架推广到 N=1 情形（特别是 BBBW 解族），并利用新推导的质量矩阵计算 MN1 解上的普适 KK 谱，从而建立与对偶 N=1 SCFT 中轻算符谱的联系。

Method: 使用例外广义几何/场论（Exceptional Generalised Geometry/Field Theory）方法，将 D=11 超引力在 BBBW 解族（含 MN1）上进行相容截断，导出相同的 D=5 N=8 规范超引力。随后利用最近推导的质量矩阵计算 MN1 背景上的 KK 谱的普适扇区，并提出从局域定义的谱中提取全局定义子扇区的方案。

Result: (1) D=5 N=8 规范超引力同样可以从 BBBW 解族（N=1 情形）的相容截断得到。 (2) 给出了 MN1 解上 KK 谱的普适扇区（仅局域定义）。 (3) 提供了提取全局定义子扇区的 prescription，该全局普适 KK 谱对偶于 MN1 对偶的 SU(2)-味 N=1 class S SCFT 中轻算符谱的普适扇区。

Conclusion: 该 D=5 N=8 规范超引力具有普适性，可统一描述 N=2 和 N=1 两类 M5-膜缠绕解。通过全局定义的普适 KK 谱，可直接关联到对偶 N=1 SCFT 的轻算符谱，为 holographic 谱分析提供了新的系统方法。

Abstract: We recently presented a new $D=5$ $\mathcal{N}=8$ gauged supergravity involving the local trombone scaling symmetry. It arises by consistent truncation of M-theory on the internal space of the Maldacena-Núñez AdS$_5$ solution dual to the $\mathcal{N}=2$ four-dimensional superconformal field theory (SCFT) of class $\mathcal{S}$ associated to M5-branes wrapped on an unpunctured Riemann surface. Using exceptional generalised geometry/field theory, we extend that construction to show that the same $D=5$ $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity also arises by consistent truncation of $D=11$ supergravity on the family of $\mathcal{N}=1$ M5-brane-wrapped solutions of Bah-Beem-Bobev-Wecht, including the $\mathcal{N}=1$ Maldacena-Núñez (MN1) configuration. Then, using recently derived mass matrices, we compute universal sectors of the Kaluza-Klein spectrum on the MN1 solution. In general, this universal spectrum is only locally defined, and we give a prescription for extracting globally defined subsectors thereof. This globally defined universal Kaluza-Klein spectrum is dual to a universal sector of the light operator spectrum of the SU(2)-flavoured $\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}$ SCFT dual to MN1.

</details>


### [178] [Krylov-Space Memory Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26011)
*Mohsen Alishahiha,Mohammad Javad Vasli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出"Krylov空间记忆核心"概念，作为一种稳态的深度分辨结构，用于揭示热化非可积系统中异常初态记忆在Krylov空间内的组织方式。通过稳态占据谱、残余涨落、Gibbs偏差及Krylov流涨落等诊断量，发现弱热化、受限诱导异常动力学和多体疤痕等场景中，异常初态会形成紧凑的低深度记忆核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热化系统中，某些异常初态（如多体疤痕态、弱热化态）会长期保留初始信息，违背常规热化预期。作者旨在寻找一个稳态框架，在Krylov空间中定位和表征这种结构化量子记忆的组织方式，区分异常态与一般参考态。

Method: 引入Krylov空间记忆核心作为稳态深度分辨结构，结合多组诊断工具：稳态占据谱（定位Krylov链上晚期概率集中区域）、残余平衡涨落、与Gibbs参考态的偏差、以及长时间Krylov流涨落。在弱热化、受限动力学和多体疤痕三种场景下验证，并以可积系统作为辅助对照，确认记忆核心的态选择性。

Result: 异常初态在所有测试场景中均形成紧凑的低深度记忆核心，携带显著残余涨落、Gibbs失配和持续流涨落活性；这些核心常嵌于更宽的稳态占据晕中。一般参考态未展现类似的信号强度与空间紧凑性组合。可积系统对比表明，紧凑Krylov记忆具有态选择性，并非可积性的普遍后果。

Conclusion: Krylov空间记忆核心为识别结构化量子记忆的位置及其动态编码方式提供了一个稳态框架，揭示了非可积系统中异常初态记忆的普适组织规律。

Abstract: We introduce Krylov-space memory cores as stationary, depth-resolved structures that reveal how anomalous initial-state memory is organized inside the Krylov space of otherwise thermalizing nonintegrable systems. The stationary occupation profile identifies where late-time probability is concentrated along the Krylov chain, while complementary diagnostics of residual equilibration fluctuations, deviation from the Gibbs reference, and long-time Krylov-current fluctuations determine the physical character of that region. Across weak thermalization, confinement-induced anomalous dynamics, and many-body scarring, anomalous initial states develop compact low-depth memory cores that carry appreciable residual fluctuations, Gibbs mismatch, and persistent current-fluctuation activity. These cores are often embedded within substantially broader stationary occupation halos. Generic reference states, by contrast, do not exhibit a comparable combination of signal strength and spatial compactness. An auxiliary integrable comparison further shows that compact Krylov memory is state selective rather than a generic consequence of integrability. Krylov-space memory cores therefore provide a stationary framework for identifying where structured quantum memory resides and how it remains dynamically encoded.

</details>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [TempLat: a versatile C++ engine for lattice field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24908)
*Adrien Florio,Franz R. Sattler*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: TempLat 是一个基于表达式模板和 Kokkos 的 C++ 格点场论模拟框架，支持任意维度、多架构（CPU/NVIDIA/AMD GPU），实现了接近数学表达式的符号化编程，并具备良好的性能可移植性与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点场论模拟（包括经典统计模拟和蒙特卡洛模拟）通常需要高性能计算，但现有框架在跨架构可移植性、代码表达力和可扩展性方面存在不足。作者旨在提供一个既能保持数学表达清晰度、又能自动生成高度优化内核，且能无缝适配当前及未来硬件架构的统一框架。

Method: TempLat 采用 C++ 表达式模板（Expression Templates）技术实现符号化语言，使数学公式几乎逐字翻译为 C++ 代码，同时编译为高度优化的计算内核。底层基于 Kokkos 实现性能可移植性，通过将硬件抽象为统一的 device 概念，支持 CPU 集群、NVIDIA 和 AMD GPU，并便于扩展到未来架构。此外，作者开发了独立的并行离散傅里叶变换库 ParaFaFT，支持任意维度和上述所有硬件平台。

Result: TempLat 在 CPU 和 GPU 上均展示了优异的强缩放和弱缩放性能；ParaFaFT 库的并行 FFT 缩放基准测试也表现良好。该框架已实际部署于新版本的 CosmoLattice（早期宇宙模拟库），使其首次支持 GPU 运行。

Conclusion: TempLat 成功实现了格点场论模拟中高表达力、高性能与跨架构可移植性的统一，通过表达式模板与 Kokkos 的结合，为当前及未来硬件提供了可扩展的解决方案，并已在宇宙学模拟中得到实际应用验证。

Abstract: We present TempLat, a C++ framework for lattice field theory simulations in arbitrary dimensions. Its symbolic language, built on expression templates, translates mathematical expressions almost verbatim into C++ while compiling to highly optimized kernels, in contexts ranging from classical-statistical to Monte Carlo simulations. TempLat is performance-portable: building on Kokkos, it supports large CPU clusters, as well as NVIDIA and AMD GPUs. Its abstraction of hardware into a device concept makes TempLat extensible to future architectures. We demonstrate excellent strong and weak scaling on both CPUs and GPUs. We also present ParaFaFT, a standalone parallel discrete Fourier transform library supporting arbitrary dimensions on all of the above hardware, and benchmark its scaling. Both are open source and available on GitHub, and power a new release of CosmoLattice, a widely used early-universe simulation library now available on GPUs.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [A Generalized Tangent Approximation based Variational Inference Framework for Strongly Super-Gaussian Likelihoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05431)
*Somjit Roy,Pritam Dey,Debdeep Pati,Bani K. Mallick*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于切空间变换的新型变分推断框架，利用凸对偶性构造对数似然的切空间下界（tangent minorants），适用于具有强超高斯似然（strongly super-Gaussian likelihoods）的广泛概率模型。该方法在高斯先验下诱导共轭性，并提供算法收敛保证与近极小极大最优的变分风险界，在模拟和真实数据上展现出优于现有方法的可扩展性和捕获复杂数据结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分推断方法面临两难困境：要么依赖于模型特定的刚性公式，要么依赖随机黑箱优化。基于切空间逼近的结构化变分方法虽有理论基础，但应用范围长期局限于逻辑回归等少数模型。作者旨在将切空间逼近方法推广到更广泛的概率模型类别，同时克服黑箱方法缺乏理论保证的缺陷。

Method: 1. 利用凸对偶性（convex duality）为强超高斯似然构造对数似然的切空间下界函数（tangent minorants）；2. 借助这些下界诱导模型参数与高斯先验之间的共轭性，使原本难以处理的推断问题可解析求解；3. 在数据生成机制的温和假设下，建立算法收敛性保证；4. 推导变分风险的近极小极大最优界。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了所提算法的收敛性，填补了黑箱变分方法理论保证不足的空白；2. 导出了变分风险的近极小极大最优界；3. 在模拟与真实数据实验中，该方法在可扩展性和一致捕获复杂数据结构方面均优于当前最先进的变分算法。

Conclusion: 基于切空间变换的变分推断框架成功扩展至具有强超高斯似然的广泛模型类别，兼具理论保证（收敛性与风险界）和实际优越性能，为结构化变分推断提供了一种更具通用性和可靠性的新范式。

Abstract: Variational inference, as an alternative to Markov chain Monte Carlo sampling, has played a transformative role in enabling scalable computation for complex Bayesian models. Nevertheless, existing approaches often depend on either rigid model-specific formulations or stochastic black-box optimization routines. Tangent approximation is a principled class of structured variational methods that exploits the geometry of the underlying probability model. However, its utility has largely been confined to logistic regression and related modeling regimes. In this article, we propose a novel variational framework based on tangent transformation for a broad class of probability models characterized by strongly super-Gaussian likelihoods. Our method leverages convex duality to construct tangent minorants of the log-likelihood, thereby inducing conjugacy with Gaussian priors over model parameters in an otherwise intractable setup. Under mild assumptions on the data-generating mechanism, we establish algorithmic convergence guarantees, a contribution that stands in contrast to the limited theoretical assurances typically available for black-box variational methods. Additionally, we derive near-minimax optimal bounds for the variational risk. Superior performance of our proposed methodology is illustrated on simulated and real-data scenarios that challenge state-of-the-art variational algorithms in terms of scalability and their ability to consistently capture complex underlying data structure.

</details>


### [181] [Generalised Robust Bayes for Joint Inference of Model and Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25665)
*Masahiro Fujisawa,Masaki Adachi,Takuo Matsubara*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: Hölder-Bayes框架通过Hölder散度构建广义联合后验，同时对模型参数和污染比例进行推断，实现了从定性鲁棒到定量鲁棒的跨越，并提供了一种无需外部阈值的概率化异常检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义贝叶斯推断(GBI)框架仅提供定性鲁棒性，缺乏内在机制来量化污染比例或识别异常观测。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个能够联合推断模型参数与污染比例的GBI框架。

Method: 作者提出Hölder-Bayes框架：(1)通过对缩放模型密度施加Hölder散度，构建覆盖模型参数和污染参数的广义联合后验；(2)理论上证明了后验影响函数的一致有界性（全局偏差鲁棒性）、有限样本超额风险界、Bernstein-von Mises逼近及重污染机制下的偏差界；(3)揭示温度校准可解释为数据空间的仿射体积缩放。

Result: 理论层面：建立了后验影响函数的一致有界性、有限样本超额风险界、BvM逼近及污染诱导偏差界。方法层面：后验在模型参数和污染比例上的不确定性被传播到观测级别的Frequency-of-Detection分数，形成自包含的概率化异常检测机制。实验层面：在鲁棒参数推断、污染水平恢复和不确定性感知异常检测三方面均表现有效。

Conclusion: Hölder-Bayes实现了从定性鲁棒到定量鲁棒的范式转变，为鲁棒贝叶斯推断提供了同时具备污染量化能力和概率化异常检测功能的统一框架。

Abstract: Generalised Bayesian inference (GBI) has emerged as a compelling robust alternative to standard Bayesian inference, mitigating sensitivity to data contamination by replacing the log-likelihood with a robust loss or divergence. However, existing robust GBI frameworks typically provide only qualitative robustness: while they can make posterior inference less sensitive to contamination, they lack an intrinsic mechanism to quantify the contamination proportion or identify anomalous observations. This paper introduces Hölder-Bayes, a GBI framework for joint inference of the model parameter and the contamination proportion. We construct a generalised joint posterior over both model and contamination parameter by applying the Hölder divergence to a scaled model density. Theoretically, we establish global bias-robustness via the uniform boundedness of the posterior influence function, derive a finite-sample excess-risk bound, and prove a Bernstein--von Mises approximation together with interpretable contamination-induced bias bounds under a heavy-contamination regime. We further show that, for the Hölder posterior, temperature calibration admits a direct interpretation as affine volume scaling of the data space. The resulting posterior yields a self-contained probabilistic mechanism for outlier detection: posterior uncertainty in both the model parameter and the contamination proportion is propagated to observation-level Frequency-of-Detection scores, without requiring an external anomaly-score threshold. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Hölder-Bayes provides robust parameter inference, contamination-level recovery, and uncertainty-aware outlier detection.

</details>


### [182] [Bias-corrected Cox regression with AI-extracted covariates via calibration summary statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25868)
*Arjun Sondhi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对AI从临床记录中提取协变量存在误差的问题，本文提出了一套针对Cox比例风险模型的偏差校正框架。该方法通过多元校准将朴素估计量的偏差分解为主导性校准项和二阶残差项，并据此构造了后验矩阵校正估计量、考虑校准不确定性的置信区间以及敏感性诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模观察性研究越来越依赖AI从非结构化临床记录中提取结构化变量。常见工作流程中，数据供应商仅提供提取后的数据集和汇总准确率统计量，下游研究者无法直接获取金标准标注。当AI提取的协变量存在误差时，直接使用这些协变量拟合Cox比例风险模型会产生偏差，而现有方法未能充分解决这一场景下的统计推断问题。

Method: 在统一的多元校准框架下，将朴素Cox估计量的偏差分解为：一个由校准关系主导的一阶项和一个随提取精度提升而消失的二阶残差项。基于一阶项构造校正估计量，可通过对标准Cox软件输出进行事后矩阵乘法实现。进一步推导了包含校准不确定性的偏差调整置信区间，并提出敏感性诊断指标以评估被忽略的残差项是否可能实质性影响推断。

Result: 合成数据实验模拟了交叉依赖的提取误差和受控的非线性校准违背情形。结果表明，所提校正方法大幅降低了偏差，且即使在线性校准假设存在轻度违背时，仍能实现接近名义水平的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据供应商提供了一个具体可操作的报告规范：一份简短的汇总统计量清单，应随任何用于生存分析的AI提取协变量数据集一并提供，从而使下游研究者能够实施所提出的偏差校正方法。

Abstract: Large-scale observational studies increasingly rely on AI pipelines to extract structured variables from unstructured clinical records. A common workflow separates the data vendor, who validates extraction accuracy with a gold-standard sample, from the downstream researcher, who receives only the extracted dataset and summary accuracy statistics. We develop a bias-correction framework for the Cox proportional hazards model when covariates are subject to AI extraction error. Within a unified multivariate calibration framework, we show that the naive Cox estimator's bias decomposes into a leading-order calibration term and a second-order residual that vanishes as extraction accuracy improves. The leading-order term yields a corrected estimator that operates as a post-hoc matrix multiplication on the output of any standard Cox software. We further derive bias-adjusted confidence intervals that incorporate calibration uncertainty and a sensitivity diagnostic for assessing whether the neglected residual could materially affect inference. Synthetic data experiments with cross-dependent extraction errors and controlled nonlinear calibration violations confirm that the correction substantially reduces bias and achieves near-nominal coverage even under mild violations of the linear calibration assumption. The framework yields a concrete reporting specification: a short list of summary statistics that data vendors should provide alongside any AI-extracted covariate dataset used in survival analysis.

</details>

### [183] [Deterministic loading of molecular arrays by microwave-assisted collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25783)
*Etienne F. Walraven,Kang Feng,Jonas Rodewald,Michael R. Tarbutt,Tijs Karman*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用微波辅助碰撞和分子激发态"搁置"策略，通过排斥性范德瓦尔斯相互作用抑制碰撞损失，实现分子光镊阵列的高效装载，预测填充率可达96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子光镊阵列可通过光辅助碰撞实现高效装载，但该方法因碰撞损失无法直接推广到分子阵列。研究者希望找到一种适用于分子的增强装载方法，以实现可扩展的分子光镊阵列。

Method: 通过将已装载分子搁置在转动或振动激发态上，使其与新装载分子之间产生排斥性范德瓦尔斯相互作用，从而抑制碰撞损失；引入微波辅助碰撞来控制分子对碰撞后的最终态及释放能量；在受控碰撞后，探索多种策略确保一对分子中有一分子被确定性弹射出去。

Result: 该方案基于当前激光可冷却分子的现有技术即可实现，预测分子阵列的填充率可达96%。

Conclusion: 通过激发态搁置和微波辅助碰撞控制，成功克服了分子光镊阵列装载中的碰撞损失问题，为可扩展分子阵列的构建铺平了道路。

Abstract: Molecular tweezer arrays offer great prospects for quantum simulation, sensing, and computing, and would benefit from methods that enhance loading efficiency. Whereas light-assisted collisions underpin enhanced loading methods for atomic tweezer arrays, this approach cannot be directly extended to molecular arrays due to collisional loss. We show how this collisional loss can be suppressed by shelving molecules in rotationally or vibrationally excited states, so that a shelved molecule interacts with a newly loaded molecule through a repulsive van der Waals interaction. By introducing microwave assisted collisions, we show how to control the final states and the energy released in a collision between a pair of molecules. Following this controlled collision, one of the two molecules can be ejected, and we explore several strategies for ensuring deterministic ejection. Our schemes rely on currently available techniques for laser-coolable molecules, and we predict achievable filling fractions up to 96%, paving the way for scalable molecular arrays.

</details>

### [184] [A Path Integral Model of Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24807)
*Haruki Emori,Kazunori Kondo,Atsushi Iriki,Andrei Khrennikov*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了认知成本优化的数学物理公式，奠定了意识的路径积分模型基础。核心思想是将目标导向的认知过程建模为投影哈密顿量下的虚时间演化（ITE），并证明了三个关键结果：ITE与双括号流（Riemann梯度流）的等价性、通过Wick旋转得到酉演化的离散路径积分表示、以及用酉相互作用强度统一无意识到有意识处理的连续谱，涵盖GKSL退相干模型和"顿悟"式的投影固着极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为意识现象建立严格的数学物理基础，将目标导向的认知过程形式化，并解释从无意识处理到有意识"顿悟"的连续过渡机制，统一先前零散的模型（如Asano等人的GKSL退相干模型）。

Method: 采用虚时间演化（ITE）下的投影哈密顿量建模认知过程；利用双括号流（double-bracket flow）证明ITE对应Riemann梯度流；通过Wick旋转将非酉下降转化为等价酉演化，推导离散路径积分表示；最后用认知系统与神经环境探针之间的酉相互作用强度参数化无意识到有意识的连续谱。

Result: （1）ITE与Hilbert-Schmidt代价函数的双括号流/Riemann梯度流等价，唯一极小值即为解；（2）Wick旋转给出精确离散路径积分表示，其中oracle和初始态扩散投影子分别扮演势能与动能角色；（3）无意识-有意识连续谱由酉相互作用强度控制：弱耦合Markov极限恢复Asano等人的GKSL退相干模型，强耦合极限对应可报告的"顿悟"式固着，两者共享同一ITE和路径积分结构。

Conclusion: Wick旋转是一种重新描述技术而非物理机制转变，整个认知处理谱系——从无意识退相干到有意识洞察——统一于同一数学框架之下，仅由测量-相互作用强度这一个参数区分。

Abstract: We develop the mathematical and physical formulation of cognitive cost optimization that underlies the path-integral model of consciousness. The goal-directed cognitive process is modeled as imaginary-time evolution (ITE) under a projector Hamiltonian that rewards configurations consistent with a target concept. We establish three results. First, this ITE coincides with a double-bracket flow and is therefore the Riemannian gradient flow of a Hilbert--Schmidt cost whose unique minimum is the solution. Second, a Wick rotation re-expresses this non-unitary descent as an equivalent unitary evolution on the same Hilbert space, which admits an exact discrete path-integral representation in which the oracle and the initial-state diffusion projector play the roles of potential and kinetic energy. Third, we identify the continuum from unconscious to conscious processing with the strength of the unitary interaction between the cognitive system and a neural-environment probe, recovering the Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) decoherence model of Asano \textit{et al.} in the Markovian weak-coupling limit, and the projective, reportable fixation of an optimized state in the strong-coupling limit, an insight-like ``Aha'' endpoint. Both regimes share the same ITE and path-integral structure, and only the measurement-interaction strength varies. The Wick rotation is therefore a technique of re-description, not a physical regime change.

</details>
